\hypertarget{how-to-read-this-document}{%
\section{How to read this document}\label{how-to-read-this-document}}

\hypertarget{what-this-document-contains}{%
\subsection{What this document
contains}\label{what-this-document-contains}}

This document states the complete set of coupled field equations of the
universe model UM-2, derives them from one action, and explains every
term. It is self-contained: every equation that it uses is written in
it, and every symbol of every equation is defined right after the
equation. The sections are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{The action} (IN-1 to IN-18): every term of the action, written
  out and explained: what it couples, and why it is consistent with QCD
  and with the electroweak theory.
\item
  \textbf{Gravity} (FE-1 to FE-11): the Einstein-Lovelock equations, the
  energy-momentum tensor of every field and its conservation.
\item
  \textbf{The matter fields} (FE-12 to FE-30): the field equations of
  dirac16complex, of the three generations of quarks and leptons, of the
  gauge fields (gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), photon), of the tip sigma
  field, and their boundary conditions.
\item
  \textbf{The background} (FE-31 to FE-49): the exact order-0 equations
  of the metric, the static block and its stabiliser, the radion, the
  response of the block to matter and four-dimensional gravity.
\item
  \textbf{The masses from the hidden coordinate} (FE-50 to FE-62): the
  \(x_0\) eigenvalue problems that give every particle its mass (the
  gauge bosons, the graviton tower, the twelve fermions with their
  mixing, dirac16complex).
\item
  \textbf{dirac16complex coupled to all fields} (FE-63 to FE-86): the
  Kohn-Sham equations of dirac16complex with the mean fields of every
  other field, the portals on the zero modes, the condensates of the
  Standard-Model plasma, and the block response that closes the loop.
\item
  \textbf{The linear perturbations} (FE-87 to FE-99): the metric as a
  power series, the Einstein-Boltzmann equations of the CMB, the CNB and
  the BAO.
\item
  \textbf{The three neutrino flavours} (FE-100 to FE-120): the neutrino
  masses, the four-fermion interactions, the flavour evolution in
  vacuum, in matter, in the early Universe and in dense neutrino gases.
\item
  \textbf{The thermal history} (FE-121 to FE-136): the degrees of
  freedom, the Boltzmann equations of the dark matter, of the fold bands
  of dirac16complex and of the right-handed neutrinos.
\item
  \textbf{The coupled system}: which fields appear in which equations,
  and how the system is solved, step by step.
\item
  \textbf{What was verified}: the 288 checks of the computations and the
  residuals of the solution in 26 of the equations.
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{the-status-labels}{%
\subsection{The status labels}\label{the-status-labels}}

Every equation and every result carries one of the labels

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{AUTHOR}: from the author's notebooks (the metric, the
  coordinates, the Lovelock weights, the field dirac16complex);
\item
  \textbf{RECORD}: from the Revision record of this repository (the
  dirac16complex Lagrangian and its Kohn-Sham treatment);
\item
  \textbf{STANDARD}: textbook physics;
\item
  \textbf{ASSUMED}: a choice made in UM-2 without derivation;
\item
  \textbf{INVENTED}: a new interaction or construction of UM-2 (the tip
  sigma field, the portals I11, I12, I13);
\item
  \textbf{DERIVED}: computed and verified in UM-2 (the verification is
  stated);
\item
  \textbf{ESTIMATE}: an order-of-magnitude evaluation;
\item
  \textbf{OPEN}: not settled.
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{every-equation-is-followed-by-its-symbols}{%
\subsection{Every equation is followed by its
symbols}\label{every-equation-is-followed-by-its-symbols}}

Every display equation is followed by the list ``Symbols in (FE-n)'' (or
``Symbols in this equation'' for a display without a tag) of EVERY
symbol that appears in it: every quantity, field, coupling, constant and
operator, every index letter with the values it takes, every mark
(prime, dagger, transpose, star), the imaginary unit, Euler's number and
the differential. Each item gives the meaning, the unit (natural units
\(\hbar = c = 1\)), the value where one exists, and the status. A field
equation carries the tag FE-1 to FE-136 and an interaction term of the
action the tag IN-1 to IN-18; the numbering is that of the inventory of
UM-2 and is the same in every UM-2 document. Displays without a tag are
steps of a derivation.

\hypertarget{coordinates-signature-and-units}{%
\subsection{Coordinates, signature and
units}\label{coordinates-signature-and-units}}

The author's coordinates are \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\), always
with the hidden direction first: \(x_0\) is the hidden space-like
coordinate, on which the metric depends periodically with the period
\(\pi/(6H)\) through the angle \(z = 6Hx_0\); \(x_1, x_2, x_3\) are
ordinary space, \(x_4\) is the time, and \(x_5, x_6, x_7\) are three
extra time-like coordinates. The signature is \((+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-)\) in
this order; the four observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) have the
signature \((+,+,+,-)\). \(H = 14.296\) TeV is the author's constant (an
inverse length). Units are natural, \(\hbar = c = 1\); in eight
dimensions a fermion field has the dimension GeV\(^{7/2}\) and the
gravitational coupling \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\) the dimension GeV\(^{-6}\).
A dot is the derivative with respect to the time \(x_4\); a prime is
used only in the cosmological perturbation equations, for the derivative
with respect to the conformal time.

The author orders a model universe with the following coupled fields;
UM-2 adds the tip sigma field (which replaces the Higgs field) and the
stabiliser of the block.

The gauge fields of the action are the SU(3) field \(G\), the SU(2)
field \(W\) (components \(W^1\), \(W^2\), \(W^3\)) and the hypercharge
field \(B\); \(W^+\), \(W^-\), \(Z\) and the photon \(A\) are their
physical combinations (IN-10). The right-handed neutrinos \(\nu_R\) are
gauge singlets: they give the neutrinos Dirac masses at the tip and are
never thermalised.

\hypertarget{conventions-of-version-4}{%
\subsection{Conventions of version 4}\label{conventions-of-version-4}}

Version 4 changes the presentation of version 3, not its physics. Every
symbol has one meaning in the whole document (the symbols of version 3
that had several meanings are replaced); Euler's number is
\(\mathrm{e}\), the imaginary unit \(\mathrm{i}\), the differential
\(\mathrm{d}\); a word or a particle name in a subscript is upright
(\(M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\), \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\), \(n_{\mathrm{e}}\)), an italic
letter in a subscript is an index. \(\Delta\) denotes a change (a
variation, a first-order perturbation, a level shift), \(\delta\) the
Kronecker delta and \(\delta_{\mathrm{D}}\) the Dirac delta function.
The gauge fields \(G\), \(W\), \(B\) are in the geometric normalisation
(the couplings stand in front of their kinetic terms), in eight and in
four dimensions; \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), \(A\) and \(F^V_{\mu\nu}\) are
canonically normalised. \(W^+\) carries the electric charge \(+e\) and
\(W^-\) the charge \(-e\). Two equations of version 3 are written more
explicitly: the block components of the background equations (FE-40,
FE-42, FE-43) show the pressure \(p_0\) of the hidden direction and the
pressure \(p_5\) of the extra times separately (version 3 wrote only
their average \(\bar p\), and in one place \(p_0\); section ``The
background'', subsection ``The block pressures''), and the overlap
integrals of the portals carry their factor \(1/H\) explicitly (FE-70,
FE-135). No number of version 3 changes.

\hypertarget{the-action}{%
\section{The action}\label{the-action}}

All field equations of UM-2 follow from one action by varying it with
respect to every field. This section writes out every term of the action
(IN-1 to IN-18) and says in plain words what it couples. The
Standard-Model part has exactly the gauge group, the fermion content
(three generations, each with a right-handed neutrino) and the gauge
couplings of the Standard Model, but no Higgs field: the electroweak
symmetry is broken at the tip of the hidden direction by a non-linear
sigma field, and every mass comes from the dependence of the wave
functions on \(x_0\). dirac16complex is a singlet of the Standard-Model
gauge group and couples to the Standard Model only through three
gauge-invariant portals.

\hypertarget{the-sum-of-all-terms}{%
\subsection{The sum of all terms}\label{the-sum-of-all-terms}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
S ={}& S_{\mathrm{EL}} + S_{\mathrm{stab}} + S_\Psi + S_{\mathrm{gauge}} + S_{\mathrm{f}} + S_{\mathrm{tip}}
 + S_{\mathrm{I11}} + S_{\mathrm{I12}} + S_{\mathrm{I13}}
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-1}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-1).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S\): The action of UM-2 (the integral of the Lagrangian density over
  spacetime); its variation with respect to every field gives the field
  equations. Unit: dimensionless (\(\hbar = 1\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\): The Einstein-Lovelock action of gravity. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The action of the block stabiliser (a vacuum
  energy of the block). Unit: dimensionless. Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(S_\Psi\): The action of the dirac16complex field (kinetic term with
  the canonical spin connection, mass term and contact
  self-interaction). Unit: dimensionless. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{gauge}}\): The action of the gauge fields of SU(3), SU(2)
  and U(1)\(_Y\) in eight dimensions. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{f}}\): The action of the Standard-Model fermions of three
  generations (kinetic and bulk-mass terms). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The action on the tip \(y = -L\): the sigma
  field and the tip Yukawa couplings. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I11}}\): The action of the portal I11 (dirac16complex
  vector current times the \(B - L\) current of the fermions). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I12}}\): The action of the portal I12 (scalar density of
  dirac16complex times the gauge kinetic invariants). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The action of the portal I13 (the \(x_0\)-chiral
  vector current of dirac16complex times the baryon-number current).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\end{itemize}

The action is the sum of nine terms: Einstein-Lovelock gravity
(\(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\)), the block stabiliser (\(S_{\mathrm{stab}}\)), the
field dirac16complex (\(S_\Psi\)), the gauge fields of SU(3), SU(2) and
U(1)\(_Y\) (\(S_{\mathrm{gauge}}\)), the Standard-Model fermions of
three generations (\(S_{\mathrm{f}}\)), the tip terms (the sigma field
and the tip Yukawa couplings, \(S_{\mathrm{tip}}\)) and the three
portals of dirac16complex (\(S_{\mathrm{I11}}\), \(S_{\mathrm{I12}}\),
\(S_{\mathrm{I13}}\)). Varying \(S\) with respect to the metric gives
the gravitational equations FE-1; with respect to \(\bar\Psi\) the
dirac16complex equation FE-12; with respect to every fermion its Dirac
equation FE-14 to FE-19; with respect to the gauge fields the Yang-Mills
equations FE-22 to FE-25; the boundary terms of the variation give the
boundary conditions FE-27 to FE-30. There is no Higgs field. Status:
ASSUMED (the choice of the terms).

\hypertarget{gravity}{%
\subsection{Gravity}\label{gravity}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_{\mathrm{EL}} &= \frac{1}{2\kappa}\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,
 \Big(\sum_{p=1}^{3}\alpha_{(p)}\,\mathcal{L}_{(p)} - 2\Lambda\Big),\qquad \kappa = 8\pi G_8,\\
\alpha_{(p)} &= w_{(p)}\,H^{2-2p},\qquad w_{(1)} = 1,\quad w_{(2)} = \frac{1}{32},\quad w_{(3)} =
 \frac{1}{1152},
 \qquad \Lambda = -10H^2
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-2}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-2).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\): The Einstein-Lovelock action of gravity. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value:
  \(\kappa/V_\perp = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 2.328\times10^{-36}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(p\): The Lovelock order \(p = 1, 2, 3\) (Einstein, Gauss-Bonnet,
  cubic). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{(p)}\): The Lovelock coupling of order \(p\),
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) (\(\alpha_{(1)} = 1\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2-2p}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\): The Lovelock density of order \(p\) of the
  metric (a scalar built from \(p\) Riemann tensors):
  \(\mathcal{L}_{(1)} = R\) (Einstein-Hilbert), \(\mathcal{L}_{(2)}\)
  the Gauss-Bonnet density, \(\mathcal{L}_{(3)}\) the cubic Lovelock
  density. Unit: 1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2p}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda\): The eight-dimensional cosmological constant,
  \(\Lambda = -10H^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Value: \(-10H^2\).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_8\): The eight-dimensional Newton constant
  (\(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(w_{(p)}\): The author's Lovelock weights, \(w_{(1)} = 1\),
  \(w_{(2)} = 1/32\), \(w_{(3)} = 1/1152\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \((1, 1/32, 1/1152)\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

Gravity is the Einstein-Lovelock theory in eight dimensions with the
author's weights. \(\mathcal{L}_{(1)}\) is the Einstein-Hilbert density
(the Ricci scalar), \(\mathcal{L}_{(2)}\) the Gauss-Bonnet density and
\(\mathcal{L}_{(3)}\) the cubic Lovelock density; in eight dimensions
the series ends with \(p = 3\) (the density of order 4 is a total
derivative in eight dimensions). The couplings
\(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) carry the author's dimensionless
weights \(w_{(1)} = 1\), \(w_{(2)} = 1/32\), \(w_{(3)} = 1/1152\), and
the cosmological constant is \(\Lambda = -10H^2\). Gravity couples to
every field through the metric that appears in every other term of the
action (in \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\), in the contractions of indices and
in the spin connection). Status: AUTHOR.

\[
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{L}_{(p)} &= \frac{1}{2^{p}}\,\delta^{M_1N_1\cdots M_pN_p}_{P_1Q_1\cdots P_pQ_p}\,
 R^{P_1Q_1}{}_{M_1N_1}\cdots R^{P_pQ_p}{}_{M_pN_p},\\
\mathcal{L}_{(1)} &= R,\qquad \mathcal{L}_{(2)} = R^2 - 4R_{MN}R^{MN} + R_{MNPQ}R^{MNPQ}
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-3}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-3).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\): The Lovelock density of order \(p\) of the
  metric (a scalar built from \(p\) Riemann tensors):
  \(\mathcal{L}_{(1)} = R\) (Einstein-Hilbert), \(\mathcal{L}_{(2)}\)
  the Gauss-Bonnet density, \(\mathcal{L}_{(3)}\) the cubic Lovelock
  density. Unit: 1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2p}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): The Lovelock order \(p = 1, 2, 3\) (Einstein, Gauss-Bonnet,
  cubic). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{\alpha e}\),
  \(\delta_{l0}\), \(\delta^{I3}\)); with upper and lower index strings
  the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\) (+1
  or -1 if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation of the lower
  ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(Q_1, Q_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(R^{MN}{}_{PQ},\ R_{MN},\ R\): The curvature of the metric
  \(g_{MN}\): with four indices the Riemann tensor, with two indices the
  Ricci tensor \(R_{MN} = R^P{}_{MPN}\), without indices the Ricci
  (scalar) curvature \(R = g^{MN}R_{MN}\); \(R^{ab}{}_{ab}\) (no sum) is
  the sectional curvature of the coordinate plane \((a, b)\). Unit:
  1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The Lovelock densities are built with the generalised Kronecker delta
from \(p\) Riemann tensors. Their key property (Lovelock's theorem,
STANDARD) is that their metric variation contains no derivatives of the
metric higher than the second, so that the field equations FE-1 remain
second-order differential equations, as in general relativity. For
\(p = 1\) the formula gives the Ricci scalar, for \(p = 2\) the
Gauss-Bonnet combination written in the second line. Status: STANDARD.

\hypertarget{the-block-stabiliser}{%
\subsection{The block stabiliser}\label{the-block-stabiliser}}

\[
S_{\mathrm{stab}} = -\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\;\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f),\qquad
\kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f) = \kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*)\Big(\frac{f_*}{f}\Big)^{8},\qquad
f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}\,b_4^{1/2} \tag{IN-4}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-4).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The action of the block stabiliser (a vacuum
  energy of the block). Unit: dimensionless. Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The energy density of the block stabiliser,
  a function of the block scale,
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}} \propto f^{-8}\); its value at the static block
  is \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*)\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Value:
  \(\kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*) = (26 + 4\sqrt{51})H^2 = 54.566\,H^2\).
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value:
  \(\kappa/V_\perp = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 2.328\times10^{-36}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(f_*\): The block scale of the stabilised (static) block,
  \(f_* = K_*^{-1/2}\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.215556. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  hidden and extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

The four directions \(x_0, x_5, x_6, x_7\) form the ``block''. The
order-0 metric (FE-31) scales all four of them with one block scale
\(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\). With the author's weights and
\(\Lambda\) alone there is no static block with attractive
four-dimensional gravity (FE-42); UM-2 therefore assumes a vacuum energy
of the block, \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\), that falls with the block scale
as \(f^{-8}\) (the law of a Casimir-type energy of a block of size
\(f\)). Its normalisation \(\kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*)\) is fixed
by the requirement that the block be static (FE-43). It couples only to
gravity: its energy-momentum tensor is FE-4. Status: ASSUMED; its
microscopic origin is OPEN.

\hypertarget{the-field-dirac16complex}{%
\subsection{The field dirac16complex}\label{the-field-dirac16complex}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_\Psi &= \int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Big[\tfrac12\big(\bar\Psi\gamma^MD_M\Psi
 - (D_M\bar\Psi)\gamma^M\Psi\big) - m_\Psi\,\bar\Psi\Psi - \frac{\lambda}{2}\big(\bar\Psi\Psi\big)^2\Big],\\
m_\Psi &= 3H\mathcal{M} = H\qquad (\mathcal{M} = 1/3)
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-5}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-5).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_\Psi\): The action of the dirac16complex field (kinetic term with
  the canonical spin connection, mass term and contact
  self-interaction). Unit: dimensionless. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex,
  \(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M} = H\). Unit: GeV. Value: 14.296 TeV. Status:
  RECORD.
\item
  \(\lambda\): The contact self-coupling of dirac16complex (the term
  \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) of the record). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value: not fixed; Kohn-Sham states computed for
  \(\lambda = 0\) and \(\pm7\times10^{-5}H^{-6}\); the fold-band
  radiation requires
  \(\lvert\lambda\rvert \lesssim 1.4\times10^{-10}H^{-6}\) (for
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} \le 0.001\)). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{M}\): The record's dimensionless mass parameter of
  dirac16complex, \(\mathcal{M} = 1/3\) (\(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M}\)).
  Unit: dimensionless. Value: 1/3. Status: RECORD.
\end{itemize}

dirac16complex \(\Psi\) is the author's 16-component complex spinor
field of eight dimensions. Its Lagrangian is the record's: the
symmetrised Dirac kinetic term (both \(\Psi\) and \(\bar\Psi\) are
differentiated, so the action is real), the mass term with the mass
parameter \(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M} = H\), and the contact
self-interaction \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\). The covariant
derivative \(D_M\) contains the canonical spin connection (IN-7),
through which \(\Psi\) couples to gravity. \(\Psi\) carries no colour,
weak isospin or hypercharge. Status: RECORD.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\gamma^a\gamma^b + \gamma^b\gamma^a = 2\eta^{ab}\,\mathbf{1},\qquad
 \eta_{ab} = \mathrm{diag}(+1, +1, +1, +1, -1, -1, -1, -1),\qquad \gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a,\\
&\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C,\qquad C = \gamma^{x_0}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3},\qquad
 \Gamma_4 = \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\gamma^{x_4},\qquad \mathcal{B} =
 -\mathrm{i}C\gamma^{x_4},\\
&\Psi = \Psi_A + \Psi_B,\qquad \mathcal{B}\Psi_A = -\Psi_A,\qquad \mathcal{B}\Psi_B = +\Psi_B
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-6}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-6).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(a\): A frame (orthonormal) index running over \(0, 1, \dots, 7\),
  aligned with \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); raised and lowered with
  \(\eta_{ab}\); a repeated index is summed (except in a sectional
  curvature \(R^{ab}{}_{ab}\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b\): A frame index running over \(0, \dots, 7\) (as \(a\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\eta_{ab}\): The flat metric of the frame,
  \(\eta_{ab} = \mathrm{diag}(+1, +1, +1, +1, -1, -1, -1, -1)\)
  (\(\eta^{ab}\) the same matrix). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{e}^a{}_M,\ \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\): The vielbein (frame field) of
  the metric: \(g_{MN} = \eta_{ab}\,\mathbf{e}^a{}_M\mathbf{e}^b{}_N\);
  \(\mathbf{e}_a{}^M\) is the inverse matrix. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(C\): The matrix of the Dirac adjoint,
  \(C = \gamma^{x_0}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\)
  (\(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\)); in each block of the reduction
  \(C = \varsigma_2\sigma_2\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_1\): The first coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_2\): The second coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_3\): The third coordinate of ordinary 3-space (also the symmetry
  axis of the fast-flavour analysis). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Gamma_4\): The four-dimensional chirality matrix,
  \(\Gamma_4 = \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\gamma^{x_4}\);
  a doublet zero mode has \(\Gamma_4 = +1\) (left-handed), a singlet
  zero mode \(\Gamma_4 = -1\) (right-handed). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{B}\): The author's operator
  \(\mathcal{B} = -\mathrm{i}C\gamma^{x_4}\); its eigenvalues \(\mp1\)
  define the halves \(\Psi_A\) and \(\Psi_B\), and it commutes with
  every interaction of the good sector. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi_A,\ \Psi_B\): The two halves of dirac16complex,
  \(\Psi = \Psi_A + \Psi_B\), the eigenspaces of \(\mathcal{B}\) with
  the eigenvalues \(-1\) and \(+1\); each half carries one dark-matter
  species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

The Dirac matrices satisfy the Clifford algebra of the signature
\((4, 4)\); the author's matrices are real \(16 \times 16\) matrices,
and the algebra holds exactly (31 checks of
\texttt{code/um2\_algebra.py}). The Dirac adjoint uses the matrix
\(C = \gamma^{x_0}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\) (in four-four
signature the usual \(\gamma^0\) is replaced by the product of the four
space-like matrices). \(\Gamma_4\) is the four-dimensional chirality.
The author's operator \(\mathcal{B} = -\mathrm{i}C\gamma^{x_4}\) has the
eigenvalues \(\pm1\) and splits \(\Psi\) into two halves, \(\Psi_A\) and
\(\Psi_B\); it commutes with every interaction of the good sector, so
the two halves never mix (each carries one dark-matter species). The
same algebra and adjoint serve every Standard-Model fermion. Status:
AUTHOR.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&D_M\Psi = \partial_M\Psi + \Omega_M\Psi,\qquad D_M\bar\Psi = \partial_M\bar\Psi - \bar\Psi\Omega_M,\qquad
 \Omega_M = \tfrac12\,\omega_{Mab}\,\mathcal{S}^{ab},\qquad \mathcal{S}^{ab} = \tfrac14\big[\gamma^a,
 \gamma^b\big],\\
&\omega_M{}^a{}_b = \mathbf{e}^a{}_N\big(\partial_M\mathbf{e}_b{}^N +
 \Gamma^N{}_{MP}\,\mathbf{e}_b{}^P\big),\qquad
 \omega_{Mab} = \eta_{ac}\,\omega_M{}^c{}_b,\qquad g_{MN} = \eta_{ab}\,\mathbf{e}^a{}_M\,\mathbf{e}^b{}_N,\\
&\Gamma^N{}_{MP} = \tfrac12\,g^{NQ}\big(\partial_Mg_{QP} + \partial_Pg_{QM} - \partial_Qg_{MP}\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-7}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-7).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Omega_M\): The spin connection acting on spinors, the matrix
  \(\Omega_M = \frac12\omega_{Mab}\mathcal{S}^{ab}\). Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\omega_M{}^a{}_b\): The coefficients of the spin connection of the
  vielbein (the Levi-Civita connection in the frame basis);
  \(\omega_{Mab} = \eta_{ac}\,\omega_M{}^c{}_b\). Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a\): A frame (orthonormal) index running over \(0, 1, \dots, 7\),
  aligned with \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); raised and lowered with
  \(\eta_{ab}\); a repeated index is summed (except in a sectional
  curvature \(R^{ab}{}_{ab}\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b\): A frame index running over \(0, \dots, 7\) (as \(a\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{S}^{ab}\): The generators of frame rotations on spinors,
  \(\mathcal{S}^{ab} = \frac14[\gamma^a, \gamma^b]\) (the commutator).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathbf{e}^a{}_M,\ \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\): The vielbein (frame field) of
  the metric: \(g_{MN} = \eta_{ab}\,\mathbf{e}^a{}_M\mathbf{e}^b{}_N\);
  \(\mathbf{e}_a{}^M\) is the inverse matrix. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\): The Christoffel symbols (the Levi-Civita
  connection) of the metric,
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP} = \frac12 g^{NQ}(\partial_Mg_{QP} + \partial_Pg_{QM} - \partial_Qg_{MP})\).
  Unit: 1/length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\eta_{ab}\): The flat metric of the frame,
  \(\eta_{ab} = \mathrm{diag}(+1, +1, +1, +1, -1, -1, -1, -1)\)
  (\(\eta^{ab}\) the same matrix). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c\): A frame index running over \(0, \dots, 7\) (as \(a\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(Q\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(Q_1, Q_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The spin connection couples every spinor to gravity: \(\Omega_M\) is
built from the vielbein \(\mathbf{e}^a{}_M\) of the metric and the
Christoffel symbols, and it makes the Dirac matrices covariantly
constant (verified symbolically). On the order-0 metric it is the
non-zero term FE-13. Status: STANDARD.

\hypertarget{the-gauge-fields}{%
\subsection{The gauge fields}\label{the-gauge-fields}}

\[
S_{\mathrm{gauge}} = -\frac14\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Big(\frac{1}{\hat
 g_3^2}\,G^A_{MN}G^{AMN}
 + \frac{1}{\hat g_2^2}\,W^I_{MN}W^{IMN} + \frac{1}{\hat g_1^2}\,B_{MN}B^{MN}\Big) \tag{IN-8}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-8).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{gauge}}\): The action of the gauge fields of SU(3), SU(2)
  and U(1)\(_Y\) in eight dimensions. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\hat g_3\): The eight-dimensional SU(3) coupling (it multiplies the
  gluon kinetic term as \(1/\hat g_3^2\));
  \(g_3^2 = \hat g_3^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

The gauge group is that of the Standard Model, SU(3) \(\times\) SU(2)
\(\times\) U(1)\(_Y\), in eight dimensions. The fields are in the
geometric normalisation: the eight-dimensional couplings \(\hat g_3\),
\(\hat g_2\), \(\hat g_1\) stand in front of the kinetic terms, and the
four-dimensional couplings are \(g_i^2 = \hat g_i^2/V_\perp\) (FE-49).
The gauge zero modes are flat in \(x_0\) (FE-52), so the
four-dimensional theory has exactly the gauge interactions of QCD and of
the electroweak theory. Status: ASSUMED (the eight-dimensional form).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&G_M = G^A_M\,t^A,\qquad G_{MN} = \partial_MG_N - \partial_NG_M - \mathrm{i}\big[G_M, G_N\big],\qquad
 G^A_{MN} = 2\,\mathrm{tr}\big(t^AG_{MN}\big),\\
&W_M = W^I_M\,\frac{\tau^I}{2},\qquad W_{MN} = \partial_MW_N - \partial_NW_M - \mathrm{i}\big[W_M,
 W_N\big],\qquad
 W^I_{MN} = \mathrm{tr}\big(\tau^IW_{MN}\big),\\
&B_{MN} = \partial_MB_N - \partial_NB_M
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-9}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-9).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(t^A\): The generators of SU(3) in the fundamental representation
  (the Gell-Mann matrices divided by 2),
  \(\mathrm{tr}(t^At^B) = \frac12\delta^{AB}\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

The field strengths are the standard non-abelian ones (STANDARD):
\(t^A\) are the generators of SU(3) and \(\tau^I/2\) those of SU(2); the
commutator terms make the gluons and the \(W\) bosons self-interacting,
as in QCD and in the electroweak theory.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&W^\pm_M = \frac{W^1_M \mp \mathrm{i}W^2_M}{\sqrt2\,\hat g_2},\qquad
 Z_M = \frac{W^3_M - B_M}{\hat g_Z},\qquad A_M = \frac{\hat g_1^2\,W^3_M + \hat g_2^2\,B_M}{\hat g_1\hat
 g_2\hat g_Z},\\
&\hat g_Z = \sqrt{\hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2},\qquad \cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \frac{\hat g_2}{\hat g_Z},\qquad
 \sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \frac{\hat g_1}{\hat g_Z}
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-10}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-10).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(W^\pm_M,\ W^+,\ W^-\): The charged weak bosons, canonically
  normalised:
  \(W^\pm_M = (W^1_M \mp \mathrm{i}W^2_M)/(\sqrt2\,\hat g_2)\); \(W^+\)
  carries the electric charge \(+e\) and \(W^-\) the charge \(-e\) (they
  are each other's antiparticles and have one mass). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(Z_M\): The \(Z\) boson field, canonically normalised,
  \(Z_M = (W^3_M - B_M)/\hat g_Z\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\hat g_Z\): The eight-dimensional coupling of the \(Z\),
  \(\hat g_Z = (\hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2)^{1/2}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A_M\): The photon field, canonically normalised,
  \(A_M = (\hat g_1^2W^3_M + \hat g_2^2B_M)/(\hat g_1\hat g_2\hat g_Z)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The weak mixing angle of the couplings,
  \(\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_2/\hat g_Z\),
  \(\sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_1/\hat g_Z\); fixed by the measured
  \(W\) and \(Z\) masses with \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.776368\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The physical electroweak fields are the canonically normalised
combinations of the SU(2) and hypercharge fields: \(W^+\) and \(W^-\)
from \(W^1\) and \(W^2\), the \(Z\) and the photon \(A\) from \(W^3\)
and \(B\), rotated by the weak mixing angle \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of
the couplings. \(W^+\) carries the electric charge \(+e\) and \(W^-\)
the charge \(-e\) (DERIVED from the charge operator of IN-12); they are
each other's antiparticles. Status: STANDARD.

\hypertarget{the-standard-model-fermions}{%
\subsection{The Standard-Model
fermions}\label{the-standard-model-fermions}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_{\mathrm{f}} &= \sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\psi}\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert
 g\rvert}\,\Big[\tfrac12\big(\bar\psi_i\gamma^MD_M\psi_i
 - (D_M\bar\psi_i)\gamma^M\psi_i\big) - \mu_{\psi i}\,\bar\psi_i\psi_i\Big],\\
\psi &\in \{Q,\ u_R,\ d_R,\ \ell,\ e_R,\ \nu_R\},\qquad \mu_{\psi i} = \mu_Q\ (\psi = Q),\qquad
 \mu_{\psi i} = \mu_\ell\ (\psi = \ell)
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-11}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-11).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{f}}\): The action of the Standard-Model fermions of three
  generations (kinetic and bulk-mass terms). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\): The bulk mass of a Standard-Model fermion:
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\) of the multiplet \(\psi\) of the generation \(i\);
  with the hidden direction it fixes the localisation \(c = f\mu L\) of
  the zero mode. Unit: GeV. Value: \(\mu_{\psi i} = c/(fL)\) with the
  localisation \(c\) of the zero mode (doublets: \(\mu_Q = 5.2304\,H\),
  \(\mu_\ell = 9.2055\,H\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(e_R,\ e_{Ri}\): The right-handed charged-lepton singlet: \(e_R\) the
  multiplet, \(e_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(e_R\),
  \(\mu_R\), \(\tau_R\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\nu_R,\ \nu_{Ri}\): The right-handed neutrino (a gauge singlet):
  \(\nu_R\) the multiplet, \(\nu_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\);
  its zero modes give Dirac neutrino masses. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mu_Q\): The common bulk mass of the three quark doublets. Unit:
  GeV. Value: \(5.2304\,H\) (\(c_Q = 3.38230\)). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mu_\ell\): The common bulk mass of the three lepton doublets. Unit:
  GeV. Value: \(9.2055\,H\) (\(c_\ell = 5.95288\)). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

Each generation \(i = 1, 2, 3\) contains the quark doublet \(Q_i\)
(left-handed up and down quarks, three colours, hypercharge
\(\frac16\)), the up-type singlet \(u_{Ri}\) (\(\frac23\)), the
down-type singlet \(d_{Ri}\) (\(-\frac13\)), the lepton doublet
\(\ell_i\) (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton, \(-\frac12\)), the
charged-lepton singlet \(e_{Ri}\) (\(-1\)) and the right-handed neutrino
\(\nu_{Ri}\) (a gauge singlet). These are eight-dimensional spinor
fields with bulk masses \(\mu_{\psi i}\); the doublets of a sector share
one bulk mass (\(\mu_Q\), \(\mu_\ell\)). The bulk mass fixes the
localisation of the four-dimensional zero mode along \(x_0\), and with
the tip mixing it fixes the four-dimensional mass (FE-56 to FE-60). With
this content the gauge anomalies cancel generation by generation,
exactly as in the Standard Model (DERIVED, exact fractions; a lepton
generation without its quark generation would leave the anomalies
\(\mathfrak{A}_{\mathrm{SU(2)^2U(1)}} = -1\) and
\(\mathfrak{A}_{\mathrm{U(1)^3}} = 3/2\)): the three quark generations
are required together with the three lepton generations. Status: ASSUMED
(the eight-dimensional form; the boundary conditions FE-29 are
INVENTED).

\[
D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M - \mathrm{i}G^A_M\,t^A - \mathrm{i}W^I_M\,\frac{\tau^I}{2} - \mathrm{i}\,Y
 B_M,\qquad
Q_{\mathrm{em}} = T_3 + Y \tag{IN-12}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-12).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Omega_M\): The spin connection acting on spinors, the matrix
  \(\Omega_M = \frac12\omega_{Mab}\mathcal{S}^{ab}\). Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(t^A\): The generators of SU(3) in the fundamental representation
  (the Gell-Mann matrices divided by 2),
  \(\mathrm{tr}(t^At^B) = \frac12\delta^{AB}\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(Y\): The hypercharge (the generator of U(1)\(_Y\)); \(Y_\psi\) the
  hypercharge of the multiplet \(\psi\) (\(Q\): \(\frac16\), \(u_R\):
  \(\frac23\), \(d_R\): \(-\frac13\), \(\ell\): \(-\frac12\), \(e_R\):
  \(-1\), \(\nu_R\): 0). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(Q_{\mathrm{em}}\): The electric charge in units of the elementary
  charge, \(Q_{\mathrm{em}} = T_3 + Y\); \(Q^f_{\mathrm{em}}\) that of
  the fermion \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_3\): The third component of the weak isospin (\(+\frac12\) for the
  upper and \(-\frac12\) for the lower member of a doublet, 0 for a
  singlet); \(T_3^f\) that of the fermion \(f\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

Every fermion couples to gravity through the spin connection and to the
gauge fields through its covariant derivative, with the generators of
its representation (\(t^A\) for quarks, \(\tau^I/2\) for doublets, the
hypercharge \(Y\)). The electric charge is
\(Q_{\mathrm{em}} = T_3 + Y\): \(+\frac23\) and \(-\frac13\) for the
quarks, 0 and \(-1\) for the leptons. Status: STANDARD.

\hypertarget{the-tip-the-sigma-field-and-the-yukawa-couplings-no-higgs-particle}{%
\subsection{The tip: the sigma field and the Yukawa couplings (no Higgs
particle)}\label{the-tip-the-sigma-field-and-the-yukawa-couplings-no-higgs-particle}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_{\mathrm{tip}} &= \int_{y=-L}\mathrm{d}^7X_{\mathrm{tip}}\sqrt{\lvert g_{\mathrm{tip}}\rvert}\,\Big[
 -\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\mathrm{tr}\big((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma\big) -
 \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}}\Big],\\
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}} &=
 \sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\Big[\bar\ell_i\,\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_\nu)_{ij}\,\nu_{Rj}\\
 (\mathcal{Y}_e)_{ij}\,e_{Rj}\end{pmatrix}
 + \bar Q_i\,\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_u)_{ij}\,u_{Rj}\\ (\mathcal{Y}_d)_{ij}\,d_{Rj}
 \end{pmatrix}\Big]
 + \text{h.c.}
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-13}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-13).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The action on the tip \(y = -L\): the sigma
  field and the tip Yukawa couplings. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The seven coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_7\) of the
  tip \(y = -L\) of the hidden direction;
  \(\mathrm{d}^7X_{\mathrm{tip}}\) is their coordinate volume element.
  Unit: length (each coordinate). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(g_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The metric induced on the seven-dimensional tip
  \(y = -L\) (the metric \(g_{MN}\) restricted to the tip directions
  \(x_1, \dots, x_7\)); \(\lvert g_{\mathrm{tip}}\rvert\) is the
  absolute value of its determinant. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}}\): The tip Yukawa Lagrangian: the couplings
  of the doublets to the singlets through the sigma field and the tip
  matrices \(\mathcal{Y}\) (plus the Hermitian conjugate, h.c.). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu,\ \mathcal{Y}_e,\ \mathcal{Y}_u,\ \mathcal{Y}_d\):
  The complex \(3 \times 3\) tip Yukawa matrices of the neutrino,
  charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors (generation indices
  \(i, j\)); on the static block they become the tip mixing matrices
  \(\mathcal{K}\) (the normalisation of that relation is not fixed:
  OPEN). Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\nu_R,\ \nu_{Ri}\): The right-handed neutrino (a gauge singlet):
  \(\nu_R\) the multiplet, \(\nu_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\);
  its zero modes give Dirac neutrino masses. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(e_R,\ e_{Ri}\): The right-handed charged-lepton singlet: \(e_R\) the
  multiplet, \(e_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(e_R\),
  \(\mu_R\), \(\tau_R\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

At the regular tip \(y = -L\) of the hidden direction, a non-linear
sigma field \(\Sigma\) (a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix) breaks the
electroweak symmetry. Its kinetic term is the gauged non-linear sigma
model of the electroweak chiral Lagrangian, located on the
seven-dimensional tip: it gives masses to \(W^+\), \(W^-\) and \(Z\) and
none to the photon (IN-15). \(\Sigma\) has no radial mode, so there is
no Higgs particle; its three modes become the longitudinal \(W^+\),
\(W^-\) and \(Z\). The Yukawa terms \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}}\) couple
the doublets to the singlets through \(\Sigma\) with complex
\(3 \times 3\) matrices \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu\), \(\mathcal{Y}_e\),
\(\mathcal{Y}_u\), \(\mathcal{Y}_d\) (generation indices \(i\), \(j\));
they mix the doublet and singlet zero modes at the tip and give the
fermions their masses and their mixing (FE-30, FE-59, FE-60). Every term
is invariant under SU(2) \(\times\) U(1)\(_Y\) (IN-14), so the
construction is consistent with the electroweak theory at tree level; it
is the Higgsless limit of the Standard Model, which conflicts with the
observed Higgs boson at 125 GeV and lets the longitudinal \(W^+W^-\)
scattering violate perturbative unitarity near 1.2 TeV (a conflict with
data inherent in the order to omit the Higgs particle). Status:
INVENTED.

\[
D_\mu\Sigma = \partial_\mu\Sigma - \mathrm{i}W^I_\mu\,\frac{\tau^I}{2}\,\Sigma +
 \mathrm{i}B_\mu\,\Sigma\,\frac{\tau^3}{2},\qquad
\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1},\qquad \Sigma \to U_L\,\Sigma\,U_Y^\dagger,\qquad
U_Y = \mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\vartheta\tau^3/2} \tag{IN-14}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-14).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U_L\): An SU(2) gauge transformation (a unitary \(2 \times 2\)
  matrix acting on doublets from the left). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(U_Y\): A U(1)\(_Y\) gauge transformation of the sigma field,
  \(U_Y = \mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\vartheta\tau^3/2}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vartheta\): The angle (gauge parameter) of a U(1)\(_Y\)
  transformation. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The covariant derivative of \(\Sigma\) contains the SU(2) field from the
left and the hypercharge field from the right, so that \(\Sigma\)
transforms as \(\Sigma \to U_L\,\Sigma\,U_Y^\dagger\). The tip terms are
invariant under this transformation for arbitrary complex tip matrices
(DERIVED with random group elements, deviation \(1.1\times10^{-14}\)).
Status: INVENTED.

\[
-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\mathrm{tr}\big((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma\big)\Big\vert_{\Sigma = \mathbf{1}}
= -\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{8}\Big[2\hat g_2^2\,W^+_\mu W^-{}^\mu + \hat g_Z^2\,Z_\mu Z^\mu\Big] \tag{IN-15}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-15).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(W^\pm_M,\ W^+,\ W^-\): The charged weak bosons, canonically
  normalised:
  \(W^\pm_M = (W^1_M \mp \mathrm{i}W^2_M)/(\sqrt2\,\hat g_2)\); \(W^+\)
  carries the electric charge \(+e\) and \(W^-\) the charge \(-e\) (they
  are each other's antiparticles and have one mass). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_Z\): The eight-dimensional coupling of the \(Z\),
  \(\hat g_Z = (\hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2)^{1/2}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Z_M\): The \(Z\) boson field, canonically normalised,
  \(Z_M = (W^3_M - B_M)/\hat g_Z\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

In the unitary gauge \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) the sigma-field kinetic
term becomes a mass term of \(W^+\), \(W^-\) and \(Z\) on the tip.
\(W^+\) and \(W^-\) share one mass (particle and antiparticle); the
\(Z\) term has the coefficient \(\hat g_Z^2 = \hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2\)
instead of \(\hat g_2^2\) (at leading order the \(Z\) is heavier by
\(1/\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\), the custodial relation); the photon
receives no term and stays massless. On the static block this term
becomes the Robin condition of the \(W^\pm\) and \(Z\) profiles (FE-51).
Status: DERIVED.

\hypertarget{the-portals-of-dirac16complex}{%
\subsection{The portals of
dirac16complex}\label{the-portals-of-dirac16complex}}

\(\Psi\) is a singlet of the Standard-Model gauge group, so it can
couple to the Standard Model only through gauge-invariant composite
operators of the Standard-Model fields. UM-2 contains three of them;
they respect every symmetry of QCD and of the electroweak theory, and
none of them breaks a gauge symmetry or gives a gauge boson a mass.
Their strengths are fixed by the cosmology (the relic density of the
dark matter, the freeze-in of the fold bands, partial-wave unitarity).

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_{\mathrm{I11}} &= \int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\;\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,
 \big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^M\Psi\big)\,J^{B-L}_M,\qquad
 J^{B-L}_M = \sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{\psi} q_\psi\,\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\psi_i\gamma_M\psi_i\big),\\
q_Q &= q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13,\qquad q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-16}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-16).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I11}}\): The action of the portal I11 (dirac16complex
  vector current times the \(B - L\) current of the fermions). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J^{B-L}_M\): The \(B - L\) current of the Standard-Model fermions
  (baryon minus lepton number), summed over the generations. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
