  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

I11 couples the vector current of \(\Psi\) (both halves, including the
massless fold bands) to the \(B - L\) current of the Standard-Model
fermions of three generations: baryon number minus lepton number, with
the charges \(q_\psi\). \(B - L\) is conserved in the Standard Model
and, with the right-handed neutrinos, free of gauge anomalies, so I11 is
consistent with QCD and the electroweak theory; every term is separately
gauge invariant and generation blind. \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\) is
dimensionless and \(\Lambda_\star\) is the common scale of the portals.
On the zero modes it becomes the contact interaction FE-70; its
benchmark strength keeps the fold bands out of equilibrium (FE-129).
Status: INVENTED.

\[
S_{\mathrm{I12}} = -\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert
 g\rvert}\;\frac{\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3}\,\mathcal{F},\qquad
\mathcal{F} = c_{\mathrm{G}}\,G^A_{MN}G^{AMN} + c_{\mathrm{W}}\,W^I_{MN}W^{IMN} +
 c_{\mathrm{B}}\,B_{MN}B^{MN} \tag{IN-17}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-17).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I12}}\): The action of the portal I12 (scalar density of
  dirac16complex times the gauge kinetic invariants). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{F}\): The gauge-kinetic operator of the portal I12,
  \(\mathcal{F} = c_{\mathrm{G}}G^A_{MN}G^{AMN} + c_{\mathrm{W}}W^I_{MN}W^{IMN} + c_{\mathrm{B}}B_{MN}B^{MN}\);
  \(\langle\mathcal{F}\rangle\) its mean value in an environment. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}\): The coefficient of the gluon term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}\): The coefficient of the SU(2) term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}\): The coefficient of the hypercharge term of the
  portal I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

I12 couples the scalar density \(\bar\Psi\Psi\) of dirac16complex to the
gauge-kinetic invariants of the three gauge groups. Each invariant is
gauge invariant and a Lorentz scalar; the coupling acts as a
field-dependent gauge coupling (FE-21) and does not break any symmetry
of QCD or of the electroweak theory. On the dark matter it is FE-71; it
is set to the unitarity-safe benchmark. Status: INVENTED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
S_{\mathrm{I13}} &= \int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\;\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}
 \sum_{M\ne x_0}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^MP_b\Psi\big)\,J^{B}_M,\qquad P_b = \frac{1 - \gamma^{x_0}}{2},
 \qquad P_a = \frac{1 + \gamma^{x_0}}{2},\\
J^{B}_M &= \frac13\sum_{i=1}^{3}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar Q_i\gamma_MQ_i + \mathrm{i}\bar u_{Ri}\gamma_Mu_{Ri}
 + \mathrm{i}\bar d_{Ri}\gamma_Md_{Ri}\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{IN-18}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (IN-18).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The action of the portal I13 (the \(x_0\)-chiral
  vector current of dirac16complex times the baryon-number current).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(J^{B}_M\): The baryon-number current of the quarks (one third per
  quark), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

I13 couples the \(x_0\)-chiral vector current of \(\Psi\) (the projector
\(P_b\) selects one \(x_0\)-chirality) to the baryon-number current of
the quarks (one third per quark, three generations). The sum leaves out
the direction \(x_0\), for which the bilinear is not Hermitian (DERIVED
with the author's matrices: \(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^MP_b\Psi\) is
Hermitian for \(M \ne x_0\) only). Baryon number is conserved in QCD and
in the perturbative electroweak theory, and \(J^B_M\) is gauge
invariant, so I13 is consistent with both. On the zero modes it is the
contact interaction FE-70, whose strength is fixed by the relic density
of the dark matter (FE-126); it vanishes on the fold bands (to
\(7\times10^{-14}\)), which have no lower component. Status: INVENTED.

\hypertarget{gravity-1}{%
\section{Gravity}\label{gravity-1}}

\hypertarget{the-einstein-lovelock-equations}{%
\subsection{The Einstein-Lovelock
equations}\label{the-einstein-lovelock-equations}}

Varying the gravitational action IN-2 with respect to the inverse metric
gives, up to a total derivative,

\[
\Delta S_{\mathrm{EL}} = \frac{1}{2\kappa}\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Big(\sum_{p=1}^{3}\alpha_{(p)}\,\mathcal{E}_{(p)MN}
 + \Lambda\,g_{MN}\Big)\Delta g^{MN},
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\): The Einstein-Lovelock action of gravity. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value:
  \(\kappa/V_\perp = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 2.328\times10^{-36}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(p\): The Lovelock order \(p = 1, 2, 3\) (Einstein, Gauss-Bonnet,
  cubic). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{(p)}\): The Lovelock coupling of order \(p\),
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) (\(\alpha_{(1)} = 1\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2-2p}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N\): The Lovelock tensor of order \(p\), the
  metric variation of \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\):
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(1)}\) is the Einstein tensor
  \(R^M{}_N - \frac12\delta^M_NR\); every \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}\) is
  divergence free. Unit: 1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2p}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda\): The eight-dimensional cosmological constant,
  \(\Lambda = -10H^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Value: \(-10H^2\).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

because the variation of \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\) is
\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathcal{E}_{(p)MN}\Delta g^{MN}\) (the
defining property of the Lovelock tensor \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}\),
STANDARD) and the variation of \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\) is
\(-\frac12\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,g_{MN}\Delta g^{MN}\). The variation of
the rest of the action defines the energy-momentum tensor (FE-2),
\(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}} = -\frac12\int\mathrm{d}^8X\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\, \mathcal{T}_{MN}\Delta g^{MN}\);
the sum of the two vanishes for every \(\Delta g^{MN}\):

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\sum_{p=1}^{3}\alpha_{(p)}\,\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N + \Lambda\,\delta^M_N = \kappa\,\mathcal{T}^M{}_N,\\
&\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N = -\frac{1}{2^{p+1}}\,\delta^{MM_1N_1\cdots M_pN_p}_{NP_1Q_1\cdots P_pQ_p}\,
 R^{P_1Q_1}{}_{M_1N_1}\cdots R^{P_pQ_p}{}_{M_pN_p}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-1}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-1).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(p\): The Lovelock order \(p = 1, 2, 3\) (Einstein, Gauss-Bonnet,
  cubic). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{(p)}\): The Lovelock coupling of order \(p\),
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) (\(\alpha_{(1)} = 1\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2-2p}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N\): The Lovelock tensor of order \(p\), the
  metric variation of \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\):
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(1)}\) is the Einstein tensor
  \(R^M{}_N - \frac12\delta^M_NR\); every \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}\) is
  divergence free. Unit: 1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2p}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda\): The eight-dimensional cosmological constant,
  \(\Lambda = -10H^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Value: \(-10H^2\).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{\alpha e}\),
  \(\delta_{l0}\), \(\delta^{I3}\)); with upper and lower index strings
  the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\) (+1
  or -1 if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation of the lower
  ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value:
  \(\kappa/V_\perp = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 2.328\times10^{-36}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}^M{}_N\): The energy-momentum tensor of all matter (every
  field except gravity, the stabiliser included), the source of gravity;
  defined by the change of the matter action under a change of the
  metric. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(Q_1, Q_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(R^{MN}{}_{PQ},\ R_{MN},\ R\): The curvature of the metric
  \(g_{MN}\): with four indices the Riemann tensor, with two indices the
  Ricci tensor \(R_{MN} = R^P{}_{MPN}\), without indices the Ricci
  (scalar) curvature \(R = g^{MN}R_{MN}\); \(R^{ab}{}_{ab}\) (no sum) is
  the sectional curvature of the coordinate plane \((a, b)\). Unit:
  1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(\mathcal{E}_{(1)}\) is the Einstein tensor
\(R^M{}_N - \frac12\delta^M_NR\) of general relativity;
\(\mathcal{E}_{(2)}\) (Gauss-Bonnet) and \(\mathcal{E}_{(3)}\) (cubic
Lovelock) are the higher-order Lovelock tensors, products of two and
three curvature tensors contracted with the generalised Kronecker delta,
weighted by \(\alpha_{(2)} = H^{-2}/32\) and
\(\alpha_{(3)} = H^{-4}/1152\). \(\Lambda\delta^M_N\) is the
eight-dimensional cosmological constant. The right side is the
energy-momentum tensor of every field (FE-3), multiplied by the
gravitational coupling \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). These equations couple the
metric to every field of UM-2. Each \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}\) contains at
most second derivatives of the metric, so the system is of second order.
Status: AUTHOR (the weights and \(\Lambda\)), the equations DERIVED from
the action. They are solved exactly at order 0 (FE-38 to FE-43: the
residual of the stored static block in the printed equations is
\(3.9\times10^{-13}\) of the largest term) and at the first order
through the four-dimensional reduction (FE-48, FE-49).

\hypertarget{the-energy-momentum-tensors}{%
\subsection{The energy-momentum
tensors}\label{the-energy-momentum-tensors}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}} = -\frac12\int\mathrm{d}^8X\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\;\mathcal{T}_{MN}\,\Delta
 g^{MN},\qquad
 \rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4},\qquad p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\quad \text{($M \ne x_4$, no sum)},\\
&\mathcal{T}^M{}_N = \mathrm{diag}\big(p_0,\ p_3,\ p_3,\ p_3,\ -\rho,\ p_5,\ p_5,\ p_5\big)\quad
 \text{(a diagonal source)},\qquad \bar p = \frac{p_0 + 3p_5}{4}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-2}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-2).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(S_{\mathrm{m}}\): The matter action: all terms of the action except
  \(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\) (the stabiliser included). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}^M{}_N\): The energy-momentum tensor of all matter (every
  field except gravity, the stabiliser included), the source of gravity;
  defined by the change of the matter action under a change of the
  metric. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_0\): The pressure along the hidden direction \(x_0\),
  \(p_0 = \mathcal{T}^{x_0}{}_{x_0}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_3\): The pressure of ordinary 3-space,
  \(p_3 = \mathcal{T}^{x_1}{}_{x_1} = \mathcal{T}^{x_2}{}_{x_2} = \mathcal{T}^{x_3}{}_{x_3}\)
  for an isotropic source. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_5\): The pressure along the extra times,
  \(p_5 = \mathcal{T}^{x_5}{}_{x_5} = \mathcal{T}^{x_6}{}_{x_6} = \mathcal{T}^{x_7}{}_{x_7}\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar p\): The hidden pressure, the pressure averaged over the block,
  \(\bar p = (p_0 + 3p_5)/4\); for a species at fixed temperature
  \(\bar p = \frac14\,\partial p/\partial\ln f\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (or \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) for a four-dimensional
  density). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(\mathcal{T}_{MN}\) is the response of the matter
action \(S_{\mathrm{m}}\) (every term of IN-1 except
\(S_{\mathrm{EL}}\)) to a change \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric (for
spinors the change is made in the vielbein, which carries the metric).
Its time-time component gives the energy density \(\rho\) and its
diagonal space-like components the pressures \(p_M\) along each
direction: \(p_0\) along the hidden direction \(x_0\), \(p_3\) along
ordinary space, \(p_5\) along the extra times. Every source of UM-2 is
diagonal on the order-0 metric, with one pressure for \(x_1, x_2, x_3\)
and one for \(x_5, x_6, x_7\); \(\bar p\) is the pressure averaged over
the four block directions, the hidden pressure. Status: STANDARD.

\[
\mathcal{T}^M{}_N = \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{stab}}{}^M{}_N + \mathcal{T}_\Psi{}^M{}_N +
 \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{gauge}}{}^M{}_N
 + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{f}}{}^M{}_N + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{tip}}{}^M{}_N + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I11}}{}^M{}_N
 + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I12}}{}^M{}_N + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I13}}{}^M{}_N \tag{FE-3}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-3).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}^M{}_N\): The energy-momentum tensor of all matter (every
  field except gravity, the stabiliser included), the source of gravity;
  defined by the change of the matter action under a change of the
  metric. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  stabiliser. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_\Psi\): The energy-momentum tensor of the dirac16complex
  field. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{gauge}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  gauge fields. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{f}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  Standard-Model fermions. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the tip
  terms (non-zero only for the tip directions). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I11}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I11. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I12}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I13. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The total source is the sum of the energy-momentum
tensors of the stabiliser (FE-4), of dirac16complex (FE-5), of the
Standard-Model fermions (FE-6), of the gauge fields (FE-7), of the tip
terms (FE-8) and of the three portals (FE-9, FE-10). At order 0 the
stabiliser dominates; the matter terms enter the block through their
block averages (FE-45 to FE-47): today their size relative to the
stabiliser is \(2.4\times10^{-93}\), at the dark-matter freeze-out
\(3.0\times10^{-32}\) (DERIVED from the stored sources). Status:
DERIVED.

The stabiliser density depends on the metric only through the block
scale \(f\), the fourth root of the proper volume of the block
directions. A change of the metric changes \(f\) by

\[
\Delta\ln f = -\frac18\sum_{M\in\{x_0,\,x_5,\,x_6,\,x_7\}}g_{MM}\,\Delta g^{MM},\qquad
\Delta\big(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\big) = \sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Big(-\frac12\,\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\,g_{MN}\Delta g^{MN}
 + f\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}}{\mathrm{d}f}\,\Delta\ln f\Big),
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_5\): The first of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_6\): The second of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_7\): The third of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The energy density of the block stabiliser,
  a function of the block scale,
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}} \propto f^{-8}\); its value at the static block
  is \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*)\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Value:
  \(\kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*) = (26 + 4\sqrt{51})H^2 = 54.566\,H^2\).
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

and for \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}} \propto f^{-8}\) (IN-4),
\(f\,\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}/\mathrm{d}f = -8\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\):
the second term turns the coefficient of the block components from
\(-\frac12\) into \(+\frac12\), so that
\(\mathcal{T}_{MN} = -g_{MN}\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\) for the observable
directions \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) and
\(\mathcal{T}_{MN} = +g_{MN}\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\) for the block
directions:

\[
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{stab}}{}^M{}_N =
 \rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\;\mathrm{diag}(1,\ -1,\ -1,\ -1,\ -1,\ 1,\ 1,\ 1):\qquad
\rho = \rho_{\mathrm{stab}},\qquad p_3 = -\rho_{\mathrm{stab}},\qquad p_0 = p_5 = \rho_{\mathrm{stab}}
 \tag{FE-4}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-4).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  stabiliser. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\): The energy density of the block stabiliser,
  a function of the block scale,
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}} \propto f^{-8}\); its value at the static block
  is \(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*)\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Value:
  \(\kappa\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}(f_*) = (26 + 4\sqrt{51})H^2 = 54.566\,H^2\).
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_3\): The pressure of ordinary 3-space,
  \(p_3 = \mathcal{T}^{x_1}{}_{x_1} = \mathcal{T}^{x_2}{}_{x_2} = \mathcal{T}^{x_3}{}_{x_3}\)
  for an isotropic source. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_0\): The pressure along the hidden direction \(x_0\),
  \(p_0 = \mathcal{T}^{x_0}{}_{x_0}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_5\): The pressure along the extra times,
  \(p_5 = \mathcal{T}^{x_5}{}_{x_5} = \mathcal{T}^{x_6}{}_{x_6} = \mathcal{T}^{x_7}{}_{x_7}\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The stabiliser is a vacuum energy of the block: in
ordinary space it acts like a cosmological constant (energy density
\(\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\), pressure \(p_3 = -\rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\)),
along the block directions it pushes outwards with the pressures
\(p_0 = p_5 = \rho_{\mathrm{stab}}\). It is block-isotropic
(\(p_0 = p_5\)). It is conserved (FE-41 with \(\mathcal{H}_f\):
\(\dot\rho = -4\mathcal{H}_f(\rho + \bar p) = -8\mathcal{H}_f\rho\)
gives \(\rho \propto f^{-8}\), consistent with IN-4). Its value at the
static block is fixed in FE-43. Status: DERIVED (from the ASSUMED IN-4).

\[
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{T}_\Psi)_{MN} &= -\frac14\Big[\bar\Psi\gamma_MD_N\Psi + \bar\Psi\gamma_ND_M\Psi -
 (D_M\bar\Psi)\gamma_N\Psi
 - (D_N\bar\Psi)\gamma_M\Psi\Big] + g_{MN}\,\mathcal{L}_\Psi,\\
\mathcal{L}_\Psi &= \tfrac12\big(\bar\Psi\gamma^PD_P\Psi - (D_P\bar\Psi)\gamma^P\Psi\big) -
 m_\Psi\,\bar\Psi\Psi
 - \frac{\lambda}{2}\big(\bar\Psi\Psi\big)^2
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-5}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-5).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_\Psi\): The energy-momentum tensor of the dirac16complex
  field. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_\Psi\): The Lagrangian density of dirac16complex (the
  bracket of \(S_\Psi\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex,
  \(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M} = H\). Unit: GeV. Value: 14.296 TeV. Status:
  RECORD.
\item
  \(\lambda\): The contact self-coupling of dirac16complex (the term
  \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) of the record). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value: not fixed; Kohn-Sham states computed for
  \(\lambda = 0\) and \(\pm7\times10^{-5}H^{-6}\); the fold-band
  radiation requires
  \(\lvert\lambda\rvert \lesssim 1.4\times10^{-10}H^{-6}\) (for
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} \le 0.001\)). Status: RECORD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The first bracket is the symmetrised kinetic stress
of the spinor (each of \(\gamma_M D_N\) and \(\gamma_N D_M\), acting on
\(\Psi\) and on \(\bar\Psi\), with the spin connection inside \(D\));
\(g_{MN}\mathcal{L}_\Psi\) comes from the variation of
\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\). \(\mathcal{L}_\Psi\) contains the mass
\(m_\Psi\) and the contact self-interaction \(\lambda\). It couples
dirac16complex to gravity. For the Kohn-Sham states of dirac16complex it
takes the form FE-67 (verified by the energy identity and the
\(y\)-conservation law FE-68). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{f}})_{MN} &= \sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_\psi\Big\{-\frac14\Big[\bar\psi_i\gamma_MD_N\psi_i
 + \bar\psi_i\gamma_ND_M\psi_i - (D_M\bar\psi_i)\gamma_N\psi_i - (D_N\bar\psi_i)\gamma_M\psi_i\Big]
 + g_{MN}\,\mathcal{L}_{\psi i}\Big\},\\
\mathcal{L}_{\psi i} &= \tfrac12\big(\bar\psi_i\gamma^PD_P\psi_i - (D_P\bar\psi_i)\gamma^P\psi_i\big)
 - \mu_{\psi i}\,\bar\psi_i\psi_i
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-6}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-6).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{f}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  Standard-Model fermions. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{\psi i}\): The Lagrangian density of the fermion
  multiplet \(\psi\) of the generation \(i\) (the bracket of
  \(S_{\mathrm{f}}\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\): The bulk mass of a Standard-Model fermion:
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\) of the multiplet \(\psi\) of the generation \(i\);
  with the hidden direction it fixes the localisation \(c = f\mu L\) of
  the zero mode. Unit: GeV. Value: \(\mu_{\psi i} = c/(fL)\) with the
  localisation \(c\) of the zero mode (doublets: \(\mu_Q = 5.2304\,H\),
  \(\mu_\ell = 9.2055\,H\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The same form for every Standard-Model fermion
multiplet \(\psi\) of every generation \(i\), with the bulk mass
\(\mu_{\psi i}\) in place of \(m_\Psi\), no self-interaction, and the
gauge fields in \(D\). It enters the block through the block averages of
the plasma and of cold matter (ideal and degenerate gases of particles
with their \(x_0\) masses) in the block response FE-45 and in the hidden
pressure FE-81. Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{gauge}})_{MN} ={}& \frac{1}{\hat g_3^2}\Big(G^A_{MP}G^A{}_N{}^P -
 \frac14g_{MN}G^A_{PQ}G^{APQ}\Big)
 + \frac{1}{\hat g_2^2}\Big(W^I_{MP}W^I{}_N{}^P - \frac14g_{MN}W^I_{PQ}W^{IPQ}\Big)\\
&+ \frac{1}{\hat g_1^2}\Big(B_{MP}B_N{}^P - \frac14g_{MN}B_{PQ}B^{PQ}\Big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-7}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-7).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{gauge}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  gauge fields. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_3\): The eight-dimensional SU(3) coupling (it multiplies the
  gluon kinetic term as \(1/\hat g_3^2\));
  \(g_3^2 = \hat g_3^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(Q\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(Q_1, Q_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The standard energy-momentum tensor of the gauge
fields (geometric normalisation, so each group carries its inverse
squared coupling): the stress \(F_{MP}F_N{}^P\) minus the trace part. It
is traceless in four dimensions, so radiation drops out of the block
response (FE-47: \(c_3 = -3c_\rho\)); what remains is the trace anomaly
of the interacting plasma (FE-75, FE-82). Status: STANDARD.

\[
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{tip}})_{\mu\nu} &=
 \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\Big[\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\mathrm{tr}\big((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D_\nu\Sigma
 + (D_\nu\Sigma)^\dagger D_\mu\Sigma\big) + g_{\mu\nu}\,\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{tip}}\Big],\qquad
 \delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \frac{\delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)}{f},\\
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{tip}} &= -\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\mathrm{tr}\big((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger
 D^\mu\Sigma\big) - \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-8}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-8).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the tip
  terms (non-zero only for the tip directions). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over
  \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (as \(\mu\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The Lagrangian density of the tip
  action (the bracket of \(S_{\mathrm{tip}}\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{D}}\): The Dirac delta function:
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)\) is zero except at \(y = -L\) and has
  the unit integral over \(y\). Unit: 1/length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Y}}\): The tip Yukawa Lagrangian: the couplings
  of the doublets to the singlets through the sigma field and the tip
  matrices \(\mathcal{Y}\) (plus the Hermitian conjugate, h.c.). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The tip terms act only on the tip \(y = -L\):
\(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) is the Dirac
delta function in proper length. The first part is the stress of the
sigma field, the second the tip Lagrangian (sigma field and Yukawa
terms) times the metric; the indices \(\mu, \nu\) run over the tip
directions, and the components along \(y\) vanish. Its gravitational
effect is part of the rest energies of \(W^\pm\), \(Z\) and the
fermions, which are counted in the plasma. Status: DERIVED.

\[
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I11}})_{MN} =
 g_{MN}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^P\Psi\big)J^{B-L}_P,
\qquad (\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I13}})_{MN} = g_{MN}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}
 \sum_{P\ne x_0}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^PP_b\Psi\big)J^{B}_P \tag{FE-9}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-9).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I11}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I11. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J^{B-L}_M\): The \(B - L\) current of the Standard-Model fermions
  (baryon minus lepton number), summed over the generations. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I13. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(J^{B}_M\): The baryon-number current of the quarks (one third per
  quark), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The portal currents are frame currents,
\(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma^a\Psi\) and
\(\mathrm{i}\bar\psi\gamma^a\psi\), which do not depend on the vielbein;
only \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\) and the explicit metric in the
contraction vary, so the energy-momentum tensor is the metric times the
interaction density. They couple dirac16complex and the fermions to
gravity; their effect on the block is negligible in every environment
(the level shifts FE-72 are below \(10^{-9}\) GeV). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I12}})_{MN} =
 \frac{4\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3}\Big[&c_{\mathrm{G}}\Big(G^A_{MP}G^A{}_N{}^P
 - \frac14g_{MN}G^A_{PQ}G^{APQ}\Big) + c_{\mathrm{W}}\Big(W^I_{MP}W^I{}_N{}^P -
 \frac14g_{MN}W^I_{PQ}W^{IPQ}\Big)\\
 &+ c_{\mathrm{B}}\Big(B_{MP}B_N{}^P - \frac14g_{MN}B_{PQ}B^{PQ}\Big)\Big]
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-10}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-10).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{I12}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of the
  portal I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}\): The coefficient of the gluon term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(P_1, P_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(Q\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(Q_1, Q_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}\): The coefficient of the SU(2) term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}\): The coefficient of the hypercharge term of the
  portal I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} I12 multiplies the gauge kinetic invariants by
\(\bar\Psi\Psi\), so its energy-momentum tensor is that of the gauge
fields (FE-7) with \(1/\hat g_i^2\) replaced by
\(4c\,\bar\Psi\Psi/\Lambda_\star^3\): I12 acts as a field-dependent
gauge coupling (FE-21). Its effect in the coupled solution is at most
\(1.7\times10^{-16}\). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\nabla_M\,\mathcal{T}^M{}_N = 0,\qquad \text{because}\qquad \nabla_M\,\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N = 0\quad
 \text{($p = 1, 2, 3$)} \tag{FE-11}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-11).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}^M{}_N\): The energy-momentum tensor of all matter (every
  field except gravity, the stabiliser included), the source of gravity;
  defined by the change of the matter action under a change of the
  metric. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^M{}_N\): The Lovelock tensor of order \(p\), the
  metric variation of \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\mathcal{L}_{(p)}\):
  \(\mathcal{E}_{(1)}\) is the Einstein tensor
  \(R^M{}_N - \frac12\delta^M_NR\); every \(\mathcal{E}_{(p)}\) is
  divergence free. Unit: 1/\(\mathrm{length}^{2p}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): The Lovelock order \(p = 1, 2, 3\) (Einstein, Gauss-Bonnet,
  cubic). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} Every Lovelock tensor is divergence-free identically
(the Bianchi identities of the Lovelock tensors, STANDARD), so the field
equations FE-1 imply that the total source is conserved; the
conservation holds when the matter fields obey their own field
equations. Status: DERIVED (the Bianchi identities verified for the
order-0 metric for \(p = 1, 2, 3\), sympy, exact). At order 0 the
conservation is FE-41.

\hypertarget{the-matter-fields}{%
\section{The matter fields}\label{the-matter-fields}}

\hypertarget{dirac16complex}{%
\subsection{dirac16complex}\label{dirac16complex}}

The terms of the action that contain \(\bar\Psi\) are \(S_\Psi\) (IN-5)
and the three portals (IN-16 to IN-18). Varying them with respect to
\(\bar\Psi\) (the kinetic term after an integration by parts, which
turns \(-(D_M\bar\Psi)\gamma^M\Psi\) into \(\bar\Psi\gamma^MD_M\Psi\))
gives

\[
\frac{\Delta S}{\Delta\bar\Psi} = \sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Big[\gamma^MD_M\Psi - \Big(m_\Psi + \lambda\,\bar\Psi\Psi
 + \frac{\mathcal{F}}{\Lambda_\star^3}\Big)\Psi + \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,J^{B-L}_M\gamma^M\Psi
 + \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\sum_{M\ne x_0}J^{B}_M\gamma^MP_b\Psi\Big] = 0,
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(S\): The action of UM-2 (the integral of the Lagrangian density over
  spacetime); its variation with respect to every field gives the field
  equations. Unit: dimensionless (\(\hbar = 1\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex,
  \(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M} = H\). Unit: GeV. Value: 14.296 TeV. Status:
  RECORD.
\item
  \(\lambda\): The contact self-coupling of dirac16complex (the term
  \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) of the record). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value: not fixed; Kohn-Sham states computed for
  \(\lambda = 0\) and \(\pm7\times10^{-5}H^{-6}\); the fold-band
  radiation requires
  \(\lvert\lambda\rvert \lesssim 1.4\times10^{-10}H^{-6}\) (for
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} \le 0.001\)). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{F}\): The gauge-kinetic operator of the portal I12,
  \(\mathcal{F} = c_{\mathrm{G}}G^A_{MN}G^{AMN} + c_{\mathrm{W}}W^I_{MN}W^{IMN} + c_{\mathrm{B}}B_{MN}B^{MN}\);
  \(\langle\mathcal{F}\rangle\) its mean value in an environment. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(J^{B-L}_M\): The \(B - L\) current of the Standard-Model fermions
  (baryon minus lepton number), summed over the generations. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(J^{B}_M\): The baryon-number current of the quarks (one third per
  quark), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\end{itemize}

which is the field equation of dirac16complex:

\[
\gamma^MD_M\Psi = \Big[m_\Psi + \lambda\,\bar\Psi\Psi + \frac{\mathcal{F}}{\Lambda_\star^3}\Big]\Psi
 - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,J^{B-L}_M\gamma^M\Psi
 - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\sum_{M\ne x_0}J^{B}_M\gamma^MP_b\Psi \tag{FE-12}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-12).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex,
  \(m_\Psi = 3H\mathcal{M} = H\). Unit: GeV. Value: 14.296 TeV. Status:
  RECORD.
\item
  \(\lambda\): The contact self-coupling of dirac16complex (the term
  \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) of the record). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value: not fixed; Kohn-Sham states computed for
  \(\lambda = 0\) and \(\pm7\times10^{-5}H^{-6}\); the fold-band
  radiation requires
  \(\lvert\lambda\rvert \lesssim 1.4\times10^{-10}H^{-6}\) (for
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} \le 0.001\)). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{F}\): The gauge-kinetic operator of the portal I12,
  \(\mathcal{F} = c_{\mathrm{G}}G^A_{MN}G^{AMN} + c_{\mathrm{W}}W^I_{MN}W^{IMN} + c_{\mathrm{B}}B_{MN}B^{MN}\);
  \(\langle\mathcal{F}\rangle\) its mean value in an environment. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(J^{B-L}_M\): The \(B - L\) current of the Standard-Model fermions
  (baryon minus lepton number), summed over the generations. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(J^{B}_M\): The baryon-number current of the quarks (one third per
  quark), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(\gamma^MD_M\Psi\) is the eight-dimensional Dirac
operator with the canonical spin connection (gravity). On the right,
\(m_\Psi\) is the mass parameter, \(\lambda\bar\Psi\Psi\) the mean field
of the contact self-interaction, and \(\mathcal{F}/\Lambda_\star^3\) the
I12 term: the gauge-field invariants act on \(\Psi\) as an extra,
field-dependent mass. The \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\) term is I11: the \(B - L\)
current of the Standard-Model fermions acts on \(\Psi\) as a vector
potential. The \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\) term is I13: the baryon-number current
acts on the \(x_0\)-chirality selected by \(P_b\) only. So
dirac16complex couples to gravity, to itself, to every fermion (through
\(J^{B-L}\) and \(J^B\)) and to every gauge field (through
\(\mathcal{F}\)). Status: DERIVED. It is reduced exactly to a
two-component problem in every block (FE-56), solved exactly for the
free spectrum (FE-62) and by Kohn-Sham theory with the mean fields of
every other field (FE-63 to FE-73).

\[
\gamma^M\Omega_M = \frac32\,\frac{H}{f}\,\gamma^{x_0} + \Big(\frac{\dot b_4}{b_4} - \frac{\dot
 a_4}{2}\Big)\gamma^{x_4} \tag{FE-13}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-13).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Omega_M\): The spin connection acting on spinors, the matrix
  \(\Omega_M = \frac12\omega_{Mab}\mathcal{S}^{ab}\). Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\dot b_4\): The time derivative \(\mathrm{d}b_4/\mathrm{d}x_4\).
  Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  hidden and extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\dot a_4\): The time derivative \(\mathrm{d}a_4/\mathrm{d}x_4\).
  Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} On the order-0 metric (FE-31) the spin connection
contracted with the Dirac matrices has two parts: a term along
\(\gamma^{x_0}\) of strength \(\frac32H/f\), from the warping of the
extra times along the hidden direction (\(\mathrm{e}^{2Hy}\) in FE-32),
and a term along \(\gamma^{x_4}\) from the time dependence of the metric
functions \(a_4\) and \(b_4\). It vanishes only in flat four-plus-four
space (\(H = 0\), constant \(a_4\), \(b_4\)), so the coupling of every
spinor to gravity is non-trivial. The prefactors of the reduction ansatz
of FE-56 remove it exactly. Status: DERIVED (exact for every \(a_4\),
\(b_4\) and on both halves of a period).

\hypertarget{the-standard-model-fermions-1}{%
\subsection{The Standard-Model
fermions}\label{the-standard-model-fermions-1}}

Varying \(S_{\mathrm{f}}\) (IN-11), the tip action (IN-13) and the
portals I11, I13 with respect to the adjoint of every multiplet gives
its Dirac equation. The quark doublets:

\[
\begin{aligned}
\gamma^MD_MQ_i ={}& \mu_Q\,Q_i -
 \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_Q\,\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_M\Psi\big)\gamma^MQ_i
 - \frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\sum_{M\ne
 x_0}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_MP_b\Psi\big)\gamma^MQ_i\\
 &+ \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_u)_{ij}\,u_{Rj}\\ (
 \mathcal{Y}_d)_{ij}\,d_{Rj}\end{pmatrix}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-14}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-14).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
