  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_Q\): The common bulk mass of the three quark doublets. Unit:
  GeV. Value: \(5.2304\,H\) (\(c_Q = 3.38230\)). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu,\ \mathcal{Y}_e,\ \mathcal{Y}_u,\ \mathcal{Y}_d\):
  The complex \(3 \times 3\) tip Yukawa matrices of the neutrino,
  charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors (generation indices
  \(i, j\)); on the static block they become the tip mixing matrices
  \(\mathcal{K}\) (the normalisation of that relation is not fixed:
  OPEN). Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(\gamma^MD_MQ_i\) contains gravity (the spin
connection) and the gauge fields: gluons, \(W\) and \(B\) (IN-12).
\(\mu_Q\) is the common bulk mass of the three quark doublets, which
localises their zero modes at the fold. The \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\) term is
I11 (the vector current of dirac16complex acting with the \(B - L\)
charge \(q_Q = \frac13\)), the \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\) term I13 (the
\(x_0\)-chiral current of dirac16complex acting with the baryon number
\(\frac13\)). The last term is the tip Yukawa coupling: at \(y = -L\)
the doublet couples, through \(\Sigma\) and the tip matrices
\(\mathcal{Y}_u\), \(\mathcal{Y}_d\), to the singlets \(u_{Rj}\),
\(d_{Rj}\) of every generation \(j\). Status: DERIVED. Solved on the
static block by the zero modes and the tip mixing (FE-56 to FE-60; exact
roots). The portal terms act on the quarks as mean fields of the vector
densities of dirac16complex; they are neglected, because in every
environment the density of the dark matter is far below that of the
Standard-Model fermions, so they are far smaller than the shifts of
dirac16complex by the quarks (FE-72) (ESTIMATE; not computed).

\[
\begin{aligned}
\gamma^MD_Mu_{Ri} ={}& \mu_{\psi i}\,u_{Ri} - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_{u_R}\,
 \big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_M\Psi\big)\gamma^Mu_{Ri}
 - \frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\sum_{M\ne
 x_0}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_MP_b\Psi\big)\gamma^Mu_{Ri}\\
 &+ \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathcal{Y}_u)^*_{ji}\,\big(\Sigma^\dagger Q_j\big)_1\qquad
 (\psi = u_R)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-15}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-15).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\): The bulk mass of a Standard-Model fermion:
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\) of the multiplet \(\psi\) of the generation \(i\);
  with the hidden direction it fixes the localisation \(c = f\mu L\) of
  the zero mode. Unit: GeV. Value: \(\mu_{\psi i} = c/(fL)\) with the
  localisation \(c\) of the zero mode (doublets: \(\mu_Q = 5.2304\,H\),
  \(\mu_\ell = 9.2055\,H\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{A}}\): The coefficient of the \(x_0\)-chiral vector
  portal I13. Unit: dimensionless. Value: fixed through
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter
  relic). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P_a,\ P_b\): The projectors on the two \(x_0\)-chiralities,
  \(P_a = (1 + \gamma^{x_0})/2\) and \(P_b = (1 - \gamma^{x_0})/2\) (in
  a block: the upper and the lower component). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu,\ \mathcal{Y}_e,\ \mathcal{Y}_u,\ \mathcal{Y}_d\):
  The complex \(3 \times 3\) tip Yukawa matrices of the neutrino,
  charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors (generation indices
  \(i, j\)); on the static block they become the tip mixing matrices
  \(\mathcal{K}\) (the normalisation of that relation is not fixed:
  OPEN). Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^*\): Complex conjugation of a number or of each entry of a
  matrix. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The up-type singlets of the three generations (the
right-handed up, charm and top quarks) couple to gravity, gluons and
\(B\) (they carry no weak isospin), have the bulk mass \(\mu_{\psi i}\)
of their generation, feel I11 with \(q_{u_R} = \frac13\) and I13 with
the baryon number \(\frac13\), and couple at the tip to the upper
component of \(\Sigma^\dagger Q_j\) through the conjugate tip matrix.
Status: DERIVED; exact roots for the up, charm and top quarks.

\[
\begin{aligned}
\gamma^MD_Md_{Ri} ={}& \mu_{\psi i}\,d_{Ri} - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_{d_R}\,
 \big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_M\Psi\big)\gamma^Md_{Ri}
 - \frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{A}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\sum_{M\ne
 x_0}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_MP_b\Psi\big)\gamma^Md_{Ri}\\
 &+ \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathcal{Y}_d)^*_{ji}\,\big(\Sigma^\dagger Q_j\big)_2\qquad
 (\psi = d_R)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-16}
\]

\textbf{The terms.} As FE-15 for the down-type singlets (the
right-handed down, strange and bottom quarks) with the lower component
of \(\Sigma^\dagger Q_j\) and the tip matrix \(\mathcal{Y}_d\); in UM-2
the tip mixing matrix of this sector is the CKM matrix (FE-30). Status:
DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
\gamma^MD_M\ell_i ={}& \mu_\ell\,\ell_i - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_\ell\,

 + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_\nu)_{ij}\,\nu_{Rj}\\ (
 \mathcal{Y}_e)_{ij}\,e_{Rj}\end{pmatrix}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-17}
\]

\textbf{The terms.} The lepton doublets contain the three flavours of
left-handed neutrinos and the left-handed charged leptons (electron,
muon, tau). They couple to gravity, \(W\) and \(B\) (no colour), have
the common bulk mass \(\mu_\ell\), feel I11 with \(q_\ell = -1\) and no
I13 term (leptons carry no baryon number), and couple at the tip to the
right-handed neutrinos (through \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu\)) and to the
right-handed charged leptons (through \(\mathcal{Y}_e\)). One doublet
zero mode serves the three generations. Status: DERIVED; exact roots for
the three neutrinos and the three charged leptons.

\[
\gamma^MD_Me_{Ri} = \mu_{\psi i}\,e_{Ri} - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_{e_R}\,
 \big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_M\Psi\big)\gamma^Me_{Ri}
 + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathcal{Y}_e)^*_{ji}\,\big(\Sigma^\dagger\ell_j\big)_2\qquad
 (\psi = e_R) \tag{FE-18}
\]

  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The right-handed charged leptons of the three
generations (electron, muon and tau singlets) couple to gravity and to
\(B\), have their bulk masses, feel I11 with \(q_{e_R} = -1\), and
couple at the tip to the lower component of \(\Sigma^\dagger\ell_j\).
Status: DERIVED; exact roots for the electron, muon and tau with the tip
matrix \(\mathbf{1}\).

\[
\gamma^MD_M\nu_{Ri} = \mu_{\psi i}\,\nu_{Ri} - \mathrm{i}\,\frac{c_{\mathrm{V}}}{\Lambda_\star^6}\,q_{\nu_R}\,
 \big(\mathrm{i}\bar\Psi\gamma_M\Psi\big)\gamma^M\nu_{Ri}
 + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathcal{Y}_\nu)^*_{ji}\,\big(\Sigma^\dagger\ell_j\big)_1\qquad
 (\psi = \nu_R) \tag{FE-19}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-19).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu_R,\ \nu_{Ri}\): The right-handed neutrino (a gauge singlet):
  \(\nu_R\) the multiplet, \(\nu_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\);
  its zero modes give Dirac neutrino masses. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\): The bulk mass of a Standard-Model fermion:
  \(\mu_{\psi i}\) of the multiplet \(\psi\) of the generation \(i\);
  with the hidden direction it fixes the localisation \(c = f\mu L\) of
  the zero mode. Unit: GeV. Value: \(\mu_{\psi i} = c/(fL)\) with the
  localisation \(c\) of the zero mode (doublets: \(\mu_Q = 5.2304\,H\),
  \(\mu_\ell = 9.2055\,H\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{V}}\): The coefficient of the vector portal I11. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: benchmark through
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status:
  INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu,\ \mathcal{Y}_e,\ \mathcal{Y}_u,\ \mathcal{Y}_d\):
  The complex \(3 \times 3\) tip Yukawa matrices of the neutrino,
  charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors (generation indices
  \(i, j\)); on the static block they become the tip mixing matrices
  \(\mathcal{K}\) (the normalisation of that relation is not fixed:
  OPEN). Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^*\): Complex conjugation of a number or of each entry of a
  matrix. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The right-handed neutrinos are gauge singlets: no
gauge field acts on them; they couple to gravity, to dirac16complex
through I11 (\(q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), and at the tip to the upper component
of \(\Sigma^\dagger\ell_j\). Their zero modes are sharply localised at
the fold (\(c_R = -31\) to \(-35\)), so their values at the tip are
tiny; mixed there with the doublet zero mode they give the small Dirac
masses of the three neutrinos (FE-100). They are never thermalised
(FE-134). Status: DERIVED.

\hypertarget{the-gauge-fields-1}{%
\subsection{The gauge fields}\label{the-gauge-fields-1}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
J_3^{AN} &= \sum_{i=1}^{3}\big(\mathrm{i}\bar Q_i\gamma^Nt^AQ_i + \mathrm{i}\bar u_{Ri}\gamma^Nt^Au_{Ri}
 + \mathrm{i}\bar d_{Ri}\gamma^Nt^Ad_{Ri}\big),\qquad
 J_2^{IN} = \sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big(\mathrm{i}\bar Q_i\gamma^N\frac{\tau^I}{2}Q_i +
 \mathrm{i}\bar\ell_i\gamma^N\frac{\tau^I}{2}\ell_i\Big),\\
J_1^N &= \sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_\psi Y_\psi\,\mathrm{i}\bar\psi_i\gamma^N\psi_i,\qquad
 J_{\mathrm{em}}^N = J_2^{3N} + J_1^N,\qquad J_2^{\pm}{}^N = \frac{J_2^{1N} \mp \mathrm{i}J_2^{2N}}{\sqrt2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-20}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-20).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(J_3^{AN}\): The colour current of the quarks (the source of the
  gluons), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(t^A\): The generators of SU(3) in the fundamental representation
  (the Gell-Mann matrices divided by 2),
  \(\mathrm{tr}(t^At^B) = \frac12\delta^{AB}\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(J_2^{IN}\): The weak-isospin current of the doublets \(Q_i\),
  \(\ell_i\) (the source of \(W^I\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(J_1^N\): The hypercharge current of all fermions (the source of
  \(B\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi\): The label of a Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) (quark doublet, up and
  down quark singlets, lepton doublet, charged-lepton singlet,
  right-handed neutrino); a sum over \(\psi\) runs over the six. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(Y\): The hypercharge (the generator of U(1)\(_Y\)); \(Y_\psi\) the
  hypercharge of the multiplet \(\psi\) (\(Q\): \(\frac16\), \(u_R\):
  \(\frac23\), \(d_R\): \(-\frac13\), \(\ell\): \(-\frac12\), \(e_R\):
  \(-1\), \(\nu_R\): 0). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(J_{\mathrm{em}}^N\): The electromagnetic current,
  \(J_{\mathrm{em}} = J_2^3 + J_1\) (in units of the elementary charge).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_2^{\pm}{}^N\): The charged combinations of the isospin current,
  \(J_2^\pm = (J_2^1 \mp \mathrm{i}J_2^2)/\sqrt2\) (the sources of
  \(W^\pm\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The currents are the sources of the gauge fields,
summed over the three generations: \(J_3^{AN}\) (colour) from the
quarks, \(J_2^{IN}\) (weak isospin) from the doublets, \(J_1^N\)
(hypercharge) from every multiplet with its hypercharge \(Y_\psi\); the
electromagnetic current \(J_{\mathrm{em}}\) and the charged currents
\(J_2^\pm\), the sources of the photon and of \(W^\pm\), are
combinations of them. Status: STANDARD.

The gauge kinetic term IN-8 and the portal I12 (IN-17) contain the same
invariants; for the gluons together

\[
-\frac14\Big(\frac{1}{\hat g_3^2} + \frac{4c_{\mathrm{G}}\,\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3}\Big)G^A_{MN}G^{AMN},
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\hat g_3\): The eight-dimensional SU(3) coupling (it multiplies the
  gluon kinetic term as \(1/\hat g_3^2\));
  \(g_3^2 = \hat g_3^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}\): The coefficient of the gluon term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

and likewise for \(W\) and \(B\). The scalar density of dirac16complex
therefore changes the effective couplings:

\[
\frac{1}{\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}}^2} = \frac{1}{\hat g_3^2} +
 \frac{4c_{\mathrm{G}}\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3},\qquad
\frac{1}{\hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}}^2} = \frac{1}{\hat g_2^2} +
 \frac{4c_{\mathrm{W}}\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3},\qquad
\frac{1}{\hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}^2} = \frac{1}{\hat g_1^2} +
 \frac{4c_{\mathrm{B}}\bar\Psi\Psi}{\Lambda_\star^3} \tag{FE-21}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-21).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}\):
  The field-dependent eight-dimensional couplings produced by I12:
  \(1/\hat g_{i,\mathrm{eff}}^2 = 1/\hat g_i^2 + 4c\,\bar\Psi\Psi/\Lambda_\star^3\)
  with \(c = c_{\mathrm{G}}, c_{\mathrm{W}}, c_{\mathrm{B}}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\hat g_3\): The eight-dimensional SU(3) coupling (it multiplies the
  gluon kinetic term as \(1/\hat g_3^2\));
  \(g_3^2 = \hat g_3^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}\): The coefficient of the gluon term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}\): The coefficient of the SU(2) term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{W}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}\): The coefficient of the hypercharge term of the
  portal I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{B}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} Where dirac16complex has a scalar density
\(\bar\Psi\Psi\), every gauge coupling is shifted by the I12 coefficient
of its group. In four dimensions this is the coupling shift of FE-79 (at
most \(1.7\times10^{-16}\) at the dark-matter freeze-out). Status:
DERIVED.

Varying the gauge action with these couplings, the fermion action
(through the covariant derivatives) and the tip action with respect to
the gauge fields gives the Yang-Mills equations:

\[
\nabla_M\Big(\frac{G^{MN}}{\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}}^2}\Big) - \mathrm{i}\Big[G_M,\ \frac{G^{MN}}{\hat
 g_{3,\mathrm{eff}}^2}\Big]
 = J_3^{AN}\,t^A \tag{FE-22}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-22).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}\):
  The field-dependent eight-dimensional couplings produced by I12:
  \(1/\hat g_{i,\mathrm{eff}}^2 = 1/\hat g_i^2 + 4c\,\bar\Psi\Psi/\Lambda_\star^3\)
  with \(c = c_{\mathrm{G}}, c_{\mathrm{W}}, c_{\mathrm{B}}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_3^{AN}\): The colour current of the quarks (the source of the
  gluons), summed over the generations. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(t^A\): The generators of SU(3) in the fundamental representation
  (the Gell-Mann matrices divided by 2),
  \(\mathrm{tr}(t^At^B) = \frac12\delta^{AB}\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The covariant divergence of the gluon field strength
(with the field-dependent coupling of FE-21) plus the commutator term
(the self-interaction of the gluons) equals the colour current of the
quarks. There is no tip term: the gluons do not couple to \(\Sigma\).
Status: DERIVED. On the static block the gluon zero mode is exactly
massless (flat \(x_0\) profile, FE-50 to FE-52); below the confinement
scale QCD enters through the nucleon masses and the condensates (FE-74,
FE-75).

\[
\nabla_M\Big(\frac{W^{MN}}{\hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}}^2}\Big) - \mathrm{i}\Big[W_M,\ \frac{W^{MN}}{\hat
 g_{2,\mathrm{eff}}^2}\Big]
 = J_2^{IN}\,\frac{\tau^I}{2} + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\,\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\Big(W^N -
 B^N\,\frac{\tau^3}{2}\Big)\qquad
 (\Sigma = \mathbf{1}) \tag{FE-23}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-23).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}\):
  The field-dependent eight-dimensional couplings produced by I12:
  \(1/\hat g_{i,\mathrm{eff}}^2 = 1/\hat g_i^2 + 4c\,\bar\Psi\Psi/\Lambda_\star^3\)
  with \(c = c_{\mathrm{G}}, c_{\mathrm{W}}, c_{\mathrm{B}}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_2^{IN}\): The weak-isospin current of the doublets \(Q_i\),
  \(\ell_i\) (the source of \(W^I\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The SU(2) equation (matrix form) has, besides the
isospin current, the tip current of the sigma field in the unitary gauge
\(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) (\(N\) runs over the tip directions): a mass
term for the charged components \(W^1\), \(W^2\) and for the combination
\(W^3 - B\) only. Status: DERIVED. On the static block it is solved by
the \(x_0\) reduction (FE-50) with the Robin tip condition (FE-51).

\[
\nabla_M\Big(\frac{B^{MN}}{\hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}^2}\Big) = J_1^N -
 \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\,\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,
 \big(W^{3N} - B^N\big)\qquad (\Sigma = \mathbf{1}) \tag{FE-24}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-24).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_{3,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{2,\mathrm{eff}},\ \hat g_{1,\mathrm{eff}}\):
  The field-dependent eight-dimensional couplings produced by I12:
  \(1/\hat g_{i,\mathrm{eff}}^2 = 1/\hat g_i^2 + 4c\,\bar\Psi\Psi/\Lambda_\star^3\)
  with \(c = c_{\mathrm{G}}, c_{\mathrm{W}}, c_{\mathrm{B}}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(J_1^N\): The hypercharge current of all fermions (the source of
  \(B\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The hypercharge equation is abelian (no commutator);
its source is the hypercharge current, and the tip term (with the
opposite sign of FE-23) acts on \(W^3 - B\) only. Status: DERIVED.

To linear order in the fields the commutators drop out, and the
combinations of IN-10 decouple. For the photon, for example,

\[
\nabla_MA^{MN} = \frac{\hat g_1^2\,\nabla_MW^{3MN} + \hat g_2^2\,\nabla_MB^{MN}}{\hat g_1\hat g_2\hat g_Z}
 = \frac{\hat g_1\hat g_2}{\hat g_Z}\,\big(J_2^{3N} + J_1^N\big),
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A_M\): The photon field, canonically normalised,
  \(A_M = (\hat g_1^2W^3_M + \hat g_2^2B_M)/(\hat g_1\hat g_2\hat g_Z)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\hat g_Z\): The eight-dimensional coupling of the \(Z\),
  \(\hat g_Z = (\hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2)^{1/2}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_2^{IN}\): The weak-isospin current of the doublets \(Q_i\),
  \(\ell_i\) (the source of \(W^I\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_1^N\): The hypercharge current of all fermions (the source of
  \(B\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

because the tip terms of the third component of FE-23 and of FE-24,
multiplied by \(\hat g_2^2\) and \(\hat g_1^2\), cancel. The same steps
for \(W^\pm\) and \(Z\) give

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\nabla_MW^{\pm}{}^{MN} = \hat g_2\,J_2^{\pm}{}^N + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\,\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\hat
 g_2^2\,W^{\pm}{}^N,\qquad
 \nabla_MA^{MN} = \hat e\,J_{\mathrm{em}}^N,\qquad \hat e = \frac{\hat g_2\hat g_1}{\hat g_Z},\\
&\nabla_MZ^{MN} = \hat g_Z\big(J_2^{3N} - \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\,J_{\mathrm{em}}^N\big)
 + \delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\,\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\hat g_Z^2\,Z^N,\\
&W^{\pm}{}^{MN} = \nabla^MW^{\pm}{}^N - \nabla^NW^{\pm}{}^M,\qquad Z^{MN} = \nabla^MZ^N - \nabla^NZ^M,\qquad
 A^{MN} = \nabla^MA^N - \nabla^NA^M
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-25}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-25).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^\pm_M,\ W^+,\ W^-\): The charged weak bosons, canonically
  normalised:
  \(W^\pm_M = (W^1_M \mp \mathrm{i}W^2_M)/(\sqrt2\,\hat g_2)\); \(W^+\)
  carries the electric charge \(+e\) and \(W^-\) the charge \(-e\) (they
  are each other's antiparticles and have one mass). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_2\): The eight-dimensional SU(2) coupling;
  \(g_2^2 = \hat g_2^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(J_2^{\pm}{}^N\): The charged combinations of the isospin current,
  \(J_2^\pm = (J_2^1 \mp \mathrm{i}J_2^2)/\sqrt2\) (the sources of
  \(W^\pm\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}}\): The density that places a tip term in the
  field equations of eight dimensions,
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{tip}} = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(y + L)/f\) (the Dirac
  delta function at the tip with the proper length of the hidden
  direction). Unit: 1/length. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(A_M\): The photon field, canonically normalised,
  \(A_M = (\hat g_1^2W^3_M + \hat g_2^2B_M)/(\hat g_1\hat g_2\hat g_Z)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat e\): The eight-dimensional electric coupling,
  \(\hat e = \hat g_2\hat g_1/\hat g_Z\) (\(e^2 = \hat e^2/V_\perp\)).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_{\mathrm{em}}^N\): The electromagnetic current,
  \(J_{\mathrm{em}} = J_2^3 + J_1\) (in units of the elementary charge).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\hat g_1\): The eight-dimensional U(1)\(_Y\) coupling;
  \(g_1^2 = \hat g_1^2/V_\perp\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\hat g_Z\): The eight-dimensional coupling of the \(Z\),
  \(\hat g_Z = (\hat g_2^2 + \hat g_1^2)^{1/2}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Z_M\): The \(Z\) boson field, canonically normalised,
  \(Z_M = (W^3_M - B_M)/\hat g_Z\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_2^{IN}\): The weak-isospin current of the doublets \(Q_i\),
  \(\ell_i\) (the source of \(W^I\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The weak mixing angle of the couplings,
  \(\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_2/\hat g_Z\),
  \(\sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_1/\hat g_Z\); fixed by the measured
  \(W\) and \(Z\) masses with \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.776368\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(W^+\) and \(W^-\) are sourced by the charged
currents and have the tip mass term with \(\hat g_2^2\); the \(Z\) is
sourced by the neutral current
\(J_2^3 - \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}J_{\mathrm{em}}\) and has the tip
mass term with \(\hat g_Z^2\); the photon is sourced by the
electromagnetic current with the coupling \(\hat e\) and has no tip
term, so it stays massless. Status: DERIVED (exact at linear order). The
tip terms become the Robin conditions FE-51; the \(W^\pm\) and \(Z\)
masses and the tree-level \(Z\) widths of three generations reproduce
the measured values (residual \(2.7\times10^{-12}\) in the recomputed
widths; the neglected electroweak loop corrections are
\(4\times10^{-3}\) of the total width).

\hypertarget{the-tip-sigma-field}{%
\subsection{The tip sigma field}\label{the-tip-sigma-field}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\nabla^\mu j_\mu - \mathrm{i}\big[W^\mu,\ j_\mu\big] = -\mathrm{i}\,\frac{\tau^I}{2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\Big[
 \bar\ell_i\,\frac{\tau^I}{2}\,\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_\nu)_{ij}\,\nu_{Rj}\\ (
 \mathcal{Y}_e)_{ij}\,e_{Rj}\end{pmatrix}
 + \bar Q_i\,\frac{\tau^I}{2}\,\Sigma\begin{pmatrix}(\mathcal{Y}_u)_{ij}\,u_{Rj}\\ (
 \mathcal{Y}_d)_{ij}\,d_{Rj}\end{pmatrix}
 - \text{h.c.}\Big],\\
&j_\mu = j^I_\mu\,\frac{\tau^I}{2},\qquad j^I_\mu =
 \frac{\mathrm{i}v_\Sigma^2}{4}\,\mathrm{tr}\big(\tau^I(D_\mu\Sigma)\Sigma^\dagger\big),
 \qquad j^I_\mu\big\vert_{\Sigma = \mathbf{1}} = \frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\big(W^I_\mu - \delta^{I3}B_\mu\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-26}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-26).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\nabla\): The covariant derivative of a tensor with the Levi-Civita
  connection of the metric \(g_{MN}\) (Christoffel symbols
  \(\Gamma^N{}_{MP}\)); \(\nabla_M\) acts on the index \(M\),
  \(\nabla^M = g^{MN}\nabla_N\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(j_\mu,\ j^I_\mu\): The current of the sigma field on the tip,
  \(j^I_\mu = \frac{\mathrm{i}v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}(\tau^I(D_\mu\Sigma)\Sigma^\dagger)\),
  and the matrix \(j_\mu = j^I_\mu\tau^I/2\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{6}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\tau^I\): The Pauli matrices acting on weak-isospin doublets
  (\(\tau^I/2\) the generators of SU(2);
  \(\tau^3 = \mathrm{diag}(1, -1)\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\Sigma\): The tip sigma field: a unitary \(2 \times 2\) matrix field
  on the tip (\(\Sigma^\dagger\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\)) that replaces the
  Higgs doublet; its three modes are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\)
  and \(Z\), and \(\Sigma = \mathbf{1}\) is the unitary gauge. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Y}_\nu,\ \mathcal{Y}_e,\ \mathcal{Y}_u,\ \mathcal{Y}_d\):
  The complex \(3 \times 3\) tip Yukawa matrices of the neutrino,
  charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors (generation indices
  \(i, j\)); on the static block they become the tip mixing matrices
  \(\mathcal{K}\) (the normalisation of that relation is not fixed:
  OPEN). Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\nu_R,\ \nu_{Ri}\): The right-handed neutrino (a gauge singlet):
  \(\nu_R\) the multiplet, \(\nu_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\);
  its zero modes give Dirac neutrino masses. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(e_R,\ e_{Ri}\): The right-handed charged-lepton singlet: \(e_R\) the
  multiplet, \(e_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(e_R\),
  \(\mu_R\), \(\tau_R\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(v_\Sigma\): The scale of the tip sigma field (its kinetic term is
  \(-\frac{v_\Sigma^2}{4}\mathrm{tr}((D_\mu\Sigma)^\dagger D^\mu\Sigma)\));
  on the static block it fixes the Robin parameter
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5/2}\). Value: fixed
  through \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.011912\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on a spinor
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (spin connection), on a Standard-Model
  fermion with the gauge fields added; on the sigma field
  \(D_\mu\Sigma\) with the \(W\) and \(B\) fields. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{\alpha e}\),
  \(\delta_{l0}\), \(\delta^{I3}\)); with upper and lower index strings
  the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\) (+1
  or -1 if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation of the lower
  ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The sigma field carries the current \(j_\mu\) (in
the unitary gauge the mass current of \(W^I\) and \(B\)). Its field
equation states that this current is covariantly conserved up to the tip
Yukawa terms, which transfer isospin between the doublets and the
singlets. It is not an independent equation: the covariant divergence of
the left side of FE-23 vanishes identically, so the covariant divergence
of its right side, evaluated with the fermion equations FE-14 to FE-19,
gives exactly this equation (the Noether identity of the gauge
symmetry). In the unitary gauge it therefore holds automatically, and
the three modes of \(\Sigma\) are the longitudinal \(W^+\), \(W^-\) and
\(Z\). Status: STANDARD (for a gauged non-linear sigma model).

\hypertarget{the-boundary-conditions}{%
\subsection{The boundary conditions}\label{the-boundary-conditions}}

The hidden direction is periodic with the period \(\pi/(6H)\); each half
of a period is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\), joined to the other half
at the fold \(y = 0\) by the reflection \(z \to \pi - z\) and closed at
the regular tip \(y = -L\). The variational principle with these
boundaries gives:

\[
\begin{aligned}
&z \to \pi - z:\qquad \partial_yF\big\vert_{y=0} = 0\ \ \text{(even)}\quad\text{or}\quad
 F\big\vert_{y=0} = 0\ \ \text{(odd)};\\
&\text{at } y = -L\text{: the natural (Robin) condition of the tip term}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-27}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-27).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(z\): The angle variable of the hidden direction, \(z = 6Hx_0\); one
  period of \(x_0\) is \(0 < z < \pi\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(y\): The label \(y\) in \(\partial_y\): the derivative with respect
  to the hidden coordinate \(y\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(F(\xi),\ F_n\): The \(x_0\) profile of a boson mode on one half of
  the period (a function of \(y\) or of \(\xi = y/L\)): of a gauge field
  (gauge \(A_y = 0\)) or of the transverse-traceless graviton; \(F_n\)
  is the profile of the \(n\)-th Kaluza-Klein level of the graviton
  tower (\(F_0 = 1\) the flat zero mode). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} At the fold an \(x_0\) profile \(F\) is even (zero
derivative) or odd (zero value) under the reflection; at the tip the
boundary term of the variation gives the natural (Robin) condition of
the tip term (zero derivative where there is no tip term). Status:
DERIVED. For an \(x_0\) profile they are FE-51.

\[
\big(1 - \gamma^{x_0}\big)\Psi\big\vert_{y=0} = 0\ \ \text{(even)}\quad\text{or}\quad
\big(1 + \gamma^{x_0}\big)\Psi\big\vert_{y=0} = 0\ \ \text{(odd)},\qquad
\big(1 - \gamma^{x_0}\big)\Psi\big\vert_{y=-L} = 0 \tag{FE-28}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-28).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the 16-component complex spinor
  field of eight dimensions of the record (the author's field), the
  source of the dark matter (its lowest odd level) and of the massless
  fold bands; \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger C\) is its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} At the fold the even modes of dirac16complex satisfy
\((1 - \gamma^{x_0})\Psi = 0\) and the odd ones
\((1 + \gamma^{x_0})\Psi = 0\) (both parities are modes of the full
period); at the tip \((1 - \gamma^{x_0})\Psi = 0\). The conditions are
Lagrangian for the boundary form of the Dirac operator, so the operator
is self-adjoint. Status: DERIVED. In the block form they are FE-69.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&P_{\mathrm{sel}} = \frac{1 + \Gamma_4\gamma^{x_0}}{2}\,\frac{1 - \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_5}\gamma^{x_6}}{2};\\
&\text{doublets } (Q, \ell):\quad P_{\mathrm{sel}}\big(1 - \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=0}
 = \big(1 - P_{\mathrm{sel}}\big)\big(1 + \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=0} = 0,\qquad
 \big(1 - \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=-L} = 0;\\
&\text{singlets } (u_R, d_R, e_R, \nu_R):\quad P_{\mathrm{sel}}\big(1 + \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=0}
 = \big(1 - P_{\mathrm{sel}}\big)\big(1 - \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=0} = 0,\qquad
 \big(1 + \gamma^{x_0}\big)\psi\big\vert_{y=-L} = 0
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-29}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-29).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(P_{\mathrm{sel}}\): The projector that selects the two blocks of the
  half \(\mathcal{B} = -1\) in which the Standard-Model zero modes live,
  \(P_{\mathrm{sel}} = \frac{1 + \Gamma_4\gamma^{x_0}}{2}\,\frac{1 - \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_5}\gamma^{x_6}}{2}\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Gamma_4\): The four-dimensional chirality matrix,
  \(\Gamma_4 = \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\gamma^{x_4}\);
  a doublet zero mode has \(\Gamma_4 = +1\) (left-handed), a singlet
  zero mode \(\Gamma_4 = -1\) (right-handed). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_5\): The first of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_6\): The second of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(Q_i,\ \bar Q_i\): The quark doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed up and down quark of one generation, three colours) and
  its Dirac adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\ell_i,\ \bar\ell_i\): The lepton doublet of the generation \(i\)
  (left-handed neutrino and charged lepton) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(u_R,\ u_{Ri},\ \bar u_{Ri}\): The up-type quark singlet: \(u_R\) the
  multiplet, \(u_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(u_R\), \(c_R\),
  \(t_R\)), \(\bar u_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(d_R,\ d_{Ri},\ \bar d_{Ri}\): The down-type quark singlet: \(d_R\)
  the multiplet, \(d_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(d_R\),
  \(s_R\), \(b_R\)), \(\bar d_{Ri}\) its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(e_R,\ e_{Ri}\): The right-handed charged-lepton singlet: \(e_R\) the
  multiplet, \(e_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\) (\(e_R\),
  \(\mu_R\), \(\tau_R\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\nu_R,\ \nu_{Ri}\): The right-handed neutrino (a gauge singlet):
  \(\nu_R\) the multiplet, \(\nu_{Ri}\) that of the generation \(i\);
  its zero modes give Dirac neutrino masses. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\). Status: ASSUMED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(P_{\mathrm{sel}}\) selects the two blocks (of the
half \(\mathcal{B} = -1\)) in which the Standard-Model zero modes live.
The conditions give every doublet exactly one Weyl zero mode with
\(\Gamma_4 = +1\) (left-handed) and every singlet exactly one with
\(\Gamma_4 = -1\) (right-handed), all localised at the fold: the
four-dimensional fermion content is exactly that of three Standard-Model
generations, each with its right-handed neutrino. Status: INVENTED (the
conditions), self-adjoint DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\text{one generation:}\quad b_L(-L) = \mathcal{K}\,b_R(-L),\qquad a_R(-L) =
 -\mathcal{K}\,a_L(-L),\qquad \chi = (a,\ \mathrm{i}b);\\
&\text{three generations:}\quad b_L(-L) = \mathcal{K}\,b_R(-L),\qquad a_R(-L) = -\mathcal{K}^\dagger
 a_L(-L),\qquad
 \mathcal{K} \in \{\mathcal{K}_\nu, \mathcal{K}_e, \mathcal{K}_u, \mathcal{K}_d\};\\
&\text{UM-2:}\quad \mathcal{K}_e = \mathcal{K}_u = \mathbf{1},\qquad \mathcal{K}_\nu = U,\qquad
 \mathcal{K}_d = V_{\mathrm{CKM}}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-30}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-30).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(a,\ b\): The upper and lower components of a block spinor,
  \(\chi = (a, \mathrm{i}b)\), real at \(k = 0\): \(a_L, b_L\) of a
  doublet and \(a_R, b_R\) of a singlet solution (\(a_{Ri}\), \(b_{Ri}\)
  of the generation \(i\), \(b_{L\alpha}\), \(a_{L\alpha}\) of the
  doublet in the flavour basis), \(a_o, b_o\) of the orbital \(o\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{1/2}\) (normalised orbital). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The chirality label L: the left-handed (doublet) zero mode, or
  the left-handed projection \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{K}\): The tip mixing matrix of a sector in the boundary
  conditions of the fermion zero modes (a number \(\mathcal{K}\) for one
  generation, a \(3 \times 3\) matrix for three): \(\mathcal{K}_\nu\),
  \(\mathcal{K}_e\), \(\mathcal{K}_u\), \(\mathcal{K}_d\) of the
  neutrino, charged-lepton, up-quark and down-quark sectors; UM-2 takes
  \(\mathcal{K}_e = \mathcal{K}_u = \mathbf{1}\),
  \(\mathcal{K}_\nu = U\), \(\mathcal{K}_d = V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(R\): The chirality label R: the right-handed (singlet) zero mode or
  field, or the projection \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\chi,\ \chi_o,\ \chi_1,\ \chi_2\): The two-component spinor of one
  block of the reduction (a function of \(y\) and \(x_4\)),
  \(\chi = (\chi_1, \chi_2)^T = (a, \mathrm{i}b)^T\); \(\chi_o\) the
  Kohn-Sham orbital \(o\), normalised as
  \(\int_{-L}^{0}\chi_o^\dagger\chi_o\,\mathrm{d}y = 1\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{1/2}\) (normalised orbital). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\): The CKM matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\)
  mixing matrix of the quarks (the same parametrisation as \(U\)); in
  UM-2 \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}} = \mathcal{K}_u^\dagger\mathcal{K}_d\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(s_{12} = 0.22501\), \(s_{13} = 0.003732\),
  \(s_{23} = 0.04183\), \(\delta = 1.147\) (PDG). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The tip Yukawa terms are equivalent to these mixing
conditions between the lower components \(b\) and upper components \(a\)
of the doublet (\(L\)) and singlet (\(R\)) solutions at the tip, with
the tip mixing number \(\mathcal{K}\) for one generation and the
\(3 \times 3\) tip matrices of each sector for three generations. UM-2
takes \(\mathcal{K}_e = \mathcal{K}_u = \mathbf{1}\),
\(\mathcal{K}_\nu = U\) (PMNS) and \(\mathcal{K}_d = V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\),
so the mixing matrices of the leptons and quarks are those measured.
Status: DERIVED (self-adjoint for every complex matrix, sympy, 42 real
variables), the choice of the tip matrices ASSUMED; the normalisation
that relates \(\mathcal{K}\) to the Yukawa matrices \(\mathcal{Y}\) of
the action is not fixed (OPEN). Solved exactly by the mass conditions
FE-59 and FE-60.

\hypertarget{the-background}{%
\section{The background}\label{the-background}}

The background is order 0 of the metric power series (FE-87): the
homogeneous metric of the author with the block of the hidden direction
and the three extra times, sourced by the block-averaged energy-momentum
tensor of every field. Its equations are exact; they are solved in
closed form for the static block and numerically for the primordial
approach to it.

\hypertarget{the-order-0-metric-and-its-curvature}{%
\subsection{The order-0 metric and its
curvature}\label{the-order-0-metric-and-its-curvature}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
\bar g = \mathrm{diag}\Big(&\mathrm{e}^{-2a_4}b_4\cot^2z,\ \mathrm{e}^{2a_4},\ \mathrm{e}^{2a_4},\ 
 \mathrm{e}^{2a_4},\ -1,\\
&-\mathrm{e}^{-2a_4}b_4(\sin^2z)^{1/6},\ -\mathrm{e}^{-2a_4}b_4(\sin^2z)^{1/6},\ -\mathrm{e}^{-2a_4}b_4(
 \sin^2z)^{1/6}\Big),
 \qquad z = 6Hx_0
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-31}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-31).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\bar g_{MN}\): The order-0 (background) metric of the power series:
  the corrected primordial metric in the author's coordinates,
  determined by the two functions \(a_4(x_4)\) and \(b_4(x_4)\);
  \(\lvert\bar g\rvert\) is the absolute value of its determinant. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  hidden and extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(z\): The angle variable of the hidden direction, \(z = 6Hx_0\); one
  period of \(x_0\) is \(0 < z < \pi\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  AUTHOR.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The diagonal entries are, in the order
\(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\): the hidden direction, whose metric coefficient
\(\mathrm{e}^{-2a_4}b_4\cot^2z\) depends on \(x_0\) through the angle
\(z = 6Hx_0\) and on the time through \(a_4(x_4)\) and \(b_4(x_4)\);
ordinary space with the scale factor \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\); the time
\(x_4\); and the three extra times, whose coefficient depends on \(x_0\)
through \((\sin^2z)^{1/6}\). The metric is periodic in \(x_0\) with the
period \(\pi/(6H)\). It is the metric of the gravitational field only:
every other field enters through the right side of FE-1. Status: AUTHOR
(the corrected primordial metric of the record, in the author's
coordinates).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&y = \frac{\ln\lvert\sin z\rvert}{6H},\qquad \mathrm{d}y = \cot z\,\mathrm{d}x_0,\qquad \xi =
 \frac{y}{L}\in[-1, 0],\\
&\bar g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N = \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\big(\mathrm{d}x_1^2 + \mathrm{d}x_2^2 +
 \mathrm{d}x_3^2\big)
 - \mathrm{d}x_4^2 + f^2\Big[\mathrm{d}y^2 - \mathrm{e}^{2Hy}\big(\mathrm{d}x_5^2 + \mathrm{d}x_6^2 +
 \mathrm{d}x_7^2\big)\Big],\\
&f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2},\qquad K = \frac{1}{f^2},\qquad \mathcal{H} = \dot a_4,\qquad
 \mathcal{H}_f = \frac{\dot f}{f} = \frac{\dot b_4}{2b_4} - \dot a_4,\qquad \dot K = -2K\mathcal{H}_f,\\
&\sqrt{\lvert\bar g\rvert} = \mathrm{e}^{3a_4}f^4\lvert\cot z\rvert(\sin^2z)^{1/4}
 = \mathrm{e}^{3a_4}f^4\,\mathrm{e}^{3Hy}\Big\lvert\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{\mathrm{d}x_0}\Big\rvert
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-32}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-32).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(z\): The angle variable of the hidden direction, \(z = 6Hx_0\); one
  period of \(x_0\) is \(0 < z < \pi\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  AUTHOR.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\xi\): The scaled hidden coordinate, \(\xi = y/L\), running from
  \(-1\) (tip) to \(0\) (fold) on each half of a period. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\bar g_{MN}\): The order-0 (background) metric of the power series:
  the corrected primordial metric in the author's coordinates,
  determined by the two functions \(a_4(x_4)\) and \(b_4(x_4)\);
  \(\lvert\bar g\rvert\) is the absolute value of its determinant. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
