 - \frac{32\pi^2}{b_3}\,\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\qquad
\big\langle G^A_{\mu\nu}G^{A\mu\nu}\big\rangle(0) = 4\pi^2\times0.012\ \text{GeV}^4,\qquad
b_3 = 11 - \frac23\,N_{\mathrm{f}} \tag{FE-74}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-74).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(G^A_M,\ G_M,\ G_{MN},\ G^A_{MN}\): The gluon field (SU(3)), in the
  geometric normalisation of the action (couplings in front of the
  kinetic terms): the components \(G^A_M\) (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)), the
  matrix \(G_M = G^A_Mt^A\), the field strength \(G_{MN}\) (matrix) and
  its components \(G^A_{MN}\); \(G^A_{\mu\nu}\) are its components along
  the observable directions (the zero mode, flat in \(x_0\)). Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(A\): A colour (adjoint SU(3)) index running over \(1, \dots, 8\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over
  \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (as \(\mu\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(b_3,\ b_2,\ b_1,\ b_{\mathrm{G}}\): The one-loop coefficients of the
  running couplings,
  \(\mathrm{d}\alpha/\mathrm{d}\ln\mu_{\mathrm{ren}} = -b\,\alpha^2/(2\pi)\):
  \(b_3 = 11 - \frac23N_{\mathrm{f}}\) (SU(3)), \(b_2 = \frac{10}{3}\)
  (SU(2) without a Higgs particle), \(b_1 = -\frac{20}{3}\)
  (U(1)\(_Y\)), \(b_{\mathrm{G}}\) that of the group G (not related to
  the metric function \(b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\): The number of active quark flavours (quarks
  lighter than the scale). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The gluon condensate at the temperature \(T\) is the
vacuum value minus the thermal part, which the trace anomaly of QCD
relates to the interaction measure \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}} = \rho - 3p\)
of the quark-gluon plasma; \(b_3\) is the first coefficient of the QCD
beta function with \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) active flavours. The field
strength is in the geometric normalisation of the action (the canonical
field times \(g_3\)), so the vacuum value is \(4\pi^2\) times the
standard condensate \(0.012\) GeV\(^4\). Status: STANDARD (the relation
does not depend on the signature convention).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}}{T^4} = 4\exp\Big[-\frac{\ln^2(T/0.2\,\text{GeV})}{0.5}\Big]\ \ (T <
 1\,\text{GeV}),\qquad
 \frac{\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}}{T^4} = \frac{b_3}{3}\Big(1 +
 \frac{5N_{\mathrm{f}}}{12}\Big)\alpha_3^2\ \ (T > 1\,\text{GeV}),\\
&p_{\mathrm{QCD}} = \frac{8\pi^2}{45}T^4\Big[1 + \frac{21N_{\mathrm{f}}}{32}
 - \frac{15}{4}\Big(1 + \frac{5N_{\mathrm{f}}}{12}\Big)\frac{\alpha_3}{\pi}\Big],\qquad
 \frac{\mathrm{d}\alpha_3}{\mathrm{d}\ln T} = -\frac{b_3}{2\pi}\,\alpha_3^2,\qquad
 \Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}} = T^5\,\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}T}\Big(\frac{p_{\mathrm{QCD}}}{T^4}\Big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-75}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-75).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b_3,\ b_2,\ b_1,\ b_{\mathrm{G}}\): The one-loop coefficients of the
  running couplings,
  \(\mathrm{d}\alpha/\mathrm{d}\ln\mu_{\mathrm{ren}} = -b\,\alpha^2/(2\pi)\):
  \(b_3 = 11 - \frac23N_{\mathrm{f}}\) (SU(3)), \(b_2 = \frac{10}{3}\)
  (SU(2) without a Higgs particle), \(b_1 = -\frac{20}{3}\)
  (U(1)\(_Y\)), \(b_{\mathrm{G}}\) that of the group G (not related to
  the metric function \(b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\): The number of active quark flavours (quarks
  lighter than the scale). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_3\): The strong fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_3 = g_3^2/(4\pi)\), at the scale given in brackets. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}}) = 0.1180\);
  \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\mathrm{QCD}}\): The pressure of the quark-gluon plasma (leading
  orders). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: ESTIMATE.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The interaction measure of QCD: below 1 GeV a smooth
lattice-like shape peaked near 200 MeV (ESTIMATE), above 1 GeV the
leading-order perturbative result; \(p_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) is the pressure
of the quark-gluon plasma to first order in \(\alpha_3\), the running of
\(\alpha_3\) is one loop (at the scale \(2\pi T\)), and
\(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}} = T^5\,\mathrm{d}(p_{\mathrm{QCD}}/T^4)/\mathrm{d}T\)
is the definition of the interaction measure through the pressure.
Status: ESTIMATE (below 1 GeV), STANDARD (above).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\big\langle F^V_{\mu\nu}F^{V}{}^{\mu\nu}\big\rangle(T) = -2\,\Theta_V\ \ \text{(for $V = W^\pm, Z$)},\qquad
 \big\langle F_{\mathrm{geo}}^2\big\rangle = -4\,\frac{\partial p}{\partial\big(1/g_{\mathrm{G}}^2\big)}
 = 4g_{\mathrm{G}}^2\,p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\\
&p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}} = -\frac{g_{\mathrm{G}}^2T^4}{144}\,d_{\mathrm{adj}}\Big[C_{\mathrm{adj}} +
 \frac52\,T_{\mathrm{F}}N_{\mathrm{f}}\Big]
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-76}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-76).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(F^V_{\mu\nu}\): The field strength of the canonically normalised
  massive vector \(V = W^\pm\) or \(Z\),
  \(F^V_{\mu\nu} = \partial_\mu V_\nu - \partial_\nu V_\mu\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over
  \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (as \(\mu\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(F_{\mathrm{geo}}\): The field strength of the gauge group G in
  geometric normalisation; \(\langle F_{\mathrm{geo}}^2\rangle\) is its
  condensate (\(G^A_{\mu\nu}G^{A\mu\nu}\), \(W^I_{\mu\nu}W^{I\mu\nu}\)
  or \(B_{\mu\nu}B^{\mu\nu}\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{\mathrm{G}}\): The four-dimensional coupling of the gauge group G
  (one of \(g_3\), \(g_2\), \(g_1\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\ p_{\alpha,2},\ p_{\alpha,1}\): The
  order-\(\alpha\) (interaction) part of the pressure of the gauge
  plasma of the group G; \(p_{\alpha,2}\) of SU(2), \(p_{\alpha,1}\) of
  U(1)\(_Y\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(d_{\mathrm{adj}}\): The dimension of the adjoint representation of
  the gauge group (8, 3, 1). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(C_{\mathrm{adj}}\): The quadratic Casimir of the adjoint
  representation (3, 2, 0). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Dynkin index of the fermion representation
  (\(\frac12\) per Dirac fundamental; for U(1)\(_Y\) the sum of the
  squared hypercharges). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\): The number of active quark flavours (quarks
  lighter than the scale). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} For a massive vector boson \(V\) (\(W^\pm\), \(Z\))
the thermal condensate of its canonical field strength is \(-2\) times
the trace \(\Theta_V = \rho_V - 3p_V\) of its ideal gas: on shell the
Proca Lagrangian is a total derivative, so the kinetic and mass terms
have opposite expectation values. For a gauge plasma the
Feynman-Hellmann theorem gives the condensate of the geometric field
strength as the derivative of the pressure with respect to
\(1/g_{\mathrm{G}}^2\), i.e.~\(4g_{\mathrm{G}}^2\) times the
order-\(\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}\) pressure \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}}\) of a
plasma with the adjoint dimension \(d_{\mathrm{adj}}\), the Casimir
\(C_{\mathrm{adj}}\) and \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) fermions of index
\(T_{\mathrm{F}}\). Status: STANDARD (for QCD it reproduces the
trace-anomaly condensate FE-74 to \(10^{-16}\)); no scalar term, since
there is no Higgs particle (ESTIMATE for the electroweak plasma).

\[
\frac{1}{\alpha_2(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}})} = \frac{\sin^2\theta_{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}}{\alpha_{\mathrm{em}}}
 + \frac{b_2}{2\pi}\ln\frac{\mu_{\mathrm{ren}}}{m_{\mathrm{Z}}},\qquad
\frac{1}{\alpha_1(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}})} = \frac{1 -
 \sin^2\theta_{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}}{\alpha_{\mathrm{em}}}
 + \frac{b_1}{2\pi}\ln\frac{\mu_{\mathrm{ren}}}{m_{\mathrm{Z}}},\qquad b_2 = \frac{10}{3},\quad b_1 =
 -\frac{20}{3} \tag{FE-77}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-77).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\alpha_2\): The SU(2) fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_2 = g_2^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}}\): The renormalisation scale of a running
  coupling (taken as \(2\pi T\) for the condensates of a plasma). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}\): The weak mixing angle in the
  \(\overline{\mathrm{MS}}\) scheme at \(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value:
  \(\sin^2\theta_{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}} = 0.23122\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{\mathrm{em}}\): The electromagnetic fine-structure constant
  (\(\overline{\mathrm{MS}}\), at \(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(1/127.95\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b_3,\ b_2,\ b_1,\ b_{\mathrm{G}}\): The one-loop coefficients of the
  running couplings,
  \(\mathrm{d}\alpha/\mathrm{d}\ln\mu_{\mathrm{ren}} = -b\,\alpha^2/(2\pi)\):
  \(b_3 = 11 - \frac23N_{\mathrm{f}}\) (SU(3)), \(b_2 = \frac{10}{3}\)
  (SU(2) without a Higgs particle), \(b_1 = -\frac{20}{3}\)
  (U(1)\(_Y\)), \(b_{\mathrm{G}}\) that of the group G (not related to
  the metric function \(b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\): The mass of the \(Z\). Unit: GeV. Value: 91.1879
  GeV (reproduced). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_1\): The hypercharge fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_1 = g_1^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The one-loop running of the SU(2) and hypercharge
couplings from their \(\overline{\mathrm{MS}}\) values at
\(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\), with the coefficients of three generations of
fermions and the gauge bosons and without the contribution of a Higgs
doublet (\(b_2 = \frac{10}{3}\), \(b_1 = -\frac{20}{3}\)). Status:
STANDARD.

\[
\big\langle W^I_{\mu\nu}W^{I\mu\nu}\big\rangle = g_2^2\Big[2\big(-2\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\big)
 + \cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\big(-2\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\big)\Big] + 4g_2^2\,p_{\alpha,2},\qquad
\big\langle B_{\mu\nu}B^{\mu\nu}\big\rangle =
 g_1^2\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\big(-2\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\big) + 4g_1^2\,p_{\alpha,1} \tag{FE-78}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-78).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(W^I_M,\ W_M,\ W_{MN},\ W^I_{MN}\): The SU(2) gauge field (geometric
  normalisation): the components \(W^I_M\) (\(I = 1, 2, 3\); \(W^3\) the
  third component), the matrix \(W_M = W^I_M\tau^I/2\), the field
  strength \(W_{MN}\) and its components \(W^I_{MN}\); with Greek
  indices the components along the observable directions. Unit: GeV
  (field), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(I\): A weak-isospin (adjoint SU(2)) index running over \(1, 2, 3\);
  a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over
  \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (as \(\mu\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_2\): The four-dimensional SU(2) coupling
  (\(g_2^2 = 4\pi\alpha_2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(g_2^2 = 0.3781\) at \(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The weak mixing angle of the couplings,
  \(\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_2/\hat g_Z\),
  \(\sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_1/\hat g_Z\); fixed by the measured
  \(W\) and \(Z\) masses with \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.776368\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\ p_{\alpha,2},\ p_{\alpha,1}\): The
  order-\(\alpha\) (interaction) part of the pressure of the gauge
  plasma of the group G; \(p_{\alpha,2}\) of SU(2), \(p_{\alpha,1}\) of
  U(1)\(_Y\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_M,\ B_{MN}\): The hypercharge gauge field U(1)\(_Y\) (geometric
  normalisation) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\) (field strength). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(g_1\): The four-dimensional hypercharge coupling
  (\(g_1^2 = 4\pi\alpha_1\), Standard-Model normalisation without the
  GUT factor). Unit: dimensionless. Value: \(g_1^2 = 0.1380\) at
  \(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}\). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The electroweak condensates of a hot plasma: the
free parts of \(W^\pm\) (two charge states) and of the \(Z\) (with the
projections \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) onto \(W^3\) and
\(\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) onto \(B\); the photon is massless and
contributes no free part) and the interaction parts \(4g^2p_\alpha\) of
FE-76. Status: ESTIMATE (below \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) the interaction part
is multiplied by the thermal suppression of a massive vector); at the
freeze-out the \(W\) condensate is \(-5.0\times10^{11}\) GeV\(^4\),
almost all from the interaction part.

\hypertarget{dirac16complex-acting-on-the-standard-model}{%
\subsection{dirac16complex acting on the Standard
Model}\label{dirac16complex-acting-on-the-standard-model}}

In four dimensions the I12 term of FE-21 shifts the inverse squared
couplings by the I12 coefficient times the scalar density of the dark
matter, whose block integral is \(\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{DM}}\) times its
number density (both species and antiparticles):

\[
\frac{1}{g_3^2}\ \to\ \frac{1}{g_3^2} + \frac{4c_{\mathrm{G}}\,\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{DM}}\,n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}}}{\Lambda_\star^3}\qquad\Rightarrow\qquad
\frac{\Delta\alpha_3}{\alpha_3} = -g_3^2\,\Delta\Big(\frac{1}{g_3^2}\Big) = -4G_{\mathrm{g}}\,n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}},
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g_3\): The four-dimensional strong coupling
  (\(g_3^2 = 4\pi\alpha_3\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(g_3^2 = 0.7786\) at \(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}\): The coefficient of the gluon term of the portal
  I12. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(c_{\mathrm{G}}/\Lambda_\star^3 = c_{\mathrm{I12}}/\Lambda_\star^3\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{O}_o,\ \mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The scalar overlap of a
  state of dirac16complex,
  \(\mathcal{O}_o = \partial\varepsilon_o/\partial m_\Psi\)
  (Hellmann-Feynman: the integrated scalar density of the orbital);
  \(\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{DM}}\) that of the dark matter. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{DM}} = 0.7072\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}}\): The total number density of dark-matter
  quanta: both species (the lowest odd levels of \(\Psi_A\) and
  \(\Psi_B\)), particles and antiparticles,
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}} = 2(n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}})\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_\star\): The scale of the portal operators I11, I12, I13.
  Unit: GeV. Value: only the combinations \(c/\Lambda_\star^n\) enter.
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_3\): The strong fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_3 = g_3^2/(4\pi)\), at the scale given in brackets. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}}) = 0.1180\);
  \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{g}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised gluon pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(6.09\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\) (benchmark). Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

and likewise for \(\alpha_2\) and \(\alpha_1\). Within the block the
same term multiplies the kinetic term of the gauge fields by a function
of \(y\), which changes their \(x_0\) problem:

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{\Delta\alpha_3}{\alpha_3} = -4G_{\mathrm{g}}\,n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}},\qquad
 \frac{\Delta\alpha_2}{\alpha_2} = -4G_{\mathrm{W}}\,n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}},\qquad
 \frac{\Delta\alpha_1}{\alpha_1} = -4G_{\mathrm{B}}\,n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}},\\
&-\frac{1}{\Upsilon\,\mathrm{e}^{3HL\xi}}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}\xi}\Big(\Upsilon\,
 \mathrm{e}^{3HL\xi}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}F}{\mathrm{d}\xi}\Big)
 = \tilde m^2F,\qquad \Upsilon(\xi) = 1 + A_{\mathrm{mf}}\,n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}},\qquad
 n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}} = 2a_{\mathrm{DM}}b_{\mathrm{DM}},\\
&\frac{\mathrm{d}F}{\mathrm{d}\xi}(0) = 0,\qquad \Upsilon(-1)\,\frac{\mathrm{d}F}{\mathrm{d}\xi}(-1) =
 \beta_{\mathrm{W}}F(-1)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-79}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-79).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_3\): The strong fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_3 = g_3^2/(4\pi)\), at the scale given in brackets. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}}) = 0.1180\);
  \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{g}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised gluon pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(6.09\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\) (benchmark). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}}\): The total number density of dark-matter
  quanta: both species (the lowest odd levels of \(\Psi_A\) and
  \(\Psi_B\)), particles and antiparticles,
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}} = 2(n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}})\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_2\): The SU(2) fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_2 = g_2^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{W}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised SU(2) gauge pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(2.96\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\alpha_1\): The hypercharge fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_1 = g_1^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{B}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised hypercharge pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(1.08\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\Upsilon(\xi)\): The factor by which the scalar density of the dark
  matter changes the gauge kinetic term along \(x_0\) (I12),
  \(\Upsilon = 1 + A_{\mathrm{mf}}n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(L\): The depth of the regular tip: each half of a period of \(x_0\)
  ends at \(y = -L\); \(HL = 3\). Unit: length. Value: \(L = 3/H\)
  (\(fL = 8.926\times10^{-21}\) m at the stabilised block). Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\xi\): The scaled hidden coordinate, \(\xi = y/L\), running from
  \(-1\) (tip) to \(0\) (fold) on each half of a period. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(F(\xi),\ F_n\): The \(x_0\) profile of a boson mode on one half of
  the period (a function of \(y\) or of \(\xi = y/L\)): of a gauge field
  (gauge \(A_y = 0\)) or of the transverse-traceless graviton; \(F_n\)
  is the profile of the \(n\)-th Kaluza-Klein level of the graviton
  tower (\(F_0 = 1\) the flat zero mode). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\tilde m,\ \tilde m_n\): The dimensionless \(x_0\) eigenvalue of a
  boson mode, \(\tilde m = m\,fL\); \(\tilde m_n\) the \(n\)-th even
  Kaluza-Klein level of the tower with \(\beta = 0\) (\(\tilde m_1\) the
  first). Unit: dimensionless. Value: \(\tilde m_1^2 = 30.1196\),
  \(\tilde m_2^2 = 59.7284\), \(\tilde m_3^2 = 109.0764\) (\(HL = 3\)),
  i.e.~\(\tilde m_1 = 5.48813\), \(\tilde m_2 = 7.72842\),
  \(\tilde m_3 = 10.44397\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(A_{\mathrm{mf}}\): The amplitude of the I12 mean field in the
  \(x_0\) problem of the gauge fields (a bookkeeping parameter: the
  result is linear in it). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}\) (with the orbital
  normalisation). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The scalar density of the
  dark-matter orbital,
  \(n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}} = 2a_{\mathrm{DM}}b_{\mathrm{DM}}\).
  Unit: GeV (normalised orbital). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(a_{\mathrm{DM}},\ b_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The upper and lower components
  of the dark-matter orbital (block spinor \((a, \mathrm{i}b)\),
  normalised on one half). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{1/2}\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The Robin parameter of \(W^+\) and \(W^-\)
  (set by the tip mass term of the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 0.011912 (fitted). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The first line: the dark matter weakens every gauge
coupling in proportion to its density. The second line: the \(x_0\)
problem of FE-50 with the kinetic function
\(\Upsilon = 1 + A_{\mathrm{mf}}n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}}\), where
\(n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DM}} = 2a_{\mathrm{DM}}b_{\mathrm{DM}}\) is
the scalar density of the dark-matter orbital and \(A_{\mathrm{mf}}\)
the amplitude of the mean field; the tip condition carries
\(\Upsilon(-1)\). The flat profile still solves the problem for every
\(\Upsilon\) (the photon and the gluons stay exactly massless), while
the \(W\) eigenvalue changes by
\(\mathrm{d}\ln\tilde m_{\mathrm{W}}^2/\mathrm{d}A_{\mathrm{mf}} = -0.111253\):
the \(W\) mass follows the four-dimensional coupling. Status: DERIVED
(first-order perturbation theory with the exact \(W\) eigenfunction;
shooting agrees). The physical amplitude is
\(\lvert\Delta\alpha/\alpha\rvert \le 1.7\times10^{-16}\) (at the
freeze-out). Residual \(3.3\times10^{-12}\).

\hypertarget{gravity-closes-the-loop}{%
\subsection{Gravity closes the loop}\label{gravity-closes-the-loop}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\Delta K = \frac{c_\rho\,\kappa\Delta\rho + c_3\,\kappa\Delta p_3 + c_p\,\kappa\Delta\bar p}{H^2},\qquad
 \Delta\ln f = -\frac{\Delta K}{2K_*},\\
&\Delta\ln m_s = \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_s}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}\,\Delta\ln f,\qquad
 \kappa\Delta\rho = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\,\mathcal{G}(K_*)\,\rho_4
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-80}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-80).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(K\): The block variable, \(K = 1/f^2\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(c_\rho\): The coefficient of \(\kappa\Delta\rho\) in the adiabatic
  block response \(\Delta K\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.0358521.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value:
  \(\kappa/V_\perp = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 2.328\times10^{-36}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_3\): The coefficient of \(\kappa\Delta p_3\) in the adiabatic
  block response, \(c_3 = -3c_\rho\) (so radiation drops out). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: -0.107556. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(p_3\): The pressure of ordinary 3-space,
  \(p_3 = \mathcal{T}^{x_1}{}_{x_1} = \mathcal{T}^{x_2}{}_{x_2} = \mathcal{T}^{x_3}{}_{x_3}\)
  for an isotropic source. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_p\): The coefficient of \(\kappa\Delta\bar p\) in the adiabatic
  block response. Unit: dimensionless. Value: -0.140028. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\bar p\): The hidden pressure, the pressure averaged over the block,
  \(\bar p = (p_0 + 3p_5)/4\); for a species at fixed temperature
  \(\bar p = \frac14\,\partial p/\partial\ln f\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (or \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) for a four-dimensional
  density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(K_*\): The block variable of the stabilised block,
  \(K_* = 12 + \frac43\sqrt{51}\) (the upper root \(K_+\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: 21.5219. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(s\): The label of a particle species of the plasma or of matter
  (photon, gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), quarks, leptons, nucleons, dark
  matter); a sum over \(s\) runs over the species present. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{N}}\): Newton's constant of four-dimensional gravity,
  \(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa/(\mathcal{G}(K_*)V_\perp)\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(6.709\times10^{-39}\) GeV\(^{-2}\)
  (\(M_{\mathrm{Pl}} = G_{\mathrm{N}}^{-1/2} = 1.22091\times10^{19}\)
  GeV). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{G}(K)\): The coefficient of the four-dimensional
  Einstein-Hilbert term on a static block,
  \(\mathcal{G}(K) = 1 - 24w_{(2)}K + 72w_{(3)}K^2 = (K^2 - 12K + 16)/16\);
  four-dimensional gravity is attractive for \(\mathcal{G} > 0\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathcal{G}(K_*) = 20/3 + \sqrt{51} = 13.8081\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\rho_4\): A four-dimensional energy density (per 3-volume); its
  block average is
  \(\kappa\Delta\rho = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*)\rho_4\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The adiabatic block response FE-47, applied to the
block-averaged sources of every species of an environment: the
four-dimensional densities \(\rho_4\) (and likewise the pressures) enter
as \(\kappa\Delta\rho = 8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\mathcal{G}(K_*)\rho_4\); the
change of the block scale changes every mass \(m_s\) in proportion to
its block sensitivity \(\mathrm{d}\ln m_s/\mathrm{d}\ln f\) (FE-83 to
FE-85). The masses change the sources, so the loop mass, source, block,
mass is iterated to self-consistency. Status: DERIVED. The loop gain is
at most \(1.05\times10^{-33}\) (at the freeze-out), so one step
suffices; the block moves by at most \(\Delta\ln f = 1.1\times10^{-34}\)
(at the freeze-out) and by \(7.3\times10^{-94}\) for the cosmic mean
today. Residual of the stored environments \(2.0\times10^{-11}\).

The hidden pressure is the response of the pressure to the block scale
at fixed temperature (a change of \(\ln f\) changes the proper lengths
of the four block directions together): for an ideal gas of species of
mass \(m_s\), for the QCD plasma whose pressure depends on
\(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}\), and for the order-\(\alpha\) pressure of the
electroweak plasma,

\[
\bar p = \frac14\,\frac{\mathrm{d}p}{\mathrm{d}\ln f},\qquad \frac{\partial p_s}{\partial\ln m_s} = -\big(\rho_s - 3p_s\big),\qquad
\frac{\partial p_{\mathrm{QCD}}}{\partial\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}} = -\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\qquad
\frac{\partial p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}}}{\partial\ln\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}} = p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}}:
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\bar p\): The hidden pressure, the pressure averaged over the block,
  \(\bar p = (p_0 + 3p_5)/4\); for a species at fixed temperature
  \(\bar p = \frac14\,\partial p/\partial\ln f\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (or \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) for a four-dimensional
  density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s\): The label of a particle species of the plasma or of matter
  (photon, gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), quarks, leptons, nucleons, dark
  matter); a sum over \(s\) runs over the species present. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\mathrm{QCD}}\): The pressure of the quark-gluon plasma (leading
  orders). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: ESTIMATE.
\item
  \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(N_{\mathrm{f}})}\): The QCD scale with
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) active flavours (\(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}\),
  \(^{(5)}\), \(^{(4)}\), \(^{(3)}\)), defined by the one-loop running.
  Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\ p_{\alpha,2},\ p_{\alpha,1}\): The
  order-\(\alpha\) (interaction) part of the pressure of the gauge
  plasma of the group G; \(p_{\alpha,2}\) of SU(2), \(p_{\alpha,1}\) of
  U(1)\(_Y\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}\): The fine-structure constant of the gauge
  group G (one of \(\alpha_3\), \(\alpha_2\), \(\alpha_1\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\[
\bar p = -\frac14\sum_s\big(\rho_s - 3p_s\big)\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_s}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 - \frac14\,\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(N_{\mathrm{f}})}}{
 \mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac14\,p_{\alpha,2}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\alpha_2}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} +
 \frac14\,p_{\alpha,1}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\alpha_1}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} \tag{FE-81}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-81).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\bar p\): The hidden pressure, the pressure averaged over the block,
  \(\bar p = (p_0 + 3p_5)/4\); for a species at fixed temperature
  \(\bar p = \frac14\,\partial p/\partial\ln f\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (or \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) for a four-dimensional
  density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(s\): The label of a particle species of the plasma or of matter
  (photon, gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), quarks, leptons, nucleons, dark
  matter); a sum over \(s\) runs over the species present. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(N_{\mathrm{f}})}\): The QCD scale with
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) active flavours (\(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}\),
  \(^{(5)}\), \(^{(4)}\), \(^{(3)}\)), defined by the one-loop running.
  Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\ p_{\alpha,2},\ p_{\alpha,1}\): The
  order-\(\alpha\) (interaction) part of the pressure of the gauge
  plasma of the group G; \(p_{\alpha,2}\) of SU(2), \(p_{\alpha,1}\) of
  U(1)\(_Y\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_2\): The SU(2) fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_2 = g_2^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_1\): The hypercharge fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_1 = g_1^2/(4\pi)\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The first sum runs over the species \(s\) with
block-dependent masses (the plasma species as ideal gases with their
\(x_0\) masses; nucleons and electrons as degenerate Fermi gases in the
cold environments; the dark matter): non-relativistic matter gives
\(-\frac14\rho\,\mathrm{d}\ln m/\mathrm{d}\ln f\), radiation nothing.
The second term is the QCD plasma through the block dependence of its
scale, the last two the electroweak plasma through the block dependence
of its couplings. A change of \(\ln f\) scales the four block directions
together, so FE-81 is the average \(\bar p = \frac14(p_0 + 3p_5)\): the
mass terms depend on the hidden length only (they contribute to \(p_0\),
not to \(p_5\)), the coupling terms on the volume of all four block
directions (equal parts in \(p_0\) and \(p_5\)); the difference
\(p_0 - p_5\) is the block-shape source (OPEN). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}})}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 = -\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}})\Big[\frac{4}{\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}(m_{\mathrm{KK}})} +
 \frac{b_{\mathrm{G}}}{2\pi}\Big],\qquad
\Theta_{\mathrm{G}} = -\frac{b_{\mathrm{G}}\,\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}}{2\pi}\,p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}} \tag{FE-82}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-82).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_{\mathrm{G}}\): The fine-structure constant of the gauge
  group G (one of \(\alpha_3\), \(\alpha_2\), \(\alpha_1\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}}\): The renormalisation scale of a running
  coupling (taken as \(2\pi T\) for the condensates of a plasma). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n,\ m_{\mathrm{KK}}\): The mass of the \(n\)-th
  Kaluza-Klein graviton (and gauge) level,
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n = \tilde m_n/(fL)\);
  \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = m^{\mathrm{KK}}_1\) is the Kaluza-Klein scale.
  Unit: GeV. Value: \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = 121.32\) TeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(b_3,\ b_2,\ b_1,\ b_{\mathrm{G}}\): The one-loop coefficients of the
  running couplings,
  \(\mathrm{d}\alpha/\mathrm{d}\ln\mu_{\mathrm{ren}} = -b\,\alpha^2/(2\pi)\):
  \(b_3 = 11 - \frac23N_{\mathrm{f}}\) (SU(3)), \(b_2 = \frac{10}{3}\)
  (SU(2) without a Higgs particle), \(b_1 = -\frac{20}{3}\)
  (U(1)\(_Y\)), \(b_{\mathrm{G}}\) that of the group G (not related to
  the metric function \(b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}},\ \Theta_{\mathrm{G}},\ \Theta_V\): The trace
  \(\rho - 3p\) of the energy-momentum tensor (interaction measure) of a
  plasma component: \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\) of the quark-gluon plasma
  or hadron gas, \(\Theta_{\mathrm{G}}\) of the gauge plasma of the
  group G, \(\Theta_V\) of the thermal gas of the massive vector \(V\)
  (\(\Theta_{\mathrm{W}}\) of \(W^\pm\), \(\Theta_{\mathrm{Z}}\) of
  \(Z\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\alpha,\mathrm{G}},\ p_{\alpha,2},\ p_{\alpha,1}\): The
  order-\(\alpha\) (interaction) part of the pressure of the gauge
  plasma of the group G; \(p_{\alpha,2}\) of SU(2), \(p_{\alpha,1}\) of
  U(1)\(_Y\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The eight-dimensional couplings are constants, so
the four-dimensional couplings at the Kaluza-Klein scale scale as
\(f^{-4}\) (\(g_{\mathrm{G}}^2 = \hat g^2/V_\perp\)), and
\(m_{\mathrm{KK}} \propto 1/f\); running down to \(\mu_{\mathrm{ren}}\)
with the coefficient \(b_{\mathrm{G}}\) gives the first relation. The
second is the leading-order trace anomaly of a gauge plasma, the
electroweak analogue of \(\Theta_{\mathrm{QCD}}\). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{1}{\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}})} = \frac{1}{\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}})} +
 \frac{7}{2\pi}\ln\frac{m_{\mathrm{KK}}}{m_{\mathrm{Z}}},\qquad
 \Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)} = m_{\mathrm{KK}}\,\mathrm{e}^{-2\pi/(7\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}))},\qquad
 \Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(3)} =
 \big(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}\big)^{21/27}\big(m_{\mathrm{c}}m_{\mathrm{b}}m_{\mathrm{t}}\big)^{2/27},\\
&\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{p}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} = \Big(1 - \frac{\sigma_{\pi N} +
 \sigma_{\mathrm{s}}}{m_{\mathrm{p}}}\Big)
 \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(3)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac{\sigma_{\pi N}}{2m_{\mathrm{p}}}\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{u}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} +
 \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{d}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}\Big)
 + \frac{\sigma_{\mathrm{s}}}{m_{\mathrm{p}}}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{s}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-83}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-83).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\alpha_3\): The strong fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_3 = g_3^2/(4\pi)\), at the scale given in brackets. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}}) = 0.1180\);
  \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n,\ m_{\mathrm{KK}}\): The mass of the \(n\)-th
  Kaluza-Klein graviton (and gauge) level,
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n = \tilde m_n/(fL)\);
  \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = m^{\mathrm{KK}}_1\) is the Kaluza-Klein scale.
  Unit: GeV. Value: \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = 121.32\) TeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\): The mass of the \(Z\). Unit: GeV. Value: 91.1879
  GeV (reproduced). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(N_{\mathrm{f}})}\): The QCD scale with
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) active flavours (\(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}\),
  \(^{(5)}\), \(^{(4)}\), \(^{(3)}\)), defined by the one-loop running.
  Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{u}},\ m_{\mathrm{d}},\ m_{\mathrm{s}},\ m_{\mathrm{c}},\ m_{\mathrm{b}},\ m_{\mathrm{t}}\):
  The masses of the up, down, strange, charm, bottom and top quarks (the
  upright subscript names the quark). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.16 MeV, 4.70
  MeV, 93.5 MeV, 1.273 GeV, 4.183 GeV, 172.56 GeV (the \(x_0\) masses,
  fitted to the PDG values). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{p}},\ m_{\mathrm{n}}\): The masses of the proton and the
  neutron. Unit: GeV. Value: 0.938272 GeV, 0.939565 GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\sigma_{\pi N}\): The pion-nucleon sigma term (the contribution of
  the up and down quark masses to the nucleon mass). Unit: GeV. Value:
  45 MeV (Gasser, Leutwyler and Sainio 1991; the modern determinations
  lie between 42 and 61 MeV). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{s}}\): The strange sigma term of the nucleon. Unit:
  GeV. Value: 40 MeV (within the uncertainty of the FLAG 2024 lattice
  average \(41.0 \pm 8.8\) MeV). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The strong coupling at the Kaluza-Klein scale (one
loop, six flavours, \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\)), the QCD
scale of six flavours and its one-loop matching to three flavours at the
heavy-quark masses, and the block dependence of the proton mass: the
part of \(m_{\mathrm{p}}\) that is not the pion-nucleon and strange
sigma terms (\(\sigma_{\pi N} = 45\) MeV, \(\sigma_{\mathrm{s}} = 40\)
MeV) scales with \(\Lambda^{(3)}_{\mathrm{QCD}}\), the sigma terms with
the light-quark masses. Result:
\(\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{p}}/\mathrm{d}\ln f = -46.1\), the most
block-sensitive mass of UM-2. Status: ESTIMATE (one loop).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} = -1 -
 \frac{8\pi}{7\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}})},\qquad
 \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(5)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} =
 \frac{21}{23}\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac{2}{23}\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{t}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f},\\
&\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(4)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} =
 \frac{21}{25}\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac{2}{25}\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{t}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} + \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln
 m_{\mathrm{b}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}\Big),\\
&\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(3)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} =
 \frac{21}{27}\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac{2}{27}\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{c}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} + \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln
 m_{\mathrm{b}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}
 + \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m_{\mathrm{t}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln f}\Big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-84}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-84).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(N_{\mathrm{f}})}\): The QCD scale with
  \(N_{\mathrm{f}}\) active flavours (\(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}^{(6)}\),
  \(^{(5)}\), \(^{(4)}\), \(^{(3)}\)), defined by the one-loop running.
  Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha_3\): The strong fine-structure constant,
  \(\alpha_3 = g_3^2/(4\pi)\), at the scale given in brackets. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{Z}}) = 0.1180\);
  \(\alpha_3(m_{\mathrm{KK}}) = 0.0606\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n,\ m_{\mathrm{KK}}\): The mass of the \(n\)-th
  Kaluza-Klein graviton (and gauge) level,
  \(m^{\mathrm{KK}}_n = \tilde m_n/(fL)\);
  \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = m^{\mathrm{KK}}_1\) is the Kaluza-Klein scale.
  Unit: GeV. Value: \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} = 121.32\) TeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{u}},\ m_{\mathrm{d}},\ m_{\mathrm{s}},\ m_{\mathrm{c}},\ m_{\mathrm{b}},\ m_{\mathrm{t}}\):
  The masses of the up, down, strange, charm, bottom and top quarks (the
  upright subscript names the quark). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.16 MeV, 4.70
  MeV, 93.5 MeV, 1.273 GeV, 4.183 GeV, 172.56 GeV (the \(x_0\) masses,
  fitted to the PDG values). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The block dependence of the QCD scale with six
flavours, from
\(\Lambda^{(6)}_{\mathrm{QCD}} = m_{\mathrm{KK}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\pi/(7\alpha_3)}\)
with \(\alpha_3 \propto f^{-4}\) and \(m_{\mathrm{KK}} \propto 1/f\),
and the matching through the top, bottom and charm thresholds: \(-60.2\)
(six flavours), \(-55.6\), \(-52.0\) and \(-49.0\) (three flavours).
Status: ESTIMATE (one loop).

\[
\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln m}{\mathrm{d}\ln f} = -1 +
 \frac12\Big[\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\mathcal{Z}(c_L)^2}{\mathrm{d}\ln c_L}
 + \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln\mathcal{Z}(-c_R)^2}{\mathrm{d}\ln(-c_R)}\Big] \tag{FE-85}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-85).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Z}(c)\): The tip amplitude of a normalised zero mode with
  the localisation \(c\),
  \(\mathcal{Z}(c)^2 = c\,\mathrm{e}^{-c}/\sinh c\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(c,\ c_L,\ c_R\): The localisation of a zero mode, \(c = f\mu L\)
  (bulk mass times the proper depth); \(c_L\) of a doublet (localised at
  the fold for \(c_L > 0\)), \(c_R\) of a singlet (localised at the fold
  for \(c_R < 0\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: doublets
  \(c_L = 3.38230\) (quarks) and 5.95288 (leptons); singlets \(c_R\): up
  \(-15.4300\), down \(-14.6258\), strange \(-11.5158\), charm
  \(-8.7683\), bottom \(-7.5005\), top \(-3.3823\), electron
  \(-14.5539\), muon \(-8.9809\), tau \(-5.9529\), neutrinos
  \(-35.0452\), \(-32.8481\), \(-31.0702\) (fixed by the measured
  masses). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L\): The chirality label L: the left-handed (doublet) zero mode, or
  the left-handed projection \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(R\): The chirality label R: the right-handed (singlet) zero mode or
  field, or the projection \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} A fermion mass is \(m = \tilde\varepsilon/(fL)\)
with the first-order root of FE-59, and every localisation
\(c = f\mu L\) is proportional to \(f\): the \(-1\) is the scaling of
the hidden length, the bracket the change of the tip amplitudes. For the
quarks \(-18.82\), \(-18.01\), \(-14.90\), \(-12.15\), \(-10.89\),
\(-6.77\) (up, down, strange, charm, bottom, top): the lighter the
quark, the sharper its localisation and the stronger its block
dependence. Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&n_{\mathrm{p}} + n_{\mathrm{n}} = \eta_{\mathrm{b}}\,n_\gamma,\qquad n_\gamma =
 \frac{2\zeta(3)}{\pi^2}\,T^3,\qquad
 \frac{n_{\mathrm{n}}}{n_{\mathrm{p}}} = \mathrm{e}^{-(m_{\mathrm{n}} - m_{\mathrm{p}})/T},\qquad
 n_{\mathrm{e}} = n_{\mathrm{p}},\\
&n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}} = 2\big(n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\big) =
 2\cdot4\,\Big(\frac{M_{\mathrm{DM}}T}{2\pi}\Big)^{3/2}\mathrm{e}^{-M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T}
 \ \ \Big(\frac{M_{\mathrm{DM}}}{T} < 40\Big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-86}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-86).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{p}}\): The number density of protons (free and bound).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{n}}\): The number density of neutrons (free and bound).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\eta_{\mathrm{b}}\): The ratio of the baryon density to the photon
  density. Unit: dimensionless. Value: \(6.1\times10^{-10}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_\gamma\): The number density of photons. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\zeta\): The Riemann zeta function; only its value \(\zeta(3)\)
  occurs, the sum of \(1/n^3\) over the positive integers \(n\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\zeta(3) = 1.2020569\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{p}},\ m_{\mathrm{n}}\): The masses of the proton and the
  neutron. Unit: GeV. Value: 0.938272 GeV, 0.939565 GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{e}}\): The number density of electrons (net of
  positrons); in the Thomson terms of the photon and baryon equations
  only free (ionised) electrons count. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}}\): The total number density of dark-matter
  quanta: both species (the lowest odd levels of \(\Psi_A\) and
  \(\Psi_B\)), particles and antiparticles,
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,tot}} = 2(n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}})\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM}},\ \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The number densities of
  dark-matter particles and antiparticles of one species. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The densities of the hot environments: the net
baryon density from the baryon-to-photon ratio
\(\eta_{\mathrm{b}} = 6.1\times10^{-10}\) shared between protons and
neutrons in equilibrium, as many electrons as protons, and the dark
matter in equilibrium (two species and their antiparticles, four states
each, hence \(2\cdot4\)) while \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T < 40\), its relic
density afterwards. The cold environments (the cosmic mean today, the
Galaxy at the Sun, the Earth, the centre of the Sun, a white dwarf, a
neutron-star core) use measured densities. Status: ASSUMED.

\hypertarget{the-linear-perturbations}{%
\section{The linear perturbations}\label{the-linear-perturbations}}

\hypertarget{the-metric-as-a-power-series}{%
\subsection{The metric as a power
series}\label{the-metric-as-a-power-series}}

\[
g_{MN} = \bar g_{MN} + \epsilon\,h^{(1)}_{MN} + \epsilon^2\,h^{(2)}_{MN} + \dots,\qquad
h^{(1)}_{MN}(X^\mu, x_0) = \sum_{n\ge0}h^{(1,n)}_{MN}(X^\mu)\,F_n(x_0),\qquad \epsilon \sim 10^{-5}
 \tag{FE-87}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-87).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar g_{MN}\): The order-0 (background) metric of the power series:
  the corrected primordial metric in the author's coordinates,
  determined by the two functions \(a_4(x_4)\) and \(b_4(x_4)\);
  \(\lvert\bar g\rvert\) is the absolute value of its determinant. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\epsilon\): The bookkeeping parameter of the power series of the
  metric: the term of order \(q\) is multiplied by \(\epsilon^q\) (the
  size of the first-order potentials). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\epsilon \sim 10^{-5}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(h^{(1)}_{MN},\ h^{(2)}_{MN}\): The first- and second-order terms of
  the power series of the metric. Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_0\): The hidden space-like coordinate (listed first); the metric
  depends on it through \(z = 6Hx_0\), periodically with the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(n\): The level number of the Kaluza-Klein tower
  (\(n = 1, 2, 3, \dots\); \(n = 0\) the flat zero mode). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(h^{(1,n)}_{MN}\): The four-dimensional coefficient of the \(n\)-th
  \(x_0\) profile \(F_n\) in the first-order term (\(n = 0\): the flat
  zero mode, the perturbations of four-dimensional gravity; \(n \ge 1\):
  the Kaluza-Klein gravitons). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(F(\xi),\ F_n\): The \(x_0\) profile of a boson mode on one half of
  the period (a function of \(y\) or of \(\xi = y/L\)): of a gauge field
  (gauge \(A_y = 0\)) or of the transverse-traceless graviton; \(F_n\)
  is the profile of the \(n\)-th Kaluza-Klein level of the graviton
  tower (\(F_0 = 1\) the flat zero mode). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The metric of UM-2 is the order-0 metric (FE-31)
plus terms of decreasing magnitude, counted by the bookkeeping parameter
\(\epsilon \sim 10^{-5}\) (the size of the cosmological potentials).
Each term is expanded in the \(x_0\) profiles \(F_n\) of FE-50:
\(n = 0\) is the flat profile (four-dimensional gravity, the
cosmological perturbations below), \(n \ge 1\) are the Kaluza-Klein
gravitons of FE-53. Status: DERIVED. The terms decrease strictly: order
0 is 1, order 1 is at most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\) on cosmological scales
(the rms potential, FE-98), the Kaluza-Klein terms have ranges below
\(1.6\times10^{-21}\) m, the radion term is at most
\(1.1\times10^{-34}\), and order 2 is estimated as the square of order
1, \(5.8\times10^{-10}\) (ESTIMATE). Residual (the first omitted order
relative to the kept one) \(2.4\times10^{-5}\); at a neutron star order
2 is 0.03 of order 0, and there full nonlinear general relativity
applies.

\[
\begin{aligned}
g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N ={}& \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\Big[\big(1 -
 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\big)\big(\mathrm{d}x_1^2 + \mathrm{d}x_2^2
 + \mathrm{d}x_3^2\big) + \mathrm{d}\vec x\cdot h^{\mathrm{TT}}\cdot\mathrm{d}\vec x\Big] - \big(1 +
 2\Phi\big)\,\mathrm{d}x_4^2\\
 &+ f^2\big(1 + 2\sigma_{\mathrm{r}}\big)\Big[\mathrm{d}y^2 - \mathrm{e}^{2Hy}\big(\mathrm{d}x_5^2 +
 \mathrm{d}x_6^2 + \mathrm{d}x_7^2\big)\Big]
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-88}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-88).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval;
  \(g^{MN}\) is its inverse, \(g^M{}_N = \delta^M_N\), and
  \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its determinant, so that
  \(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper volume element.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_1\): The first coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_2\): The second coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_3\): The third coordinate of ordinary 3-space (also the symmetry
  axis of the fast-flavour analysis). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\vec x\): The position in ordinary 3-space,
  \(\vec x = (x_1, x_2, x_3)\);
  \(\mathrm{d}\vec x^{\,2} = \mathrm{d}x_1^2 + \mathrm{d}x_2^2 + \mathrm{d}x_3^2\).
  Unit: length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(h^{\mathrm{TT}}\): The transverse-traceless (tensor) part of the
  first-order metric of 3-space (gravitational waves). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: an input (tensor-to-scalar ratio below 0.036).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(f\): The block scale: the common scale factor of the hidden
  direction and the extra times, \(f = \mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4^{1/2}\) (the
  block is \(f^2\) times a four-dimensional space of constant curvature
  \(-H^2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{r}}\): The radion perturbation: the first-order
  change of the block scale, \(f^2 \to f^2(1 + 2\sigma_{\mathrm{r}})\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most \(1.1\times10^{-34}\) (static
  response). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(y\): The hidden coordinate of the \(y\) chart,
  \(y = \ln\lvert\sin z\rvert/(6H)\), with \(y = 0\) at the fold
  (\(z = \pi/2\)) and \(y = -L\) at the regular tip; each half of a
  period of \(x_0\) is the interval \(-L \le y \le 0\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings, the cosmological
  constant \(\Lambda = -10H^2\) and the mass parameter \(m_\Psi = H\).
  Unit: GeV (1/length). Value: 14.296 TeV (from the fit of
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\) and \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\)). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_5\): The first of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_6\): The second of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_7\): The third of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The order-1, \(n = 0\) term in eight dimensions, in
the Newtonian gauge: \(\Phi\) is the Newtonian potential (in the time
component), \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\) the curvature potential of ordinary
space, \(h^{\mathrm{TT}}\) the transverse-traceless tensor of
gravitational waves, and \(\sigma_{\mathrm{r}}\) the radion, a common
change of the block scale. The radion is massive (48.42 TeV, FE-44): its
static response to matter is at most \(1.1\times10^{-34}\), so it is
absent on every observable scale; the tensor amplitude is an input,
bounded by the tensor-to-scalar ratio below 0.036. Status: DERIVED.

\[
g^{(4)}_{\mu\nu}\,\mathrm{d}X^\mu\mathrm{d}X^\nu = \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\Big[-\big(1 +
 2\Phi\big)\,\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}^2
 + \big(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\big)\,\mathrm{d}\vec x^{\,2}\Big],\qquad \mathrm{d}x_4 =
 \mathrm{e}^{a_4}\,\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}} \tag{FE-89}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-89).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g^{(4)}_{\mu\nu}\): The four-dimensional metric of the observable
  coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (the metric of the effective
  four-dimensional general relativity on the stabilised block). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over
  \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (as \(\mu\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first; \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index
  \(M\),
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element and \(X^\mu\) (four-dimensional index
  \(\mu\)) the observable coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\). Unit: length
  (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\): The conformal time,
  \(\mathrm{d}x_4 = \mathrm{e}^{a_4}\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\);
  primes denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\). Unit:
  length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec x\): The position in ordinary 3-space,
  \(\vec x = (x_1, x_2, x_3)\);
  \(\mathrm{d}\vec x^{\,2} = \mathrm{d}x_1^2 + \mathrm{d}x_2^2 + \mathrm{d}x_3^2\).
  Unit: length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The four-dimensional part of FE-88 in the conformal
time \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) of the expanding Universe
(\(\mathrm{d}x_4 = \mathrm{e}^{a_4}\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\)), the
form used by the Einstein-Boltzmann codes. A prime denotes the
derivative with respect to \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\); \(a_4'\) is the
conformal expansion rate. Status: STANDARD (Newtonian gauge, Ma and
Bertschinger).

\hypertarget{the-einstein-boltzmann-equations}{%
\subsection{The Einstein-Boltzmann
equations}\label{the-einstein-boltzmann-equations}}

The order-1, \(n = 0\) part of FE-1, reduced on the stabilised block
(FE-49), is general relativity with the four-dimensional energy-momentum
tensor of all species; for one Fourier mode \(k\):

\[
\begin{aligned}
&k^2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}} + 3a_4'\big(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}' + a_4'\Phi\big) = -4\pi
 G_{\mathrm{N}}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\,\Delta\rho,\qquad
 k^2\big(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}' + a_4'\Phi\big) = 4\pi
 G_{\mathrm{N}}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\big(\rho_{\mathrm{tot}} + p_{\mathrm{tot}}\big)\theta_{\mathrm{tot}},\\
&\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}'' + a_4'\big(\Phi' + 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}'\big) + \big(2a_4'' + a_4'^2\big)\Phi +
 \frac{k^2}{3}\big(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}} - \Phi\big)
 = 4\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\,\Delta p,\\
&k^2\big(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}} - \Phi\big) = 12\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\big(\rho_{\mathrm{tot}}
 + p_{\mathrm{tot}}\big)\sigma_{\mathrm{an}}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-90}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-90).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \({}'\): The prime on a function of the conformal time
  \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) is its derivative
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) (it is used only in the
  cosmological perturbation equations; two primes are the second
  derivative). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{N}}\): Newton's constant of four-dimensional gravity,
  \(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa/(\mathcal{G}(K_*)V_\perp)\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(6.709\times10^{-39}\) GeV\(^{-2}\)
  (\(M_{\mathrm{Pl}} = G_{\mathrm{N}}^{-1/2} = 1.22091\times10^{19}\)
  GeV). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{tot}},\ p_{\mathrm{tot}}\): The total background
  energy density and pressure of four-dimensional cosmology (all
  species). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{tot}}\): The velocity divergence of the total
  matter, weighted with \(\rho + p\) of each species. Unit: 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
