  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{an}}\): The anisotropic stress (shear) of the total
  matter (the quantity \(\sigma\) of Ma and Bertschinger), weighted with
  \(\rho + p\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The four linearised Einstein equations: the energy
constraint (the change \(\Delta\rho\) of the total energy density
sources \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\), the Poisson equation inside the horizon),
the momentum constraint (the velocity divergence
\(\theta_{\mathrm{tot}}\) of the total fluid), the pressure equation
(the change \(\Delta p\) of the total pressure) and the
anisotropic-stress equation (\(\sigma_{\mathrm{an}}\) makes \(\Phi\) and
\(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\) differ; it comes from the free-streaming neutrinos
and photons). All species of UM-2 contribute to the sources: the dark
matter, the baryons, the photons, the three neutrino mass states and the
fold bands produced by freeze-in. Status: STANDARD (solved with CAMB
2.0.5 at the Planck 2018 parameters; the TT and EE spectra agree with
Planck \(\Lambda\)CDM to \(2.5\times10^{-4}\) and \(2.7\times10^{-4}\)).

\[
\delta_{\mathrm{DM}}' = -\theta_{\mathrm{DM}} + 3\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}',\qquad \theta_{\mathrm{DM}}' =
 -a_4'\,\theta_{\mathrm{DM}} + k^2\Phi \tag{FE-91}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-91).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{DM}},\ \theta_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The density contrast
  and the velocity divergence of the dark matter. Unit: dimensionless;
  1/length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}'\): The prime on a function of the conformal time
  \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) is its derivative
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) (it is used only in the
  cosmological perturbation equations; two primes are the second
  derivative). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The dark matter (the lowest odd quanta of \(\Psi_A\)
and \(\Psi_B\)) is cold: its density contrast \(\delta_{\mathrm{DM}}\)
changes with its velocity divergence \(\theta_{\mathrm{DM}}\) and the
change of the curvature potential, and its velocity responds only to the
expansion and to the gradient of \(\Phi\); it has no pressure
(Kohn-Sham: \(p_3 = 0\) for quanta at rest) and no interaction at these
energies (its portal couplings are of order \(10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) and
below). Status: STANDARD.

\[
\delta_{\mathrm{b}}' = -\theta_{\mathrm{b}} + 3\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}',\qquad
\theta_{\mathrm{b}}' = -a_4'\,\theta_{\mathrm{b}} + c_{\mathrm{s}}^2k^2\delta_{\mathrm{b}}
 + R_{\mathrm{b}}\,\mathrm{e}^{a_4}n_{\mathrm{e}}\sigma_{\mathrm{T}}\big(\theta_\gamma -
 \theta_{\mathrm{b}}\big) + k^2\Phi,\qquad
R_{\mathrm{b}} = \frac{4\rho_\gamma}{3\rho_{\mathrm{b}}} \tag{FE-92}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-92).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{b}},\ \theta_{\mathrm{b}}\): The density contrast
  and the velocity divergence of the baryons (nucleons and electrons).
  Unit: dimensionless; 1/length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}'\): The prime on a function of the conformal time
  \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) is its derivative
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) (it is used only in the
  cosmological perturbation equations; two primes are the second
  derivative). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c_{\mathrm{s}}\): The sound speed of the baryon fluid. Unit:
  dimensionless (c = 1). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(R_{\mathrm{b}}\): The photon-to-baryon momentum ratio of the Thomson
  drag, \(R_{\mathrm{b}} = 4\rho_\gamma/(3\rho_{\mathrm{b}})\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{e}}\): The number density of electrons (net of
  positrons); in the Thomson terms of the photon and baryon equations
  only free (ionised) electrons count. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{T}}\): The Thomson cross-section. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(6.6524587\times10^{-29}\) m\(^2\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_\gamma,\ \theta_\gamma\): The density contrast and the
  velocity divergence of the photons. Unit: dimensionless; 1/length.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_\gamma\): The energy density of the photons,
  \(\rho_\gamma = \pi^2T_\gamma^4/15\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{b}}\): The energy density of the baryons. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The baryons (the up and down quarks confined in
protons and neutrons, and the electrons): the same continuity equation,
with the sound speed \(c_{\mathrm{s}}\) of the baryon fluid and the
Thomson drag of the photons (\(n_{\mathrm{e}}\) the free-electron
density, \(\sigma_{\mathrm{T}}\) the Thomson cross-section,
\(R_{\mathrm{b}}\) the ratio of the photon and baryon inertia). Status:
STANDARD.

\[
\delta_\gamma' = -\frac43\,\theta_\gamma + 4\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}',\qquad
\theta_\gamma' = k^2\Big(\frac14\,\delta_\gamma - \sigma_{\mathrm{an},\gamma}\Big) + k^2\Phi
 + \mathrm{e}^{a_4}n_{\mathrm{e}}\sigma_{\mathrm{T}}\big(\theta_{\mathrm{b}} - \theta_\gamma\big) \tag{FE-93}
\]

\textbf{The terms.} The photon density contrast and velocity divergence:
radiation (\(\frac43\)), the pressure gradient \(\frac14\delta_\gamma\),
the anisotropic stress \(\sigma_{\mathrm{an},\gamma}\), gravity, and the
Thomson scattering on the free electrons. Status: STANDARD.

\textbf{The terms.} The Legendre multipoles \(F^\gamma_l\) of the photon
temperature perturbation: free streaming couples each multipole to its
neighbours, Thomson scattering damps them, and the quadrupole is fed by

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{N}^{(i)\prime}_0 = -\frac{k_\nu k}{E^{\mathrm{c}}_i}\,\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_1
 - \Psi_{\mathrm{N}}'\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln f_{\mathrm{FD}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln k_\nu},\qquad
 \mathcal{N}^{(i)\prime}_1 = \frac{k_\nu k}{3E^{\mathrm{c}}_i}\big(\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_0 -
 2\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_2\big)
 - \frac{E^{\mathrm{c}}_ik}{3k_\nu}\,\Phi\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln f_{\mathrm{FD}}}{\mathrm{d}\ln k_\nu},\\
&\mathcal{N}^{(i)\prime}_l = \frac{k_\nu k}{(2l + 1)E^{\mathrm{c}}_i}\big[l\,\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_{l-1} -
 (l + 1)\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_{l+1}\big]
 \quad (l \ge 2),\qquad E^{\mathrm{c}}_i = \sqrt{k_\nu^2 + \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}m_i^2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-96}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-96).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_l\): The multipole \(l\) of the perturbation of
  the distribution of the neutrino mass state \(i\) (a function of
  \(k\), \(k_\nu\) and \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\); the quantity \(\Psi_l\) of
  Ma and Bertschinger). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}'\): The prime on a function of the conformal time
  \(\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) is its derivative
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{c}}\) (it is used only in the
  cosmological perturbation equations; two primes are the second
  derivative). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k_\nu\): The comoving momentum of a neutrino (the physical momentum
  times \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\)). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E^{\mathrm{c}}_i\): The comoving energy of a neutrino of the mass
  state \(i\),
  \(E^{\mathrm{c}}_i = (k_\nu^2 + \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}m_i^2)^{1/2}\). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\): The curvature potential of the Newtonian gauge
  (the perturbation of the 3-space metric, \(1 - 2\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\));
  not related to the field \(\Psi\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at most
  \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f_{\mathrm{FD}}\): The Fermi-Dirac distribution of the relic
  neutrinos, \(f_{\mathrm{FD}} = 1/(\mathrm{e}^{k/T_\nu} + 1)\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The lapse potential of the Newtonian gauge (the perturbation
  of the time component, \(1 + 2\Phi\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value: at
  most \(2.4\times10^{-5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(l\): The multipole (angular moment) number \(l = 0, 1, 2, \dots\) of
  a Boltzmann hierarchy or of an angular moment \(\int u^l\cdots\); as a
  coefficient it is the same number. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} Each neutrino mass state \(\nu_i\) (after decoupling
the flavour coherence is lost and each mass state propagates on its own)
has the multipoles \(\mathcal{N}^{(i)}_l\) of its perturbed distribution
at the comoving momentum \(k_\nu\); \(E^{\mathrm{c}}_i\) is its comoving
energy with the mass \(m_i\) (FE-100), \(f_{\mathrm{FD}}\) the
Fermi-Dirac distribution. The masses make the neutrinos slow at late
times (they then cluster like matter). The fold bands of dirac16complex
obey the massless limit. Status: STANDARD (each mass state with the
degeneracy \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}/3 = 1.0144\); the masses in CAMB agree
with the \(x_0\) masses to \(7.8\times10^{-12}\)).

\[
a_4'^2 = \frac{8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}}{3}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}\rho_{\mathrm{tot}} +
 \frac{\Lambda_4}{3}\,\mathrm{e}^{2a_4},\qquad
T_\nu = \Big(\frac{4}{11}\Big)^{1/3}T_\gamma,\qquad
P_{\mathcal{R}}(k) = A_{\mathrm{s}}\Big(\frac{k}{k_0}\Big)^{n_{\mathrm{s}} - 1} \tag{FE-97}
\]

\item

  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a

  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A_{\mathrm{s}}\): The amplitude of the primordial spectrum at

  (\(\ln(10^{10}A_{\mathrm{s}}) = 3.044\), Planck 2018). Status:

\item
  \(k_0\): The pivot wavenumber of the primordial spectrum. Unit:

\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{s}}\): The spectral index of the primordial spectrum.

\textbf{The terms.} The four-dimensional Friedmann equation in conformal
time (from FE-38 at first order with FE-80; \(\Lambda_4\) the tuned dark
energy, FE-48), the temperature of the neutrinos after the
electron-positron annihilation, and the primordial curvature spectrum
with the amplitude \(A_{\mathrm{s}} = 2.099\times10^{-9}\) and the index
\(n_{\mathrm{s}} = 0.9649\) at the pivot \(k_0\). Status: STANDARD
(inputs: the Planck 2018 primordial parameters;
\(N_{\mathrm{eff}} = 3.0432\) in the neutrinos and 0.0010 in the fold
bands; results: \(\mathcal{H}_0 = 67.38\) km/s/Mpc, the sound horizon at
the drag epoch 147.10 Mpc, BAO \(\chi^2 = 31.5\) for 13 DESI DR2
points).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\Phi_{\mathrm{rms}}(k) = \Big[\frac{k^3P_{\Phi_{\mathrm{W}}}(k)}{2\pi^2}\Big]^{1/2},\qquad
 \Phi_{\mathrm{W}} = \frac{\Phi + \Psi_{\mathrm{N}}}{2},\qquad
 \Phi_{\mathrm{W}} = -\frac32\,\Omega_{\mathrm{m}}\mathcal{H}_0^2\,\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{m}}}{
 \mathrm{e}^{a_4}k^2}\ \ \text{(inside the horizon)},\\
&\Phi_{\mathrm{W}} = -\frac35\,\mathcal{R}\,\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}D_+\ \ \text{(largest scales)},\qquad
 D_+ = \frac52\,\Omega_{\mathrm{m}}\,\frac{\mathcal{H}}{\mathcal{H}_0}\int_{-\infty}^{a_4}\Big(\frac{
 \mathcal{H}_0}{\mathcal{H}}\Big)^3
 \mathrm{e}^{-2\tilde a_4}\,\mathrm{d}\tilde a_4,\\
&\frac{\mathcal{H}}{\mathcal{H}_0} = \big(\Omega_{\mathrm{m}}\mathrm{e}^{-3a_4} + 1 -
 \Omega_{\mathrm{m}}\big)^{1/2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-98}
\]

\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{R}\): The primordial comoving curvature perturbation
  (conserved outside the horizon), counted positive where the density is
  above its mean, so that the Newtonian potentials \(\Phi\),
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{N}}\) and \(\Phi_{\mathrm{W}}\) are negative there (on
  the largest scales in the matter era
  \(\Phi_{\mathrm{W}} = -\frac35\mathcal{R}\,\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}D_+\)).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_+\): The linear growth factor of the matter perturbations,
  normalised as \(D_+ = \mathrm{e}^{a_4}\) in the matter era. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(D_+\mathrm{e}^{-a_4} = 0.7878\) today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde a_4\): An earlier value of \(a_4\) (the integration variable
  of the growth integral). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The size of the first-order potentials:
\(\Phi_{\mathrm{rms}}\) is the rms Weyl potential \(\Phi_{\mathrm{W}}\)
per logarithmic wavenumber, from its power spectrum
\(P_{\Phi_{\mathrm{W}}}\). Inside the horizon it follows from the matter
density contrast \(\delta_{\mathrm{m}}\) (the Poisson equation, from
FE-90), on the largest scales from the primordial curvature perturbation
\(\mathcal{R}\) with the growth factor \(D_+\), which the dark energy
suppresses at late times. The signs are those of FE-89 and FE-90: the
potentials of an overdensity (\(\delta_{\mathrm{m}} > 0\),
\(\mathcal{R} > 0\)) are negative; the computation uses only the rms
value, which does not depend on the sign. Status: STANDARD.
\(\Phi_{\mathrm{rms}} = 2.42\times10^{-5}\) at \(k = 10^{-4}\)
Mpc\(^{-1}\) down to \(5.03\times10^{-8}\) at 1 Mpc\(^{-1}\); the
Poisson form agrees with the CAMB solution to \(1.5\times10^{-6}\) for
\(k \ge 0.01\) Mpc\(^{-1}\) (the residual of the stored first-order
solution in this equation) and the large-scale form to
\(4.4\times10^{-5}\) (\(D_+\mathrm{e}^{-a_4} = 0.7878\) today, with
\(\Omega_{\mathrm{m}} = 0.3150\)).

\[
\sigma_8^2 = \int_0^\infty\frac{\mathrm{d}k}{k}\,\frac{k^3P_{\mathrm{m}}(k)}{2\pi^2}\,W_{
 \mathrm{th}}(kR_8)^2,\qquad
W_{\mathrm{th}}(kR_8) = \frac{3\big[\sin(kR_8) - kR_8\cos(kR_8)\big]}{(kR_8)^3},\qquad R_8 =
 8\,h_{100}^{-1}\,\text{Mpc} \tag{FE-99}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-99).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\sigma_8\): The root-mean-square linear matter fluctuation in
  spheres of radius \(R_8\) today. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.8118.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P_{\mathrm{m}}(k)\): The linear matter power spectrum today. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{length}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(W_{\mathrm{th}}\): The Fourier transform of a spherical top-hat
  window. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(R_8\): The radius of the spheres of \(\sigma_8\),
  \(R_8 = 8\,h_{100}^{-1}\) Mpc. Unit: length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(h_{100}\): The reduced Hubble constant, \(\mathcal{H}_0/(100\) km
  s\(^{-1}\) Mpc\(^{-1})\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.6738. Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The rms density fluctuation in spheres of radius
\(8\,h_{100}^{-1}\) Mpc, from the linear matter power spectrum
\(P_{\mathrm{m}}\) and the top-hat window \(W_{\mathrm{th}}\). Status:
STANDARD. \(\sigma_8 = 0.8118\) for UM-2 (0.8119 without the fold bands)
against 0.8109 for \(\Lambda\)CDM.

\hypertarget{the-three-neutrino-flavours}{%
\section{The three neutrino
flavours}\label{the-three-neutrino-flavours}}

The three flavours of left-handed neutrinos are the upper members of the
lepton doublets \(\ell_i\). Their masses come from the tip mixing with
the right-handed neutrinos (FE-59, FE-60), their mixing from the tip
matrix \(\mathcal{K}_\nu = U\), and their interactions from the exchange
of \(W^+\), \(W^-\) and \(Z\). This section collects the equations of
their masses, of their interactions at low energy and of their flavour
evolution in vacuum, in matter, in the early Universe and in dense
neutrino gases, and the cosmic neutrino background.

\hypertarget{the-masses-and-the-interactions-at-low-energy}{%
\subsection{The masses and the interactions at low
energy}\label{the-masses-and-the-interactions-at-low-energy}}

\[
m_1 = 1.000\ \text{meV},\qquad m_2 = \sqrt{m_1^2 + \Delta m_{21}^2} = 8.712\ \text{meV},\qquad
m_3 = \sqrt{m_1^2 + \Delta m_{31}^2} = 50.140\ \text{meV} \tag{FE-100}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-100).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(m_1,\ m_2,\ m_3\): The masses of the neutrino mass states \(\nu_1\),
  \(\nu_2\), \(\nu_3\) (normal ordering). Unit: GeV (quoted in meV).
  Value: 1.000, 8.712, 50.140 meV (\(m_1\) ASSUMED). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2,\ \Delta m_{31}^2,\ \Delta m_{32}^2\): The
  differences of the squared neutrino masses,
  \(\Delta m_{ij}^2 = m_i^2 - m_j^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\)
  (quoted in \(\mathrm{eV}^{2}\)). Value:
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2 = 7.49\times10^{-5}\) eV\(^2\),
  \(\Delta m_{31}^2 = 2.513\times10^{-3}\) eV\(^2\) (NuFIT 6.0). Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The masses of the three mass states \(\nu_1\),
\(\nu_2\), \(\nu_3\): the lightest mass \(m_1\) is chosen and the
ordering is normal (ASSUMED), the two splittings
\(\Delta m_{21}^2 = 7.49\times10^{-5}\) eV\(^2\) and
\(\Delta m_{31}^2 = 2.513\times10^{-3}\) eV\(^2\) are the measured ones
(NuFIT 6.0). In UM-2 they are Dirac masses and \(x_0\) eigenvalues of
FE-59: the doublet localisation \(c_L = 5.95288\) of the leptons and the
right-handed localisations \(c_R = -35.0452\), \(-32.8481\),
\(-31.0702\), with tip mixing of order one; the smallness of the masses
comes from the sharp localisation of the right-handed neutrinos at the
fold. Status: ASSUMED (\(m_1\), ordering), STANDARD (splittings),
DERIVED (as \(x_0\) eigenvalues). The same masses enter the flavour
evolution, the cosmic neutrino background and the CMB computation (they
agree across the three to \(5.7\times10^{-12}\)).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{CC}} = -2\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\big(\bar\nu_\alpha\gamma^\mu P_L\,\ell^-_\alpha\big)
 \big(\bar\ell^-_\beta\gamma_\mu P_L\,\nu_\beta\big),\qquad
 \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{NC}} = -2\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\big(\bar\nu_\alpha\gamma^\mu P_L\,\nu_\alpha\big)
 \sum_f\bar\psi_f\gamma_\mu\big(\tilde g^f_LP_L + \tilde g^f_RP_R\big)\psi_f,\\
&\nu_\alpha = \sum_{i=1}^{3}U_{\alpha i}\,\nu_i,\qquad P_L = \frac{1 + \Gamma_4}{2},\qquad P_R = \frac{1
 - \Gamma_4}{2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-101}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-101).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{CC}},\ \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{NC}}\): The
  charged-current and neutral-current four-fermion Lagrangians (exchange
  of \(W^\pm\) and of \(Z\) far below their masses). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar\nu_\alpha\): The antineutrino of the flavour \(\alpha\); in a
  Lagrangian the Dirac adjoint of the neutrino field (it annihilates
  antineutrinos). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha\): A flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\) (the flavour
  states of the leptons and neutrinos). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\gamma^a,\ \gamma^M,\ \gamma^\mu\): The Dirac matrices: \(\gamma^a\)
  with a frame index are the author's real \(16 \times 16\) matrices of
  the Clifford algebra of signature \((4, 4)\);
  \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^M\gamma^a\) along a coordinate
  (\(\gamma^{x_0}\), \(\gamma^{x_4}\), \ldots{} the matrices along
  \(x_0\), \(x_4\), \ldots), \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\);
  \(\gamma^\mu\) the matrices of the four observable directions in
  four-dimensional formulas. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) (signature \(+,+,+,-\));
  on the tip it runs over the tip directions; a repeated index is
  summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(P_L,\ P_R\): The projectors on the four-dimensional chiralities,
  \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\) (left-handed) and
  \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\) (right-handed). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(L\): The chirality label L: the left-handed (doublet) zero mode, or
  the left-handed projection \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\ell^-_\alpha,\ \bar\ell^-_\alpha\): The charged lepton of the
  flavour \(\alpha\) (\(e^-\), \(\mu^-\), \(\tau^-\); the lower member
  of the lepton doublet) at low energy, and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\beta\): A second flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu_\alpha,\ \nu_i\): A left-handed neutrino: \(\nu_\alpha\) of the
  flavour \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\) (\(\nu_e\), \(\nu_\mu\),
  \(\nu_\tau\)), \(\nu_i\) of the mass state \(i\) (mass \(m_i\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\psi_i,\ \bar\psi_i\): A Standard-Model fermion field of eight
  dimensions: \(\psi_i\) is the multiplet
  \(\psi \in \{Q, u_R, d_R, \ell, e_R, \nu_R\}\) of the generation
  \(i\), \(\psi_f\) one fermion component \(f\) (in four-dimensional
  formulas its zero mode); \(\bar\psi = \psi^\dagger C\) the Dirac
  adjoint. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7/2}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\) (four dimensions). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\tilde g^f_L,\ \tilde g^f_R,\ \tilde g^f_V\): The neutral-current
  couplings of the fermion \(f\) to the \(Z\) (left-handed, right-handed
  and vector),
  \(\tilde g^f_L = T_3^f - Q^f_{\mathrm{em}}\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(R\): The chirality label R: the right-handed (singlet) zero mode or
  field, or the projection \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Gamma_4\): The four-dimensional chirality matrix,
  \(\Gamma_4 = \mathrm{i}\gamma^{x_1}\gamma^{x_2}\gamma^{x_3}\gamma^{x_4}\);
  a doublet zero mode has \(\Gamma_4 = +1\) (left-handed), a singlet
  zero mode \(\Gamma_4 = -1\) (right-handed). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} At momentum transfers far below \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\)
and \(m_{\mathrm{Z}}\) the exchange of \(W^\pm\) (FE-25) between the
zero modes of the lepton doublets gives the charged-current contact
interaction \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{CC}}\) between the neutrino and the
charged lepton of the same flavour, and the exchange of the \(Z\) the
neutral-current interaction \(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{NC}}\) of the
neutrinos with every fermion \(f\) of the medium, with the chiral
couplings \(\tilde g^f_L\) and \(\tilde g^f_R\). \(P_L\) and \(P_R\)
project on the left-handed and right-handed zero modes. The flavour
states \(\nu_\alpha\) (\(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\)) are the combinations
of the mass states with the mixing matrix \(U\), the tip matrix of the
neutrino sector. Status: STANDARD.

\[
\frac{G_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sqrt2} = \frac{g_2^2}{8m_{\mathrm{W}}^2},\qquad
\tilde g^f_L = T_3^f - Q_{\mathrm{em}}^f\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}},\qquad \tilde g^f_R =
 -Q_{\mathrm{em}}^f\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}},\qquad
\tilde g^f_V = \tilde g^f_L + \tilde g^f_R = T_3^f - 2Q_{\mathrm{em}}^f\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} \tag{FE-102}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-102).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_2\): The four-dimensional SU(2) coupling
  (\(g_2^2 = 4\pi\alpha_2\)). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(g_2^2 = 0.3781\) at \(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\): The mass of \(W^+\) and \(W^-\). Unit: GeV. Value:
  80.3625 GeV (measured, input). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde g^f_L,\ \tilde g^f_R,\ \tilde g^f_V\): The neutral-current
  couplings of the fermion \(f\) to the \(Z\) (left-handed, right-handed
  and vector),
  \(\tilde g^f_L = T_3^f - Q^f_{\mathrm{em}}\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(L\): The chirality label L: the left-handed (doublet) zero mode, or
  the left-handed projection \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_3\): The third component of the weak isospin (\(+\frac12\) for the
  upper and \(-\frac12\) for the lower member of a doublet, 0 for a
  singlet); \(T_3^f\) that of the fermion \(f\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Q_{\mathrm{em}}\): The electric charge in units of the elementary
  charge, \(Q_{\mathrm{em}} = T_3 + Y\); \(Q^f_{\mathrm{em}}\) that of
  the fermion \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The weak mixing angle of the couplings,
  \(\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_2/\hat g_Z\),
  \(\sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_1/\hat g_Z\); fixed by the measured
  \(W\) and \(Z\) masses with \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.776368\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(R\): The chirality label R: the right-handed (singlet) zero mode or
  field, or the projection \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V\): The label V of the vector coupling of a fermion to the \(Z\),
  \(\tilde g^f_V = \tilde g^f_L + \tilde g^f_R\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The Fermi constant is fixed by the four-dimensional
SU(2) coupling and the \(W\) mass; the neutral-current couplings of a
fermion with the weak isospin \(T_3^f\) and the charge
\(Q_{\mathrm{em}}^f\) contain the weak angle; \(\tilde g^f_V\) is the
vector coupling that acts in an unpolarised medium. Status: STANDARD;
UM-2 uses the measured \(G_{\mathrm{F}} = 1.1663788\times10^{-5}\)
GeV\(^{-2}\). The weak angle has two values in UM-2: the tree-level
angle of the \(x_0\) masses
(\(\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 1 - 0.776368 = 0.22363\), FE-55) and the
\(\overline{\mathrm{MS}}\) angle
\(\sin^2\theta_{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}} = 0.23122\) used in the
couplings of the \(Z\) widths; the difference changes the total \(Z\)
width by \(8.3\times10^{-3}\), the size of the electroweak loop
corrections that UM-2 does not compute.

\hypertarget{vacuum-and-matter}{%
\subsection{Vacuum and matter}\label{vacuum-and-matter}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&V_\alpha = \sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\Big[\delta_{\alpha e}\big(n_{e^-} - n_{e^+}\big) + \sum_f\tilde
 g^f_V\big(n_f - n_{\bar f}\big)\Big],\\
&V = \mathrm{diag}\big(V_{\mathrm{CC}},\ 0,\ 0\big) + V_{\mathrm{NC}}\,\mathbf{1},\qquad V_{\mathrm{CC}}
 = \sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\,n_{\mathrm{e}},
 \qquad V_{\mathrm{NC}} = -\frac{G_{\mathrm{F}}\,n_{\mathrm{n}}}{\sqrt2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-103}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-103).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(V_\alpha,\ V\): The matter potential of forward scattering:
  \(V_\alpha\) of the flavour \(\alpha\) (\(V_e\), \(V_\mu\)), \(V\) the
  \(3 \times 3\) matrix of the potentials in the flavour basis;
  antineutrinos have \(-V\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha\): A flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\) (the flavour
  states of the leptons and neutrinos). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{\alpha e}\),
  \(\delta_{l0}\), \(\delta^{I3}\)); with upper and lower index strings
  the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\) (+1
  or -1 if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation of the lower
  ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(e\): The electron flavour as a value of a flavour index
  (\(\delta_{\alpha e}\), \(U_{e2}\), \(V_e\), \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\)).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{e^-},\ n_{e^+}\): The number densities of electrons and of
  positrons. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde g^f_L,\ \tilde g^f_R,\ \tilde g^f_V\): The neutral-current
  couplings of the fermion \(f\) to the \(Z\) (left-handed, right-handed
  and vector),
  \(\tilde g^f_L = T_3^f - Q^f_{\mathrm{em}}\sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V\): The label V of the vector coupling of a fermion to the \(Z\),
  \(\tilde g^f_V = \tilde g^f_L + \tilde g^f_R\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n,\ n_o,\ n_{\psi i},\ n_f,\ n_i\): A number density: \(n(y)\) the
  particle density of dirac16complex in the Kohn-Sham equations and
  \(n_o\) the contribution of the orbital \(o\), \(n_{\psi i}(y)\) the
  density of the Standard-Model fermion \(\psi_i\) (per proper 7-volume
  of eight dimensions); \(n_f\) the density of the fermion component
  \(f\) and \(n_i\) that of the neutrino mass state \(i\) (per 3-volume
  of four dimensions). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\bar f}\): The number density of the antiparticles of the fermion
  \(f\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\mathrm{CC}},\ V_{\mathrm{NC}}\): The charged-current potential
  of electron neutrinos and the neutral-current potential common to all
  flavours. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1}\): The identity matrix (of the size fixed by the context:
  16 x 16 on spinors, 3 x 3 on the generations or flavours, 2 x 2 for
  the sigma field). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{e}}\): The number density of electrons (net of
  positrons); in the Thomson terms of the photon and baryon equations
  only free (ionised) electrons count. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{n}}\): The number density of neutrons (free and bound).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} Coherent forward scattering on the medium gives each
flavour the potential \(V_\alpha\): the charged current acts on
\(\nu_e\) only, with the net electron density; the neutral current acts
on every flavour with the net densities \(n_f - n_{\bar f}\) of the
fermions of the medium weighted by \(\tilde g^f_V\). In neutral matter
of electrons, protons and neutrons the neutral-current terms of the
electrons and the protons cancel (\(\tilde g_V\) of the proton, two up
quarks and one down quark, is minus that of the electron) and the
neutrons leave
\(V_{\mathrm{NC}} = -G_{\mathrm{F}}n_{\mathrm{n}}/\sqrt2\), common to
all flavours. Status: STANDARD (an unpolarised medium at rest;
antineutrinos have \(-V\)).

\[
\mathrm{i}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{\psi}}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = H_{\mathrm{fl}}\,\boldsymbol{\psi},\qquad
H_{\mathrm{fl}} = \frac{1}{2E}\,U\,\mathrm{diag}\big(m_1^2,\ m_2^2,\ m_3^2\big)\,U^\dagger + V,\qquad
\mathcal{U}(L_{\mathrm{b}}) = \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}H_{\mathrm{fl}}L_{\mathrm{b}}},\qquad
\mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha\to\nu_\beta) = \lvert\mathcal{U}_{\beta\alpha}\rvert^2 \tag{FE-104}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-104).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\boldsymbol{\psi}\): The vector of the three flavour amplitudes of a
  neutrino. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(H_{\mathrm{fl}}\): The flavour Hamiltonian of a neutrino of energy
  \(E\) (vacuum mass term plus matter potential). Unit: GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_1,\ m_2,\ m_3\): The masses of the neutrino mass states \(\nu_1\),
  \(\nu_2\), \(\nu_3\) (normal ordering). Unit: GeV (quoted in meV).
  Value: 1.000, 8.712, 50.140 meV (\(m_1\) ASSUMED). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_\alpha,\ V\): The matter potential of forward scattering:
  \(V_\alpha\) of the flavour \(\alpha\) (\(V_e\), \(V_\mu\)), \(V\) the
  \(3 \times 3\) matrix of the potentials in the flavour basis;
  antineutrinos have \(-V\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{U}\): The flavour evolution operator over the baseline
  \(L_{\mathrm{b}}\); \(\mathcal{U}_{\beta\alpha}\) the amplitude of
  \(\nu_\alpha \to \nu_\beta\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(L_{\mathrm{b}}\): The baseline: the distance travelled by the
  neutrino. Unit: length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{P}\): The probability of a flavour transition,
  \(\mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha \to \nu_\beta)\); \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\) the
  survival probability of electron neutrinos. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu_\alpha,\ \nu_i\): A left-handed neutrino: \(\nu_\alpha\) of the
  flavour \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\) (\(\nu_e\), \(\nu_\mu\),
  \(\nu_\tau\)), \(\nu_i\) of the mass state \(i\) (mass \(m_i\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha\): A flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\) (the flavour
  states of the leptons and neutrinos). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\beta\): A second flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} \(\boldsymbol{\psi}\) is the vector of the three
flavour amplitudes of a neutrino of energy \(E\); the flavour
Hamiltonian \(H_{\mathrm{fl}}\) contains the vacuum part (the squared
masses rotated with \(U\)) and the matter potential \(V\). For a
constant density the evolution over the baseline \(L_{\mathrm{b}}\) is
the matrix exponential \(\mathcal{U}\), and \(\mathcal{P}\) is the
probability of the flavour change. The common part
\(V_{\mathrm{NC}}\mathbf{1}\) is a phase and drops out. Status: STANDARD
(solved exactly: matrix exponential for constant density, ordered
products of layers otherwise; the vacuum formula FE-105 is reproduced to
\(3.9\times10^{-15}\), unitarity to \(1.8\times10^{-15}\)). Residual of
the stored probabilities (unitarity, vacuum formula, CPT, the T2K, NOvA
and DUNE probabilities, the solar survival probability)
\(7.1\times10^{-12}\).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha\to\nu_\beta) = \Big\lvert\sum_{i=1}^{3}U_{\beta i}U^*_{\alpha i}\,
 \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}m_i^2L_{\mathrm{b}}/(2E)}\Big\rvert^2,\qquad
 \mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha\to\nu_\beta) = \mathcal{P}(\bar\nu_\beta\to\bar\nu_\alpha),\\
&\mathcal{P}(\nu_\mu\to\nu_e) - \mathcal{P}(\nu_e\to\nu_\mu) =
 -16J_{\mathrm{CP}}\sin\Delta_{21}\sin\Delta_{31}\sin\Delta_{32},\qquad
 \Delta_{ij} = \frac{\Delta m_{ij}^2L_{\mathrm{b}}}{4E},\qquad
 J_{\mathrm{CP}} = \mathrm{Im}\big(U_{e2}U_{\mu3}U_{e3}^*U_{\mu2}^*\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-105}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-105).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{P}\): The probability of a flavour transition,
  \(\mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha \to \nu_\beta)\); \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\) the
  survival probability of electron neutrinos. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu_\alpha,\ \nu_i\): A left-handed neutrino: \(\nu_\alpha\) of the
  flavour \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\) (\(\nu_e\), \(\nu_\mu\),
  \(\nu_\tau\)), \(\nu_i\) of the mass state \(i\) (mass \(m_i\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha\): A flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\) (the flavour
  states of the leptons and neutrinos). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\beta\): A second flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \({}^*\): Complex conjugation of a number or of each entry of a
  matrix. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(L_{\mathrm{b}}\): The baseline: the distance travelled by the
  neutrino. Unit: length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar\nu_\alpha\): The antineutrino of the flavour \(\alpha\); in a
  Lagrangian the Dirac adjoint of the neutrino field (it annihilates
  antineutrinos). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(e\): The electron flavour as a value of a flavour index
  (\(\delta_{\alpha e}\), \(U_{e2}\), \(V_e\), \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\)).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(J_{\mathrm{CP}}\): The Jarlskog invariant of a mixing matrix (the
  measure of CP violation). Unit: dimensionless. Value: \(-0.01777\)
  (PMNS), \(3.117\times10^{-5}\) (CKM). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta_{ij}\): The oscillation phase of the pair of mass states
  \((i, j)\), \(\Delta_{ij} = \Delta m_{ij}^2L_{\mathrm{b}}/(4E)\).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{Im}\): The imaginary part of a complex number. Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} In vacuum the probability is the squared sum of
three amplitudes with the phases \(m_i^2L_{\mathrm{b}}/(2E)\); CPT
symmetry relates neutrinos and antineutrinos; the difference of the
probabilities of a process and its time reverse is proportional to the
Jarlskog invariant \(J_{\mathrm{CP}}\) (CP violation of the tip matrix)
and to the three oscillation phases \(\Delta_{ij}\). Status: STANDARD
(CPT checked to \(2.8\times10^{-16}\), the T-odd difference to
\(5.9\times10^{-14}\)).

In the two-flavour approximation of one sector (for example the 1-2
sector with \(\theta_{13} = 0\)) the flavour Hamiltonian of FE-104 is,
up to a multiple of \(\mathbf{1}\),

\[
H_{\mathrm{fl}} = \frac{\Delta m_{21}^2}{4E}\begin{pmatrix}-\cos2\theta_{12} & \sin2\theta_{12}\\ \sin2\theta_{12} &
 \cos2\theta_{12}\end{pmatrix} + \begin{pmatrix}V_{\mathrm{CC}} & 0\\ 0 & 0\end{pmatrix},
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(H_{\mathrm{fl}}\): The flavour Hamiltonian of a neutrino of energy
  \(E\) (vacuum mass term plus matter potential). Unit: GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2,\ \Delta m_{31}^2,\ \Delta m_{32}^2\): The
  differences of the squared neutrino masses,
  \(\Delta m_{ij}^2 = m_i^2 - m_j^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\)
  (quoted in \(\mathrm{eV}^{2}\)). Value:
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2 = 7.49\times10^{-5}\) eV\(^2\),
  \(\Delta m_{31}^2 = 2.513\times10^{-3}\) eV\(^2\) (NuFIT 6.0). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{ij}\): The mixing angles of the standard parametrisation of
  \(U\) (or of \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\)): \(\theta_{12}\), \(\theta_{13}\),
  \(\theta_{23}\); in two-flavour formulas \(\theta_{ij}\) is the angle
  of the sector \((i, j)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\) (PMNS). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\mathrm{CC}},\ V_{\mathrm{NC}}\): The charged-current potential
  of electron neutrinos and the neutral-current potential common to all
  flavours. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

whose two diagonal entries are equal, and the mixing in matter maximal,
at \(2EV_{\mathrm{CC}} = \Delta m_{21}^2\cos2\theta_{12}\); with the
third flavour the conditions become:

\[
2E\,V_{\mathrm{CC}} = \Delta m_{ee}^2\cos2\theta_{13},\qquad \Delta m_{ee}^2 = c_{12}^2\,\Delta m_{31}^2
 + s_{12}^2\,\Delta m_{32}^2,
\qquad 2E\,V_{\mathrm{CC}}\,c_{13}^2 = \Delta m_{21}^2\cos2\theta_{12} \tag{FE-106}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-106).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\mathrm{CC}},\ V_{\mathrm{NC}}\): The charged-current potential
  of electron neutrinos and the neutral-current potential common to all
  flavours. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta m_{ee}^2\): The effective squared-mass difference of
  electron-neutrino disappearance,
  \(\Delta m_{ee}^2 = c_{12}^2\Delta m_{31}^2 + s_{12}^2\Delta m_{32}^2\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{ij}\): The mixing angles of the standard parametrisation of
  \(U\) (or of \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\)): \(\theta_{12}\), \(\theta_{13}\),
  \(\theta_{23}\); in two-flavour formulas \(\theta_{ij}\) is the angle
  of the sector \((i, j)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\) (PMNS). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{12},\ c_{13},\ c_{23}\): The cosines of the mixing angles,
  \(c_{ij} = \cos\theta_{ij}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2,\ \Delta m_{31}^2,\ \Delta m_{32}^2\): The
  differences of the squared neutrino masses,
  \(\Delta m_{ij}^2 = m_i^2 - m_j^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\)
  (quoted in \(\mathrm{eV}^{2}\)). Value:
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2 = 7.49\times10^{-5}\) eV\(^2\),
  \(\Delta m_{31}^2 = 2.513\times10^{-3}\) eV\(^2\) (NuFIT 6.0). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(s_{12},\ s_{13},\ s_{23}\): The sines of the mixing angles,
  \(s_{ij} = \sin\theta_{ij}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The resonance conditions of the 1-3 sector (with the
effective splitting \(\Delta m_{ee}^2\), the \(\nu_e\)-weighted average
of \(\Delta m_{31}^2\) and \(\Delta m_{32}^2\)) and of the 1-2 sector
(with the factor \(c_{13}^2\), the \(\nu_e\) content of the 1-2
subspace). Status: STANDARD. The 1-3 resonance lies at 11.135 GeV in
rock of 2.8 g/cm\(^3\) (the exact three-flavour evolution gives 11.133
GeV); the 1-2 resonance at the centre of the Sun at 1.93 MeV.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{P}_{ee} = c_{13}^4\Big(\frac12 + \frac12\cos2\theta_{12}\cos2\theta^{\mathrm{m}}_{12}\Big) +
 s_{13}^4,\qquad
 A_{\mathrm{MSW}} = \frac{2E\,V_{\mathrm{CC}}\,c_{13}^2}{\Delta m_{21}^2},\\
&\cos2\theta^{\mathrm{m}}_{12} = \frac{\cos2\theta_{12} - A_{\mathrm{MSW}}}{\sqrt{\big(\cos2\theta_{12}
 - A_{\mathrm{MSW}}\big)^2
 + \sin^22\theta_{12}}}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-107}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-107).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{P}\): The probability of a flavour transition,
  \(\mathcal{P}(\nu_\alpha \to \nu_\beta)\); \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\) the
  survival probability of electron neutrinos. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(e\): The electron flavour as a value of a flavour index
  (\(\delta_{\alpha e}\), \(U_{e2}\), \(V_e\), \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\)).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c_{12},\ c_{13},\ c_{23}\): The cosines of the mixing angles,
  \(c_{ij} = \cos\theta_{ij}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{ij}\): The mixing angles of the standard parametrisation of
  \(U\) (or of \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\)): \(\theta_{12}\), \(\theta_{13}\),
  \(\theta_{23}\); in two-flavour formulas \(\theta_{ij}\) is the angle
  of the sector \((i, j)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\) (PMNS). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta^{\mathrm{m}}_{12}\): The effective 1-2 mixing angle in matter

  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s_{12},\ s_{13},\ s_{23}\): The sines of the mixing angles,
  \(s_{ij} = \sin\theta_{ij}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(A_{\mathrm{MSW}}\): The ratio of the matter term to the vacuum term
  in the 1-2 sector. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\mathrm{CC}},\ V_{\mathrm{NC}}\): The charged-current potential
  of electron neutrinos and the neutral-current potential common to all
  flavours. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2,\ \Delta m_{31}^2,\ \Delta m_{32}^2\): The
  differences of the squared neutrino masses,
  \(\Delta m_{ij}^2 = m_i^2 - m_j^2\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\)
  (quoted in \(\mathrm{eV}^{2}\)). Value:
  \(\Delta m_{21}^2 = 7.49\times10^{-5}\) eV\(^2\),
  \(\Delta m_{31}^2 = 2.513\times10^{-3}\) eV\(^2\) (NuFIT 6.0). Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The survival probability of solar electron neutrinos
produced at a density where \(A_{\mathrm{MSW}}\) is the ratio of the
matter term to the vacuum term: the mixing angle in matter
\(\theta^{\mathrm{m}}_{12}\) at the production point, adiabatic
evolution to the surface, and decoherence on the way to the Earth; the
1-3 sector enters through \(c_{13}^4\) and \(s_{13}^4\). Status:
STANDARD (the numerical three-flavour evolution through the solar
density agrees to \(1.3\times10^{-3}\), the size of the non-adiabatic
and production-region effects that the formula drops).

\[
n_{\mathrm{e}}^{\mathrm{res}} = \frac{\Delta m_{ij}^2\cos2\theta_{ij}}{2\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}E},\qquad
\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}} = \frac{\Delta m_{ij}^2\sin^22\theta_{ij}}{2E\cos2\theta_{ij}\,\lvert\mathrm{d}\ln
 n_{\mathrm{e}}/\mathrm{d}r\rvert} \gg 1,
\qquad \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{LZ}} = \mathrm{e}^{-\pi\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}}/2} \tag{FE-108}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-108).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{e}}^{\mathrm{res}}\): The electron density of an MSW
  resonance. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation index (\(j = 1, 2, 3\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{ij}\): The mixing angles of the standard parametrisation of
  \(U\) (or of \(V_{\mathrm{CKM}}\)): \(\theta_{12}\), \(\theta_{13}\),
  \(\theta_{23}\); in two-flavour formulas \(\theta_{ij}\) is the angle
  of the sector \((i, j)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\) (PMNS). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}}\): The adiabaticity parameter of a resonance
  (adiabatic for \(\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}} \gg 1\)). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{e}}\): The number density of electrons (net of
  positrons); in the Thomson terms of the photon and baryon equations
  only free (ionised) electrons count. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(r\): A distance: between the two masses in \(V_{\mathrm{N}}(r)\),
  from the centre of the star in the adiabaticity condition. Unit:
  length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{LZ}}\): The Landau-Zener probability of a jump
  between the matter eigenstates at a resonance. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The electron density at the resonance, the
adiabaticity parameter \(\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}}\) (the ratio of the
oscillation length at the resonance to the width of the resonance
region; the evolution is adiabatic for \(\gamma_{\mathrm{ad}} \gg 1\)),
and the Landau-Zener probability \(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{LZ}}\) of a jump
between the matter eigenstates. Status: STANDARD (two-flavour
conditions; in a supernova envelope both resonances are adiabatic,
\(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{LZ}} \le 2.6\times10^{-77}\)).

\hypertarget{the-early-universe}{%
\subsection{The early Universe}\label{the-early-universe}}

\[
V_e - V_\mu = -\frac{8\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\lvert\vec k\rvert}{3m_{\mathrm{W}}^2}\,\rho_{\mathrm{e}},\qquad
\rho_{\mathrm{e}} \simeq \frac{7\pi^2}{60}\,T^4\ \ (T \gg m_{\mathrm{e}}) \tag{FE-109}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-109).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(V_\alpha,\ V\): The matter potential of forward scattering:
  \(V_\alpha\) of the flavour \(\alpha\) (\(V_e\), \(V_\mu\)), \(V\) the
  \(3 \times 3\) matrix of the potentials in the flavour basis;
  antineutrinos have \(-V\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(e\): The electron flavour as a value of a flavour index
  (\(\delta_{\alpha e}\), \(U_{e2}\), \(V_e\), \(\mathcal{P}_{ee}\)).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec k,\ \vec k_o\): The wave vector (momentum) in ordinary 3-space;
  \(\vec k_o\) that of the orbital \(o\), a point of the lattice
  \(k_{\mathrm{lat}}\vec n_o\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{W}}\): The mass of \(W^+\) and \(W^-\). Unit: GeV. Value:
  80.3625 GeV (measured, input). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{e}},\ p_{\mathrm{e}}\): The energy density and the
  pressure of the electrons and positrons together (ideal gas). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{e}}\): The electron mass. Unit: GeV. Value: 0.51099895
  MeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} In the hot plasma the net electron density is tiny
(of the order of \(\eta_{\mathrm{b}} = 6.1\times10^{-10}\) times the
photon density), and the leading potential is the thermal one: the \(W\)
propagator expanded to the next order in
\(\lvert\vec k\rvert T/m_{\mathrm{W}}^2\), proportional to the energy
density \(\rho_{\mathrm{e}}\) of the electrons and positrons. It
suppresses the flavour mixing at high temperature. Status: STANDARD
(leading order in \(T/m_{\mathrm{W}}\); it falls below the vacuum term
at \(T = 4.99\) MeV for the atmospheric splitting and 2.39 MeV for the
solar one, at \(\lvert\vec k\rvert = 3.15\,T\)). Residual
\(8.7\times10^{-12}\); the neglected CP-asymmetric potential of the net
electron density is \(1.1\times10^{-2}\) of the thermal one at the onset
(ESTIMATE).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathrm{i}\Big(\partial_{x_4} + \frac{\vec k}{\lvert\vec
 k\rvert}\cdot\vec\nabla\Big)\boldsymbol{\rho}_{\vec k}
 = \big[H^{\mathrm{vac}}_{\vec k} + V + V_{\nu\nu},\ \boldsymbol{\rho}_{\vec k}\big] +
 \mathrm{i}\,C_{\mathrm{coll}}[\boldsymbol{\rho}],
 \qquad H^{\mathrm{vac}}_{\vec k} =
 \frac{U\,\mathrm{diag}\big(m_1^2,\ m_2^2,\ m_3^2\big)\,U^\dagger}{2\lvert\vec k\rvert},\\
&V_{\nu\nu} = \sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^3\vec k_2}{(2\pi)^3}\Big(1 - \frac{\vec
 k\cdot\vec k_2}{\lvert\vec k\rvert\lvert\vec k_2\rvert}\Big)
 \big(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{\vec k_2} - \bar{\boldsymbol{\rho}}_{\vec k_2}\big),\qquad
 \partial_{x_4} \to \partial_{x_4} - \mathcal{H}\lvert\vec k\rvert\,\partial_{\lvert\vec
 k\rvert}\ \ \text{(expanding Universe)}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-110}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-110).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) is the derivative
  with respect to the coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_y\) with respect to
  \(y\), \(\partial_{x_4}\) with respect to the time \(x_4\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\vec k,\ \vec k_o\): The wave vector (momentum) in ordinary 3-space;
  \(\vec k_o\) that of the orbital \(o\), a point of the lattice
  \(k_{\mathrm{lat}}\vec n_o\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec\nabla\): The gradient in ordinary 3-space,
  \((\partial/\partial x_1, \partial/\partial x_2, \partial/\partial x_3)\).
  Unit: 1/length. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{\vec k},\ \bar{\boldsymbol{\rho}}_{\vec k}\): The
  \(3 \times 3\) flavour density matrices of the neutrinos and of the
  antineutrinos of momentum \(\vec k\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(H^{\mathrm{vac}}_{\vec k}\): The vacuum flavour Hamiltonian of a
  neutrino of momentum \(\vec k\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_\alpha,\ V\): The matter potential of forward scattering:
  \(V_\alpha\) of the flavour \(\alpha\) (\(V_e\), \(V_\mu\)), \(V\) the
  \(3 \times 3\) matrix of the potentials in the flavour basis;
  antineutrinos have \(-V\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_{\nu\nu}\): The potential of neutrino-neutrino forward scattering
  (a matrix in flavour space). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(C_{\mathrm{coll}}\): The collision term of the quantum kinetic
  equations. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_1,\ m_2,\ m_3\): The masses of the neutrino mass states \(\nu_1\),
  \(\nu_2\), \(\nu_3\) (normal ordering). Unit: GeV (quoted in meV).
  Value: 1.000, 8.712, 50.140 meV (\(m_1\) ASSUMED). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor (for field operators the adjoint
  operator). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k_2,\ \vec k_2\): A second momentum (variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The density matrix \(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{\vec k}\) of
the neutrinos of momentum \(\vec k\) (its diagonal the occupation
numbers of the flavours, its off-diagonal entries the flavour coherence;
\(\bar{\boldsymbol{\rho}}\) that of the antineutrinos) evolves by the
commutator with the vacuum Hamiltonian \(H^{\mathrm{vac}}\), the matter
potential and the neutrino-neutrino potential \(V_{\nu\nu}\) (forward
scattering on the other neutrinos, with the angular factor of the
current-current interaction), and by the collision term
\(C_{\mathrm{coll}}\); in the expanding Universe the momenta redshift.
Status: STANDARD. It is used linearised for the fast flavour instability
(FE-117) and momentum-averaged for the decoupling (FE-111).

\[
\begin{aligned}
\frac{\mathrm{d}T_\gamma}{\mathrm{d}x_4} &= -\frac{4\mathcal{H}\rho_\gamma +
 3\mathcal{H}\big(\rho_{\mathrm{e}} + p_{\mathrm{e}}\big)
 + 3\mathcal{H}T_\gamma\,\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{\mathrm{QED}}}{\mathrm{d}T_\gamma} + C_{\nu_e} + 2C_{\nu_\mu}}
 {\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho_\gamma}{\mathrm{d}T_\gamma} + \frac{\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathrm{e}}}{\mathrm{d}T_\gamma}
 + T_\gamma\,\frac{\mathrm{d}^2p_{\mathrm{QED}}}{\mathrm{d}T_\gamma^2}},\qquad
 \frac{\mathrm{d}T_{\nu_\alpha}}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = -\mathcal{H}T_{\nu_\alpha} +
 \frac{C_{\nu_\alpha}}{\mathrm{d}\rho_{\nu_\alpha}/\mathrm{d}T_{\nu_\alpha}},\\
\mathcal{H} &= \frac{\sqrt{8\pi\rho_{\mathrm{tot}}/3}}{M_{\mathrm{Pl}}},\qquad \rho_\gamma =
 \frac{\pi^2}{15}\,T_\gamma^4,\qquad
 \rho_{\nu_\alpha} = \frac{7\pi^2}{120}\,T_{\nu_\alpha}^4
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-111}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-111).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\gamma\): The temperature of the photons (equal to the plasma
  temperature \(T\); the subscript is written where the neutrinos have
  their own temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.7255 K today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\rho_\gamma\): The energy density of the photons,
  \(\rho_\gamma = \pi^2T_\gamma^4/15\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{e}},\ p_{\mathrm{e}}\): The energy density and the
  pressure of the electrons and positrons together (ideal gas). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p_{\mathrm{QED}},\ p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(2)},\ p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(3)}\):
  The finite-temperature QED correction to the pressure of the
  electron-photon plasma, and its parts of order \(e^2\) and \(e^3\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(C_{\nu_\alpha},\ C_{\nu_e},\ C_{\nu_\mu}\): The energy transfer rate
  per volume into the neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) by collisions.
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\nu_\alpha},\ T_{\nu_e},\ T_{\nu_\mu}\): The temperature of the
  neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) (of \(\nu_e\), of \(\nu_\mu\) and
  \(\nu_\tau\)) during decoupling. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_\nu,\ \rho_{\nu_\alpha}\): The energy density of one neutrino
  flavour (neutrinos and antineutrinos) at the common temperature
  \(T_\nu\), \(\frac78\frac{\pi^2}{15}T_\nu^4\) when massless;
  \(\rho_{\nu_\alpha}\) that of the flavour \(\alpha\) at its
  temperature \(T_{\nu_\alpha}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{tot}},\ p_{\mathrm{tot}}\): The total background
  energy density and pressure of four-dimensional cosmology (all
  species). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\): The Planck mass,
  \(M_{\mathrm{Pl}} = G_{\mathrm{N}}^{-1/2}\). Unit: GeV. Value:
  \(1.22091\times10^{19}\) GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The momentum-averaged form of FE-110 for the
temperatures of the photons and of the neutrino flavours: the photon
temperature falls by the expansion (the dilution of the photons, of the
electron-positron plasma and of the QED interaction pressure
\(p_{\mathrm{QED}}\)) and rises by the energy that the collisions
\(C_{\nu_\alpha}\) take from the neutrinos; each neutrino temperature
falls with the expansion and changes by its collisions. The Hubble rate
follows from the total energy density. Status: STANDARD (integrated from
20 MeV to 5 keV with LSODA, relative tolerance \(10^{-11}\); the method
reproduces the published values of Escudero (2020) to
\(5\times10^{-4}\); \(\nu_\tau\) follows \(\nu_\mu\)). Residual
\(2.7\times10^{-12}\).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&C_{\nu_e} = \frac{G_{\mathrm{F}}^2}{\pi^5}\Big[4\Big(\big(\tilde g^{\nu_e e}_L\big)^2 + \big(\tilde
 g^{\nu_e e}_R\big)^2\Big)
 \mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}(T_\gamma, T_{\nu_e}) + 2\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}(T_{\nu_\mu}, T_{\nu_e})\Big],\\
&C_{\nu_\mu} = \frac{G_{\mathrm{F}}^2}{\pi^5}\Big[4\Big(\big(\tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_L\big)^2 + \big(\tilde
 g^{\nu_\mu e}_R\big)^2\Big)
 \mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}(T_\gamma, T_{\nu_\mu}) - \mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}(T_{\nu_\mu}, T_{\nu_e})\Big],\\
&\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}(T_{\mathrm{A}}, T_{\mathrm{B}}) = 32f_{\mathrm{a}}\big(T_{\mathrm{A}}^9 -
 T_{\mathrm{B}}^9\big)
 + 56f_{\mathrm{s}}T_{\mathrm{A}}^4T_{\mathrm{B}}^4\big(T_{\mathrm{A}} - T_{\mathrm{B}}\big),\\
&\tilde g^{\nu_e e}_L = \frac12 + \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}},\qquad \tilde g^{\nu_e e}_R = \tilde
 g^{\nu_\mu e}_R = \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}},
 \qquad \tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_L = -\frac12 + \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-112}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-112).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(C_{\nu_\alpha},\ C_{\nu_e},\ C_{\nu_\mu}\): The energy transfer rate
  per volume into the neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) by collisions.
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_e e}_L,\ \tilde g^{\nu_e e}_R\): The effective left-
  and right-handed couplings of electron neutrinos to electrons (charged
  and neutral current),
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_e e}_L = \frac12 + \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\),
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_e e}_R = \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: 0.727 and 0.233 (radiatively corrected). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(L\): The chirality label L: the left-handed (doublet) zero mode, or
  the left-handed projection \(P_L = (1 + \Gamma_4)/2\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(R\): The chirality label R: the right-handed (singlet) zero mode or
  field, or the projection \(P_R = (1 - \Gamma_4)/2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}\): The energy-transfer function of two
  species with the temperatures \(T_{\mathrm{A}}\) and
  \(T_{\mathrm{B}}\) (Maxwell-Boltzmann with the corrections
  \(f_{\mathrm{a}}\), \(f_{\mathrm{s}}\)). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{9}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\gamma\): The temperature of the photons (equal to the plasma
  temperature \(T\); the subscript is written where the neutrinos have
  their own temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.7255 K today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\nu_\alpha},\ T_{\nu_e},\ T_{\nu_\mu}\): The temperature of the
  neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) (of \(\nu_e\), of \(\nu_\mu\) and
  \(\nu_\tau\)) during decoupling. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_L,\ \tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_R\): The effective
  couplings of muon (and tau) neutrinos to electrons (neutral current
  only),
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_L = -\frac12 + \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\),
  \(\tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}_R = \sin^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(-0.273\) and 0.233 (radiatively corrected).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\mathrm{A}},\ T_{\mathrm{B}}\): The temperatures of the two
  species that exchange energy in \(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}\). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f_{\mathrm{a}},\ f_{\mathrm{s}}\): The Fermi-Dirac corrections of
  the annihilation and scattering parts of the energy transfer. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: 0.884 and 0.829. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\theta_{\mathrm{W}}\): The weak mixing angle of the couplings,
  \(\cos\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_2/\hat g_Z\),
  \(\sin\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = \hat g_1/\hat g_Z\); fixed by the measured
  \(W\) and \(Z\) masses with \(\rho_{\mathrm{EW},0}\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\cos^2\theta_{\mathrm{W}} = 0.776368\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The rates of energy transfer between the
electron-positron plasma and the neutrinos (annihilation and scattering,
with the couplings \(\tilde g^{\nu_e e}\) and \(\tilde g^{\nu_\mu e}\);
the electron neutrinos also have the charged current) and between the
neutrino flavours; \(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{tr}}\) is the transfer
function between two thermal baths of temperatures \(T_{\mathrm{A}}\)
and \(T_{\mathrm{B}}\), with the factors \(f_{\mathrm{a}} = 0.884\) and
\(f_{\mathrm{s}} = 0.829\) of the Fermi-Dirac statistics. Status:
STANDARD (the radiatively corrected couplings 0.727, 0.233, \(-0.273\),
0.233 are used; the tree-level ones change \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) by
\(4\times10^{-4}\)).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&p_{\mathrm{QED}} = p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(2)} + p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(3)},\qquad
 p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(2)} = -\frac{e^2T^2}{12\pi^2}\,\mathcal{Q}_1 - \frac{e^2}{8\pi^4}\,\mathcal{Q}_1^2
 + \frac{e^2m_{\mathrm{e}}^2}{16\pi^4}\,\mathcal{Q}_2,\\
&p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(3)} = \frac{e^3T}{12\pi^4}\,\mathcal{Q}_3^{3/2},\qquad
 \rho_{\mathrm{QED}} = -p_{\mathrm{QED}} + T\,\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{\mathrm{QED}}}{\mathrm{d}T}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-113}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-113).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(p_{\mathrm{QED}},\ p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(2)},\ p_{\mathrm{QED}}^{(3)}\):
  The finite-temperature QED correction to the pressure of the
  electron-photon plasma, and its parts of order \(e^2\) and \(e^3\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(e\): The elementary charge (the charge of the positron),
  \(e^2 = 4\pi\alpha_{\mathrm{em}}\); \(W^+\) has the charge \(+e\) and
  \(W^-\) the charge \(-e\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(e^2 = 4\pi/137.036\) at low energy. Status: STANDARD.
\item
