  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Q}_1,\ \mathcal{Q}_2,\ \mathcal{Q}_3\): The momentum
  integrals of the QED pressure. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{e}}\): The electron mass. Unit: GeV. Value: 0.51099895
  MeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{QED}}\): The QED correction to the energy density of
  the plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The finite-temperature QED correction to the
pressure of the electron-photon plasma at order \(e^2\) and \(e^3\),
from the integrals of FE-114; \(\rho_{\mathrm{QED}}\) follows by the
thermodynamic relation. It raises \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) by 0.009 (the
decoupling computed with and without it). Status: STANDARD (the massless
limits \(-5e^2T^4/288\) and \(e^3T^4/(36\sqrt3\,\pi)\) reproduced to
\(10^{-16}\)).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{Q}_1 = \int_0^\infty\mathrm{d}k\,\frac{k^2}{E}\,n_{\mathrm{D}}(k),\qquad
 \mathcal{Q}_2 = \int_0^\infty\!\!\int_0^\infty\mathrm{d}k\,\mathrm{d}k_2\,\frac{kk_2}{E(k)E(k_2)}
 \ln\Big\lvert\frac{k + k_2}{k - k_2}\Big\rvert\,n_{\mathrm{D}}(k)\,n_{\mathrm{D}}(k_2),\\
&\mathcal{Q}_3 = \int_0^\infty\mathrm{d}k\,\frac{k^2 + E^2}{E}\,n_{\mathrm{D}}(k),\qquad
 n_{\mathrm{D}}(k) = \frac{2}{\mathrm{e}^{E/T} + 1},\qquad E = \sqrt{k^2 + m_{\mathrm{e}}^2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-114}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-114).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{Q}_1,\ \mathcal{Q}_2,\ \mathcal{Q}_3\): The momentum
  integrals of the QED pressure. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{2}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{D}}(k)\): The Fermi-Dirac occupation of the electrons and
  positrons summed over the two spin states. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k_2,\ \vec k_2\): A second momentum (variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_{\mathrm{e}}\): The electron mass. Unit: GeV. Value: 0.51099895
  MeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The momentum integrals over the electron and
positron distribution \(n_{\mathrm{D}}\) (two spin states, energy \(E\)
with the electron mass \(m_{\mathrm{e}}\)). Status: STANDARD
(Gauss-Legendre panels; the logarithmic singularity at \(k_2 = k\)
removed by a substitution).

For instantaneous decoupling the entropy of the electron-positron plasma
goes to the photons alone, and

\[
T_{\nu_e} = T_{\nu_\mu} = \Big(\frac{4}{11}\Big)^{1/3}T_\gamma\quad\Rightarrow\quad
N_{\mathrm{eff}} = \Big(\frac{11}{4}\Big)^{4/3}\cdot3\Big(\frac{4}{11}\Big)^{4/3} = 3.
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(T_{\nu_\alpha},\ T_{\nu_e},\ T_{\nu_\mu}\): The temperature of the
  neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) (of \(\nu_e\), of \(\nu_\mu\) and
  \(\nu_\tau\)) during decoupling. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\gamma\): The temperature of the photons (equal to the plasma
  temperature \(T\); the subscript is written where the neutrinos have
  their own temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.7255 K today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\): The effective number of neutrino species (the
  radiation beyond the photons in units of one neutrino species with
  \(T_\nu/T_\gamma = (4/11)^{1/3}\)); \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an
  addition to it. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.0432 (neutrinos) +
  0.0010 (fold bands). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\[
N_{\mathrm{eff}} = \Big(\frac{11}{4}\Big)^{4/3}\Big[\Big(\frac{T_{\nu_e}}{T_\gamma}\Big)^4 +
 2\Big(\frac{T_{\nu_\mu}}{T_\gamma}\Big)^4\Big],
\qquad \frac{\mathrm{d}T_\nu}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = -\mathcal{H}T_\nu + \frac{C_{\nu_e} +
 2C_{\nu_\mu}}{3\,\mathrm{d}\rho_\nu/\mathrm{d}T_\nu} \tag{FE-115}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-115).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\): The effective number of neutrino species (the
  radiation beyond the photons in units of one neutrino species with
  \(T_\nu/T_\gamma = (4/11)^{1/3}\)); \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an
  addition to it. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.0432 (neutrinos) +
  0.0010 (fold bands). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\nu_\alpha},\ T_{\nu_e},\ T_{\nu_\mu}\): The temperature of the
  neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) (of \(\nu_e\), of \(\nu_\mu\) and
  \(\nu_\tau\)) during decoupling. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\gamma\): The temperature of the photons (equal to the plasma
  temperature \(T\); the subscript is written where the neutrinos have
  their own temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.7255 K today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\nu\): The temperature of the relic neutrinos (one common
  temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 1.9454 K today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(C_{\nu_\alpha},\ C_{\nu_e},\ C_{\nu_\mu}\): The energy transfer rate
  per volume into the neutrinos of the flavour \(\alpha\) by collisions.
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{5}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_\nu,\ \rho_{\nu_\alpha}\): The energy density of one neutrino
  flavour (neutrinos and antineutrinos) at the common temperature
  \(T_\nu\), \(\frac78\frac{\pi^2}{15}T_\nu^4\) when massless;
  \(\rho_{\nu_\alpha}\) that of the flavour \(\alpha\) at its
  temperature \(T_{\nu_\alpha}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The effective number of neutrino species measures
the energy density of the neutrinos relative to that of three species
decoupled instantaneously; the second relation is the common-temperature
form (efficient oscillations). Status: STANDARD. UM-2 computes 3.0476
(separate temperatures) and 3.0485 (common temperature) and adopts the
precision value 3.0432 (Cielo et al.~2023); the difference,
\(4\times10^{-3}\), is the electron-mass and momentum dependence that
the temperature method omits. The fold bands add 0.0010 (FE-129,
FE-130); the sum agrees with the value in the CMB computation to
\(5\times10^{-16}\).

\hypertarget{dense-neutrino-gases}{%
\subsection{Dense neutrino gases}\label{dense-neutrino-gases}}

\[
G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u) = \sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}\big[n_{\nu_e}\,g_{\nu_e}(u) -
 n_{\bar\nu_e}\,g_{\bar\nu_e}(u)\big],\qquad
\int_{-1}^{1}g_{\nu_e}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2} = \int_{-1}^{1}g_{\bar\nu_e}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2} = 1,\qquad
\mathcal{I}_l = \int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2}\,G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\,u^l \tag{FE-116}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-116).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\): The angular distribution of the electron
  lepton number of a dense neutrino gas (in units of a potential). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u\): The cosine of the angle between a momentum and the symmetry
  axis \(x_3\) of the neutrino gas. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}}\): The Fermi constant. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\).
  Value: \(1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) (measured). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\nu_e},\ n_{\bar\nu_e}\): The number densities of electron
  neutrinos and electron antineutrinos. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{\nu_e}(u),\ g_{\bar\nu_e}(u)\): The normalised angular
  distributions of electron neutrinos and antineutrinos. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{I}_l\): The \(l\)-th angular moment of the
  electron-lepton-number distribution. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(l\): The multipole (angular moment) number \(l = 0, 1, 2, \dots\) of
  a Boltzmann hierarchy or of an angular moment \(\int u^l\cdots\); as a
  coefficient it is the same number. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} In a dense, homogeneous and axially symmetric gas of
electron neutrinos and antineutrinos (near the neutrinosphere of a
supernova), \(G_{\mathrm{ELN}}\) is the electron-lepton-number
distribution over the cosine \(u\) of the angle to the symmetry axis
\(x_3\), with the normalised angular distributions \(g_{\nu_e}\),
\(g_{\bar\nu_e}\) and the densities \(n_{\nu_e}\), \(n_{\bar\nu_e}\);
\(\mathcal{I}_l\) are its moments. Status: STANDARD.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{C}(u) \propto \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}(\omega_{\mathrm{f}}x_4 - k_{\mathrm{f}}x_3)},\qquad
 \bar\omega = \omega_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_0,
 \qquad \bar k = k_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_1:\qquad \det\Pi(\bar\omega, \bar k) = 0,\\
&\Pi = \mathrm{diag}(1,\ -1) + \int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2}\,\frac{G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)}{\bar\omega
 - \bar ku}
 \begin{pmatrix}1 & u\\ u & u^2\end{pmatrix}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-117}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-117).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{C}(u)\): The flavour coherence (off-diagonal element of the
  density matrix) of the neutrinos moving in the direction \(u\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u\): The cosine of the angle between a momentum and the symmetry
  axis \(x_3\) of the neutrino gas. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\omega_{\mathrm{f}},\ k_{\mathrm{f}}\): The frequency and the
  wavenumber (along \(x_3\)) of a flavour wave. Unit: GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_3\): The third coordinate of ordinary 3-space (also the symmetry
  axis of the fast-flavour analysis). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\bar\omega,\ \bar k\): The frequency and wavenumber of a flavour
  wave shifted by the moments,
  \(\bar\omega = \omega_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_0\),
  \(\bar k = k_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_1\); a mode with
  \(\mathrm{Im}\,\bar\omega > 0\) grows. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{I}_l\): The \(l\)-th angular moment of the
  electron-lepton-number distribution. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Pi\): The \(2 \times 2\) dispersion matrix of the fast flavour
  instability. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{diag}\): A diagonal matrix with the listed entries on its
  diagonal (in the order of the coordinates \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\)
  when it is a tensor in eight dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\): The angular distribution of the electron
  lepton number of a dense neutrino gas (in units of a potential). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The linearised FE-110 for a small flavour coherence
\(\mathcal{C}(u)\) with the frequency \(\omega_{\mathrm{f}}\) and the
wave number \(k_{\mathrm{f}}\) along the axis (shifted by the moments,
\(\bar\omega\), \(\bar k\)): non-trivial solutions need \(\det\Pi = 0\),
the dispersion relation of fast flavour waves. Status: STANDARD (roots
for real \(k_{\mathrm{f}}\) by vectorised Newton iterations; three
independent evaluations agree to \(1.2\times10^{-14}\)).

\[
\big(1 + \mathcal{J}_0\big)\big(-1 + \mathcal{J}_2\big) - \mathcal{J}_1^2 = 0,\qquad
\mathcal{J}_l = \int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2}\,\frac{G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\,u^l}{\bar\omega - \bar
 ku};\qquad
-1 + \int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2}\,\frac{G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\,(1 - u^2)}{2(\bar\omega - \bar ku)}
 = 0 \tag{FE-118}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-118).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{J}_l\): The \(l\)-th moment of the dispersion integral of
  the fast flavour instability. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(l\): The multipole (angular moment) number \(l = 0, 1, 2, \dots\) of
  a Boltzmann hierarchy or of an angular moment \(\int u^l\cdots\); as a
  coefficient it is the same number. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u\): The cosine of the angle between a momentum and the symmetry
  axis \(x_3\) of the neutrino gas. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\): The angular distribution of the electron
  lepton number of a dense neutrino gas (in units of a potential). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar\omega,\ \bar k\): The frequency and wavenumber of a flavour
  wave shifted by the moments,
  \(\bar\omega = \omega_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_0\),
  \(\bar k = k_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_1\); a mode with
  \(\mathrm{Im}\,\bar\omega > 0\) grows. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The dispersion relation splits into the axisymmetric
branch (the first equation, with the moments \(\mathcal{J}_l\)) and the
azimuthal branch (the second). A root with
\(\mathrm{Im}\,\bar\omega > 0\) for real \(\bar k\) grows exponentially:
the fast flavour instability. Status: STANDARD (with a crossing of the
electron-lepton-number distribution the largest growth rate is
\(0.088\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}}n_{\nu_e}\); without one no mode grows).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathrm{i}\,\frac{\mathrm{d}\mathcal{C}(u)}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = \big(\mathcal{I}_0 -
 u\,\mathcal{I}_1\big)\mathcal{C}(u)
 - \int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tilde u}{2}\,G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(\tilde u)\,(1 - u\tilde
 u)\,\mathcal{C}(\tilde u);\\
&\bar\omega^2 - \big(\mathcal{I}_2 - \mathcal{I}_0\big)\bar\omega - \big(\mathcal{I}_0\mathcal{I}_2 -
 \mathcal{I}_1^2\big) = 0,\qquad
 \mathrm{Im}\,\bar\omega = \frac12\sqrt{4\mathcal{I}_1^2 - \big(\mathcal{I}_0 + \mathcal{I}_2\big)^2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-119}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-119).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{C}(u)\): The flavour coherence (off-diagonal element of the
  density matrix) of the neutrinos moving in the direction \(u\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(u\): The cosine of the angle between a momentum and the symmetry
  axis \(x_3\) of the neutrino gas. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{I}_l\): The \(l\)-th angular moment of the
  electron-lepton-number distribution. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde u\): A second direction cosine (variable of integration).
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{ELN}}(u)\): The angular distribution of the electron
  lepton number of a dense neutrino gas (in units of a potential). Unit:
  GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\bar\omega,\ \bar k\): The frequency and wavenumber of a flavour
  wave shifted by the moments,
  \(\bar\omega = \omega_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_0\),
  \(\bar k = k_{\mathrm{f}} - \mathcal{I}_1\); a mode with
  \(\mathrm{Im}\,\bar\omega > 0\) grows. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{Im}\): The imaginary part of a complex number. Status:
  STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The homogeneous equation for \(\mathcal{C}(u)\) and
the quadratic equation of the mode \(k_{\mathrm{f}} = \mathcal{I}_1\),
whose imaginary part gives the growth rate in closed form. Status:
STANDARD (the eigenvalues of the discretised equation, 300 nodes, agree
with the dispersion relation; near the neutrinosphere the coherence
grows by a factor \(\mathrm{e}\) in \(6\times10^{-10}\) to
\(6\times10^{-11}\) s). Residual of the stored moments, growth rates and
eigenvalues \(3.3\times10^{-13}\).

\hypertarget{the-cosmic-neutrino-background}{%
\subsection{The cosmic neutrino
background}\label{the-cosmic-neutrino-background}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&f_{\mathrm{FD}}(k) = \frac{1}{\mathrm{e}^{k/T_\nu} + 1},\qquad
 n_i = 2\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^3\vec k}{(2\pi)^3}\,f_{\mathrm{FD}} = \frac{3\zeta(3)}{2\pi^2}\,T_\nu^3,\qquad
 \rho_i = 2\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^3\vec k}{(2\pi)^3}\,\sqrt{k^2 + m_i^2}\,f_{\mathrm{FD}},\\
&\bar v_i = \frac{\int_0^\infty k^2\,\frac{k}{\sqrt{k^2 +
 m_i^2}}\,f_{\mathrm{FD}}\,\mathrm{d}k}{\int_0^\infty k^2\,f_{\mathrm{FD}}\,\mathrm{d}k},
 \qquad \text{flavour content of }\nu_i:\ \lvert U_{\alpha i}\rvert^2
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-120}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-120).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(f_{\mathrm{FD}}\): The Fermi-Dirac distribution of the relic
  neutrinos, \(f_{\mathrm{FD}} = 1/(\mathrm{e}^{k/T_\nu} + 1)\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\nu\): The temperature of the relic neutrinos (one common
  temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 1.9454 K today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n,\ n_o,\ n_{\psi i},\ n_f,\ n_i\): A number density: \(n(y)\) the
  particle density of dirac16complex in the Kohn-Sham equations and
  \(n_o\) the contribution of the orbital \(o\), \(n_{\psi i}(y)\) the
  density of the Standard-Model fermion \(\psi_i\) (per proper 7-volume
  of eight dimensions); \(n_f\) the density of the fermion component
  \(f\) and \(n_i\) that of the neutrino mass state \(i\) (per 3-volume
  of four dimensions). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{7}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec k,\ \vec k_o\): The wave vector (momentum) in ordinary 3-space;
  \(\vec k_o\) that of the orbital \(o\), a point of the lattice
  \(k_{\mathrm{lat}}\vec n_o\). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\zeta\): The Riemann zeta function; only its value \(\zeta(3)\)
  occurs, the sum of \(1/n^3\) over the positive integers \(n\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\zeta(3) = 1.2020569\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\bar v_i\): The mean velocity of the relic neutrinos of the mass
  state \(i\). Unit: dimensionless (c = 1). Value: 0.435, 0.0604, 0.0105
  today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu_\alpha,\ \nu_i\): A left-handed neutrino: \(\nu_\alpha\) of the
  flavour \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\) (\(\nu_e\), \(\nu_\mu\),
  \(\nu_\tau\)), \(\nu_i\) of the mass state \(i\) (mass \(m_i\)). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3/2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(U\): The PMNS matrix, the unitary \(3 \times 3\) mixing matrix of
  the leptons, \(\nu_\alpha = \sum_iU_{\alpha i}\nu_i\) (rows
  \(\alpha = e, \mu, \tau\), columns the mass states \(i\)); in UM-2
  \(U = \mathcal{K}_e^\dagger\mathcal{K}_\nu\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: \(\sin^2\theta_{12} = 0.308\), \(\sin^2\theta_{13} = 0.02215\),
  \(\sin^2\theta_{23} = 0.470\), \(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}} = 212^\circ\)
  (NuFIT 6.0). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\alpha\): A flavour index running over \(e, \mu, \tau\) (the flavour
  states of the leptons and neutrinos). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} Today the relic neutrinos are three mass states
(decoherent), each with the Fermi-Dirac distribution of the temperature
\(T_\nu\) (the momenta redshift, so the massless form is kept), the
number density \(n_i\), the energy density \(\rho_i\) with the mass
\(m_i\), and the mean velocity \(\bar v_i\); the flavour content of
\(\nu_i\) is \(\lvert U_{\alpha i}\rvert^2\). Status: STANDARD (exact
integrals): \(T_\nu = 1.9454\) K, 112.0 per cm\(^3\) per mass state,
mean velocities 0.435, 0.0604 and 0.0105 (in units of the speed of
light), and \(\Omega_\nu h_{100}^2 = 6.382\times10^{-4}\) (the value in
the CMB computation agrees to \(1.9\times10^{-5}\)). Residual
\(2.9\times10^{-12}\).

\hypertarget{the-thermal-history}{%
\section{The thermal history}\label{the-thermal-history}}

The hot Universe of UM-2 is the plasma of the Standard-Model zero modes
with their \(x_0\) masses (no Higgs particle), in which the dark matter
freezes out through the portals I12 and I13, the fold bands of
dirac16complex are produced out of equilibrium through I11, and the
right-handed neutrinos are never produced. The Hubble rate is that of
four-dimensional gravity on the stabilised block (FE-47, FE-49).

\hypertarget{the-degrees-of-freedom}{%
\subsection{The degrees of freedom}\label{the-degrees-of-freedom}}

\[
\rho_s = \frac{d_s}{2\pi^2}\int_0^\infty\frac{k^2E\,\mathrm{d}k}{\mathrm{e}^{E/T} \pm 1},\qquad
p_s = \frac{d_s}{6\pi^2}\int_0^\infty\frac{k^4\,\mathrm{d}k}{E\,(\mathrm{e}^{E/T} \pm 1)},\qquad
s_s = \frac{\rho_s + p_s}{T},\qquad E = \sqrt{k^2 + m_s^2} \tag{FE-121}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-121).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s\): The label of a particle species of the plasma or of matter
  (photon, gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), quarks, leptons, nucleons, dark
  matter); a sum over \(s\) runs over the species present. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(d_o,\ d_s\): A degeneracy: \(d_o = 4r_3(\lvert\vec n_o\rvert^2)\)
  the number of states of the momentum shell of the orbital \(o\);
  \(d_s\) the number of internal states (spin, colour, particle and
  antiparticle) of the species \(s\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(k\): The magnitude of the wave vector (the momentum, \(\hbar = 1\))
  of a mode: in the reduction, the Kohn-Sham and the perturbation
  equations the comoving wavenumber (physical momentum
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}k\)); in the thermal integrals the momentum of a
  particle at the time considered (the variable of integration). Unit:
  GeV (1/length). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(p\): A pressure: \(p_M = \mathcal{T}^M{}_M\) (no sum) for the
  direction \(M \ne x_4\), \(p_s\) the pressure of the species \(s\);
  \(\Delta p\) the pressure perturbation in the cosmological
  perturbation equations; in the Feynman-Hellmann relation the pressure
  of a gauge plasma. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight dimensions),
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s,\ s_s\): The entropy density of the plasma,
  \(s = 2\pi^2g_sT^3/45\); \(s_s\) that of the species \(s\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m,\ m_s,\ m_i\): A four-dimensional mass: \(m\) the mass of a mode
  (the eigenvalue of an \(x_0\) problem) or of a fermion; \(m_s\) the
  mass of the species \(s\); \(m_i\) the mass of the neutrino mass state
  \(i\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The energy density, pressure and entropy density of
a species \(s\) in equilibrium at the temperature \(T\): \(d_s\)
internal states, the energy \(E\) with the \(x_0\) mass \(m_s\),
Fermi-Dirac (upper sign) or Bose-Einstein statistics. The species are
the photon, the gluons, \(W^+\), \(W^-\) and \(Z\) (three polarisations
each), the six quarks, the three charged leptons and the three
neutrinos; there is no Higgs particle, and the right-handed neutrinos,
the fold bands, the radion and the Kaluza-Klein modes are not in
equilibrium. Status: STANDARD (exact integrals).

\[
g_\rho = \frac{30}{\pi^2T^4}\sum_s\rho_s,\qquad g_s = \frac{45}{2\pi^2T^3}\sum_s s_s,\qquad
\mathcal{H} = \Big(\frac{8\pi^3g_\rho}{90}\Big)^{1/2}\frac{T^2}{M_{\mathrm{Pl}}} \tag{FE-122}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-122).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g_\rho,\ g_s\): The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
  freedom of the energy density and of the entropy density of the
  plasma. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 105.75 when every species of UM-2
  in equilibrium is relativistic (no Higgs particle), 10.75 near 1 MeV,
  \(g_s = 43/11\) today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s\): The label of a particle species of the plasma or of matter
  (photon, gluons, \(W^\pm\), \(Z\), quarks, leptons, nucleons, dark
  matter); a sum over \(s\) runs over the species present. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): An energy density: in eight dimensions
  \(\rho = -\mathcal{T}^{x_4}{}_{x_4}\) (of the total source, or of the
  species named by a subscript; \(\rho_s\) of the species \(s\),
  \(\rho_i\) of the neutrino mass state \(i\)); in the cosmological
  perturbation equations \(\Delta\rho\) is the density perturbation
  summed over the species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{8}\) (eight
  dimensions), \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (four dimensions). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s,\ s_s\): The entropy density of the plasma,
  \(s = 2\pi^2g_sT^3/45\); \(s_s\) that of the species \(s\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\): The Planck mass,
  \(M_{\mathrm{Pl}} = G_{\mathrm{N}}^{-1/2}\). Unit: GeV. Value:
  \(1.22091\times10^{19}\) GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
freedom for the energy (\(g_\rho\)) and the entropy (\(g_s\)), and the
Hubble rate of the radiation era (the four-dimensional Friedmann
equation with \(\rho = \frac{\pi^2}{30}g_\rho T^4\)). Status: STANDARD
(checked against the exact limits 105.75 above the electroweak scale,
which is the Standard-Model value 106.75 without the Higgs particle,
86.25, 10.75 and \(43/11\)). Residual (adaptive quadrature against the
tabulated values) \(5.0\times10^{-9}\); the neglected order-\(\alpha\)
interaction pressure of the plasma is \(-5.4\) \% of the ideal-gas
pressure at the dark-matter freeze-out (QCD \(-4.7\) \%, SU(2) \(-0.7\)
\%, U(1) \(-0.1\) \%).

\[
w_{\mathrm{QGP}}(T) = \frac12\Big[1 + \tanh\frac{T - T_{\mathrm{c}}}{T_{\mathrm{w}}}\Big],\qquad
\frac{T_\nu}{T} = \Big(\frac{g_{s,e\gamma}(T)}{11/2}\Big)^{1/3},\qquad
\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln T}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = -\frac{\mathcal{H}}{1 + \frac13\,\mathrm{d}\ln g_s/\mathrm{d}\ln
 T} \tag{FE-123}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-123).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(w_{\mathrm{QGP}}(T)\): The weight of the quark-gluon plasma in the
  model of the QCD crossover (1 above, 0 below). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_{\mathrm{c}},\ T_{\mathrm{w}}\): The temperature and the width of
  the QCD crossover. Unit: GeV. Value: 155 MeV and 15 MeV. Status:
  ASSUMED.
\item
  \(T_\nu\): The temperature of the relic neutrinos (one common
  temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 1.9454 K today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_{s,e\gamma}\): The entropy degrees of freedom of the electrons,
  positrons and photons only (after neutrino decoupling). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(11/2\) before and 2 after the annihilation of
  \(e^\pm\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(g_\rho,\ g_s\): The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
  freedom of the energy density and of the entropy density of the
  plasma. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 105.75 when every species of UM-2
  in equilibrium is relativistic (no Higgs particle), 10.75 near 1 MeV,
  \(g_s = 43/11\) today. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The QCD crossover as a smooth switch
\(w_{\mathrm{QGP}}\) between the quark-gluon plasma and a gas of the
twelve lightest hadron multiplets at \(T_{\mathrm{c}} = 155\) MeV with
the width \(T_{\mathrm{w}} = 15\) MeV; the neutrino temperature after an
instantaneous decoupling at 2 MeV from the entropy \(g_{s,e\gamma}\) of
the electron-photon plasma; and the cooling law of the plasma from the
conservation of entropy. Status: ASSUMED (the model of the crossover and
the instantaneous decoupling; the radiation density after decoupling
differs from that of the CMB computation by \(5.9\times10^{-3}\), which
enters only the entropy today).

\hypertarget{the-dark-matter-relic}{%
\subsection{The dark-matter relic}\label{the-dark-matter-relic}}

The dark matter is two Dirac species (the lowest odd levels of
\(\Psi_A\) and \(\Psi_B\)), each with its antiparticle. For one species
with \(n_{\mathrm{DM}} = \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\), the abundance per
entropy and the variable \(x = M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T\) obey (entropy
conservation, FE-123)

\[
\frac{\mathrm{d}Y_{\mathrm{DM}}}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = -\frac{s\,\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle}{2}\big(Y_{\mathrm{DM}}^2 - Y_{\mathrm{eq}}^2\big),\qquad
\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{\mathrm{d}x_4} = \frac{x\,\mathcal{H}}{1 + \frac13\,\mathrm{d}\ln g_s/\mathrm{d}\ln T},
\]

\textbf{Symbols in this equation.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Y_{\mathrm{DM}},\ Y_{\mathrm{eq}},\ Y_\infty\): The abundance
  (number density per entropy density) of one dark-matter species
  (particles and antiparticles), its equilibrium value and its final
  value. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(s,\ s_s\): The entropy density of the plasma,
  \(s = 2\pi^2g_sT^3/45\); \(s_s\) that of the species \(s\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): A cross-section (of the dark-matter annihilation or
  production process named in the equation; summed over the spins where
  marked). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(v_{\mathrm{rel}}\): The relative velocity of the two annihilating
  particles; \(\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle\) the thermal
  average. Unit: dimensionless (c = 1). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x\): The ratio \(x = M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T\) of the dark-matter mass to
  the temperature. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(x_{\mathrm{f}} = 31.23\) at the freeze-out. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(g_\rho,\ g_s\): The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
  freedom of the energy density and of the entropy density of the
  plasma. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 105.75 when every species of UM-2
  in equilibrium is relativistic (no Higgs particle), 10.75 near 1 MeV,
  \(g_s = 43/11\) today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

which combine to the second form of:

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{\mathrm{d}n_{\mathrm{DM}}}{\mathrm{d}x_4} + 3\mathcal{H}n_{\mathrm{DM}}
 = -\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle\big(n_{\mathrm{DM}}\bar n_{\mathrm{DM}} -
 n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}}^2\big)\ \Rightarrow
 \frac{\mathrm{d}Y_{\mathrm{DM}}}{\mathrm{d}x} = -\Big(1 + \frac13\frac{\mathrm{d}\ln g_s}{\mathrm{d}\ln
 T}\Big)
 \frac{s\,\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle}{2x\mathcal{H}}\big(Y_{\mathrm{DM}}^2 -
 Y_{\mathrm{eq}}^2\big),\\
&Y_{\mathrm{DM}} = \frac{n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}}{s},\qquad x =
 \frac{M_{\mathrm{DM}}}{T},\qquad
 s = \frac{2\pi^2}{45}\,g_sT^3,\qquad Y_{\mathrm{eq}} = \frac{4M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2TK_2(x)}{2\pi^2s}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-124}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-124).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM}},\ \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The number densities of
  dark-matter particles and antiparticles of one species. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\sigma\): A cross-section (of the dark-matter annihilation or
  production process named in the equation; summed over the spins where
  marked). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(v_{\mathrm{rel}}\): The relative velocity of the two annihilating
  particles; \(\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle\) the thermal
  average. Unit: dimensionless (c = 1). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}},\ \bar n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}}\): The equilibrium
  number densities of dark-matter particles and antiparticles of one
  species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(Y_{\mathrm{DM}},\ Y_{\mathrm{eq}},\ Y_\infty\): The abundance
  (number density per entropy density) of one dark-matter species
  (particles and antiparticles), its equilibrium value and its final
  value. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x\): The ratio \(x = M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T\) of the dark-matter mass to
  the temperature. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(x_{\mathrm{f}} = 31.23\) at the freeze-out. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(g_\rho,\ g_s\): The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
  freedom of the energy density and of the entropy density of the
  plasma. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 105.75 when every species of UM-2
  in equilibrium is relativistic (no Higgs particle), 10.75 near 1 MeV,
  \(g_s = 43/11\) today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s,\ s_s\): The entropy density of the plasma,
  \(s = 2\pi^2g_sT^3/45\); \(s_s\) that of the species \(s\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(K_1,\ K_2\): The modified Bessel functions of the second kind of
  orders 1 and 2 (not related to the block variable \(K\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The number density of the dark matter changes by the
expansion (\(3\mathcal{H}n_{\mathrm{DM}}\)) and by annihilation and
inverse annihilation with the thermally averaged cross-section times
relative velocity \(\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle\);
\(Y_{\mathrm{DM}}\) is the abundance per entropy density \(s\)
(particles and antiparticles), \(Y_{\mathrm{eq}}\) its equilibrium value
(Bessel function \(K_2\)). Status: STANDARD (integrated implicitly,
Radau, \(\ln Y_{\mathrm{DM}}\) from \(x = 5\) to 2000 with relative
tolerance \(10^{-10}\), with the degrees of freedom of UM-2; each of the
two species freezes out separately). Residual \(6.1\times10^{-9}\) (the
relic condition re-solved and the late-time integral identity); the
leading neglected effect is the missing order-\(\alpha\) pressure of the
plasma, which would shift \(\Omega h^2\) by about \(+2.7\) \%
(ESTIMATE).

\[
\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle =
 \frac{1}{8M_{\mathrm{DM}}^4TK_2(x)^2}\int_{4M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2}^{\infty}\sigma\,
 \big(E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2 - 4M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2\big)\,E_{\mathrm{cm}}\,K_1\Big(\frac{E_{\mathrm{cm}}}{T}\Big)\,
 \mathrm{d}\big(E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2\big) \tag{FE-125}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-125).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\sigma\): A cross-section (of the dark-matter annihilation or
  production process named in the equation; summed over the spins where
  marked). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(v_{\mathrm{rel}}\): The relative velocity of the two annihilating
  particles; \(\langle\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}}\rangle\) the thermal
  average. Unit: dimensionless (c = 1). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(K_1,\ K_2\): The modified Bessel functions of the second kind of
  orders 1 and 2 (not related to the block variable \(K\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x\): The ratio \(x = M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T\) of the dark-matter mass to
  the temperature. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(x_{\mathrm{f}} = 31.23\) at the freeze-out. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The total energy of the colliding pair in its
  centre-of-mass frame. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The relativistic thermal average over the
centre-of-mass energy \(E_{\mathrm{cm}}\) of the pair, with the Bessel
functions \(K_1\), \(K_2\) of the Maxwell-Boltzmann distributions.
Status: STANDARD (Gondolo and Gelmini; adaptive quadrature in the
kinetic energy of the pair with exponentially scaled Bessel functions).

\[
\Omega_{\mathrm{DM}}h_{100}^2 = \frac{M_{\mathrm{DM}}\,Y_\infty\,s_0}{\rho_{\mathrm{crit}}/h_{100}^2},\qquad
\frac{\rho_{\mathrm{crit}}}{h_{100}^2} = 1.05368\times10^{-5}\ \text{GeV}/\text{cm}^3 \tag{FE-126}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-126).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Omega_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The dark-matter density today in units of
  the critical density. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\Omega_{\mathrm{DM}}h_{100}^2 = 0.120\) (both species). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(h_{100}\): The reduced Hubble constant, \(\mathcal{H}_0/(100\) km
  s\(^{-1}\) Mpc\(^{-1})\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.6738. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(Y_{\mathrm{DM}},\ Y_{\mathrm{eq}},\ Y_\infty\): The abundance
  (number density per entropy density) of one dark-matter species
  (particles and antiparticles), its equilibrium value and its final
  value. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(s_0\): The entropy density today. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\) (quoted
  per \(\mathrm{cm}^{3}\)). Value: 2891.3 cm\(^{-3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{crit}}\): The critical density,
  \(3\mathcal{H}_0^2M_{\mathrm{Pl}}^2/(8\pi)\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\) (quoted in GeV/\(\mathrm{cm}^{3}\)). Value:
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{crit}}/h_{100}^2 = 1.05368\times10^{-5}\)
  GeV/cm\(^3\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The relic density from the final abundance
\(Y_\infty\), the entropy density today \(s_0\) and the critical
density. Status: STANDARD. Each species gives 0.060 (0.120 together, the
measured cold dark matter, which is the input of the CMB computation;
the two agree to \(3.1\times10^{-9}\)) for
\(G_{\mathrm{A}} = 1.278\times10^{-9}\) GeV\(^{-2}\), with the
freeze-out at \(x = 31.23\) (\(T = 1.42\) TeV).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\sigma_{\mathrm{I12}} = \frac{G_{\mathrm{I12}}^2\,E_{\mathrm{cm}}^3\big(E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2 -
 4M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2\big)^{1/2}}{8\pi},\qquad
 G_{\mathrm{I12}}^2 = 8G_{\mathrm{g}}^2 + 3G_{\mathrm{W}}^2 + G_{\mathrm{B}}^2,\\
&\sigma_{\mathrm{I13}} = \sum_f\frac{N_{\mathrm{c}}\,G_f^2\,E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2\big(1 +
 2M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2/E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2\big)}{24\pi\beta_{\mathrm{cm}}},
 \qquad G_f = G_{\mathrm{A}}\,q^{B}_f\,\tilde I^{(b)}_{of},\\
&\beta_{\mathrm{cm}} = \sqrt{1 - \frac{4M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2}{E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2}},\qquad
 k_{\mathrm{cm}} = \sqrt{\frac{E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2}{4} - M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-127}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-127).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{I12}},\ \sigma_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The spin-averaged
  annihilation cross-sections of a dark-matter pair through I12 (into
  gauge-boson pairs) and through I13 (into quark pairs). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{I12}}\): The combined coupling of I12 to all gauge-boson
  pairs,
  \(G_{\mathrm{I12}}^2 = 8G_{\mathrm{g}}^2 + 3G_{\mathrm{W}}^2 + G_{\mathrm{B}}^2\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\). Value: benchmark
  \(\pi/(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}^3) = 1.801\times10^{-14}\) GeV\(^{-3}\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(E_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The total energy of the colliding pair in its
  centre-of-mass frame. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{g}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised gluon pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(6.09\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\) (benchmark). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{W}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised SU(2) gauge pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(2.96\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{B}}\): The coupling of a dark-matter pair to canonically
  normalised hypercharge pairs through I12. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-3}\).
  Value: \(1.08\times10^{-15}\) GeV\(^{-3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\): The number of Weyl components of a fermion
  multiplet that share its zero-mode profile and overlap (colour times
  isospin): 6 for a quark doublet, 3 for a quark singlet, 2 for a lepton
  doublet, 1 for a charged-lepton singlet. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_f\): The I13 coupling of the dark matter to the quark component
  \(f\), \(G_f = G_{\mathrm{A}}q^B_f\tilde I^{(b)}_{of}\). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\beta_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The velocity of each particle of the pair in
  the centre-of-mass frame. Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}}\): The four-dimensional strength of the portal I13 on
  the zero modes,
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = c_{\mathrm{A}}H/(2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}\Lambda_\star^6)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(1.278\times10^{-9}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter relic density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(q^B_\psi,\ q^B_f\): The baryon number of the multiplet \(\psi\) or
  of the fermion component \(f\) (\(\frac13\) for quarks, 0 for
  leptons). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{of}\): The overlap of the lower component of the
  state \(o\) with the zero mode of \(f\),
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{of} = H^{-1}\int_{-L}^{0}b_o^2\varrho_f\mathrm{e}^{-3Hy}\mathrm{d}y\)
  (the overlap of I13); \(\tilde I^{(b)}_Q\), \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{u_R}\),
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{d_R}\), \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{\nu_R}\) those of the dark
  matter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: dark matter:
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_Q = 2.6135\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(o\): The label of a Kohn-Sham orbital (a state of dirac16complex in
  the cell: block, momentum shell, fold parity and level); a sum over
  \(o\) runs over the orbitals. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(k_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The momentum of each particle of the pair in the
  centre-of-mass frame. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The two annihilation channels: through I12 into
pairs of gauge bosons (eight gluons, three \(W\), one \(B\); a p-wave
from the state \(^3P_0\), so
\(\sigma v_{\mathrm{rel}} \propto v_{\mathrm{rel}}^2\) at low velocity),
and through I13 into quark-antiquark pairs of the multiplets \(f\), each
with \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components (colour times isospin; an s-wave
from \(^3S_1\)), with the couplings \(G_f\) of FE-70 (\(o\) the
dark-matter orbital; the sum of
\(N_{\mathrm{c}}(q^B_f\tilde I^{(b)}_{of})^2\) over the multiplets is
\(\mathcal{S}_B = 16.351\)); \(\beta_{\mathrm{cm}}\) and
\(k_{\mathrm{cm}}\) are the velocity and the momentum of the dark matter
in the centre-of-mass frame. Status: DERIVED (spin averaged; the
polarisation sum and the spin trace checked numerically); I12 at the
unitarity-safe benchmark
\(G_{\mathrm{I12}} = \pi/(2M_{\mathrm{DM}}^3) = 1.801\times10^{-14}\)
GeV\(^{-3}\), which alone would leave far too much dark matter, so I13
sets the relic.

\[
\sigma_{\mathrm{I12}} \le \frac{\pi}{4k_{\mathrm{cm}}^2}\ \ \text{(total angular momentum 0)},\qquad
\sigma_{\mathrm{I13}} \le \frac{3\pi}{4k_{\mathrm{cm}}^2}\ \ \text{(total angular momentum 1)} \tag{FE-128}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-128).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\sigma_{\mathrm{I12}},\ \sigma_{\mathrm{I13}}\): The spin-averaged
  annihilation cross-sections of a dark-matter pair through I12 (into
  gauge-boson pairs) and through I13 (into quark pairs). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(k_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The momentum of each particle of the pair in the
  centre-of-mass frame. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The partial-wave unitarity bounds of the two
channels (total angular momentum 0 for I12, 1 for I13). Where an
effective cross-section exceeds its bound it is replaced by the bound.
Status: DERIVED (I13 reaches its bound only above
\(v_{\mathrm{rel}} = 0.51\), which gives 22 \% of the freeze-out rate;
I12 alone would need a strongly coupled \(^3P_0\) channel).

\hypertarget{the-fold-bands}{%
\subsection{The fold bands}\label{the-fold-bands}}

\[
\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}} =
 \frac{T}{32\pi^4}\int_0^\infty\mathrm{d}\big(E_{\mathrm{cm}}^2\big)\Big(\sum_{\text{spins}}\sigma
 \Big)E_{\mathrm{cm}}^3
 K_1\Big(\frac{E_{\mathrm{cm}}}{T}\Big) =
 \frac{4}{\pi^5}\,N_{\mathrm{fold}}\,G_{\mathrm{V}}^2\,T^8\sum_fN_{\mathrm{c}}\big(q_f\tilde I_{of}\big)^2,
\qquad \frac{\mathrm{d}\big(\rho_{\mathrm{fold}}\,\mathrm{e}^{4a_4}\big)}{\mathrm{d}x_4} =
 8T\,\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}\,\mathrm{e}^{4a_4} \tag{FE-129}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-129).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}\): The rate per volume of the production of
  fold-band pairs from the plasma through I11 (freeze-in). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\infty\): Infinity: as a limit of an integral or a sum it means that
  the range has no upper bound. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E_{\mathrm{cm}}\): The total energy of the colliding pair in its
  centre-of-mass frame. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): A cross-section (of the dark-matter annihilation or
  production process named in the equation; summed over the spins where
  marked). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(K_1,\ K_2\): The modified Bessel functions of the second kind of
  orders 1 and 2 (not related to the block variable \(K\)). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{fold}}\): The number of fold bands: the massless fold
  zero modes of \(\Psi_A\) and of \(\Psi_B\) (two Dirac fermions). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: 2. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}}\): The four-dimensional strength of the vector portal
  I11 on the zero modes,
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = c_{\mathrm{V}}H/(2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}\Lambda_\star^6)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: benchmark \(4.38\times10^{-17}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\) (\(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = 0.001\)). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\): The number of Weyl components of a fermion
  multiplet that share its zero-mode profile and overlap (colour times
  isospin): 6 for a quark doublet, 3 for a quark singlet, 2 for a lepton
  doublet, 1 for a charged-lepton singlet. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde I_{of}\): The overlap of the state \(o\) of dirac16complex
  with the zero mode of the fermion component \(f\),
  \(\tilde I_{of} = H^{-1}\int_{-L}^{0}(a_o^2 + b_o^2)\varrho_f\mathrm{e}^{-3Hy}\mathrm{d}y\)
  (the integral in units of \(H\)); \(\tilde I_Q\), \(\tilde I_{u_R}\),
  \(\tilde I_{d_R}\), \(\tilde I_\ell\), \(\tilde I_{e_R}\) those of the
  dark matter with \(Q\), \(u_R\), \(d_R\), \(\ell\), \(e_R\),
  \(\tilde I_{\nu_R,i}\) with the right-handed neutrino of the
  generation \(i\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: dark matter:
  \(\tilde I_Q = 8.2722\), \(\tilde I_\ell = 1.2524\),
  \(\tilde I_{u_R} = 0.3854\), \(\tilde I_{d_R} = 0.3961\),
  \(\tilde I_{e_R} = 0.3972\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(o\): The label of a Kohn-Sham orbital (a state of dirac16complex in
  the cell: block, momentum shell, fold parity and level); a sum over
  \(o\) runs over the orbitals. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{fold}}\): The energy density of the fold bands. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: the scale factor of cosmology is
  \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The massless fold bands of dirac16complex
(\(N_{\mathrm{fold}}\) species, two Dirac fermions) are produced from
pairs of Standard-Model fermions through I11: \(\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}\)
is the production rate per volume (Maxwell-Boltzmann statistics,
massless particles; \(\sigma\) the cross-section summed over spins,
\(K_1\) the Bessel function), proportional to \(G_{\mathrm{V}}^2T^8\)
and to the sum over the fifteen multiplets of three generations of
\(N_{\mathrm{c}}(q_f\tilde I_{of})^2\) (\(o\) the fold band; 126.85);
each production puts the energy \(8T\) on average into the fold bands,
whose comoving energy density \(\rho_{\mathrm{fold}}\mathrm{e}^{4a_4}\)
grows accordingly. Status: DERIVED (integrated from the highest
temperature 10 TeV, ASSUMED):
\(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = 5.21\times10^{29}\,G_{\mathrm{V}}^2\)
GeV\(^4\), so the benchmark \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = 4.38\times10^{-17}\)
GeV\(^{-2}\) gives 0.001. Residual (re-integration with Gauss-Legendre
panels) \(3.9\times10^{-5}\).

\[
\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = \frac{\rho_{\mathrm{fold}}}{\rho_\nu},\qquad \rho_\nu =
 \frac78\,\frac{\pi^2}{15}\,T_\nu^4,\qquad
\frac{2\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}}{n_{\mathrm{fold,eq}}\,\mathcal{H}} \ll 1,\qquad
n_{\mathrm{fold,eq}} = N_{\mathrm{fold}}\cdot4\cdot\frac34\,\frac{\zeta(3)}{\pi^2}\,T^3 \tag{FE-130}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-130).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\): The effective number of neutrino species (the
  radiation beyond the photons in units of one neutrino species with
  \(T_\nu/T_\gamma = (4/11)^{1/3}\)); \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an
  addition to it. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.0432 (neutrinos) +
  0.0010 (fold bands). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho_{\mathrm{fold}}\): The energy density of the fold bands. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\rho_\nu,\ \rho_{\nu_\alpha}\): The energy density of one neutrino
  flavour (neutrinos and antineutrinos) at the common temperature
  \(T_\nu\), \(\frac78\frac{\pi^2}{15}T_\nu^4\) when massless;
  \(\rho_{\nu_\alpha}\) that of the flavour \(\alpha\) at its
  temperature \(T_{\nu_\alpha}\). Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\nu\): The temperature of the relic neutrinos (one common
  temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 1.9454 K today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}\): The rate per volume of the production of
  fold-band pairs from the plasma through I11 (freeze-in). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{fold,eq}}\): The number density the fold bands would have
  in thermal equilibrium. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{fold}}\): The number of fold bands: the massless fold
  zero modes of \(\Psi_A\) and of \(\Psi_B\) (two Dirac fermions). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: 2. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\zeta\): The Riemann zeta function; only its value \(\zeta(3)\)
  occurs, the sum of \(1/n^3\) over the positive integers \(n\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\zeta(3) = 1.2020569\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The extra radiation \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) is
the energy density of the fold bands in units of one neutrino species,
and the condition for the production to stay far from equilibrium: the
production rate per fold quantum,
\(2\Gamma_{\mathrm{fi}}/n_{\mathrm{fold,eq}}\), compared with the Hubble
rate (\(n_{\mathrm{fold,eq}}\) the equilibrium density of the fold
bands). Status: DERIVED (the ratio is 0.012 at the highest temperature
10 TeV and falls afterwards: the fold bands are never in equilibrium).

\hypertarget{the-right-handed-neutrinos}{%
\subsection{The right-handed
neutrinos}\label{the-right-handed-neutrinos}}

The right-handed neutrinos are gauge singlets and carry \(B - L\) charge
\(-1\); the portals could produce them from the dark matter. The
baryon-number current of I13 is the choice that prevents it; the
following equations show the counterfactual and the actual rates.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&G_{\mathrm{A}}^{B-L} = G_{\mathrm{A}}\Big[\frac{\mathcal{S}_B}{\mathcal{S}_{B-L}}\Big]^{1/2},\qquad
 \mathcal{S}_B = \sum_fN_{\mathrm{c}}\big(q^{B}_f\tilde I^{(b)}_{of}\big)^2,\qquad
 \mathcal{S}_{B-L} = \sum_fN_{\mathrm{c}}\big(q_f\tilde I^{(b)}_{of}\big)^2,\\
&\frac{\mathrm{d}n_{\nu_R}}{\mathrm{d}x_4} + 3\mathcal{H}n_{\nu_R} = \Gamma_\nu\Big(1 -
 \frac{n_{\nu_R}^2}{(n_{\nu_R}^{\mathrm{eq}})^2}\Big),\qquad
 \Gamma_\nu = 2\,n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}}\,\bar n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}}\,\frac{G_\nu^2M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2}{2\pi},\qquad
 G_\nu = G_{\mathrm{A}}^{B-L}\,\tilde I^{(b)}_{\nu_R},\\
&n_{\nu_R}^{\mathrm{eq}} = \frac34\,\frac{\zeta(3)}{\pi^2}\,T^3,\qquad
 n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}} = \bar n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}} = 2\Big(\frac{M_{\mathrm{DM}}T}{2\pi}\Big)^{3/2}\mathrm{e}^{-x}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-131}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-131).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}}^{B-L}\): The strength I13 would need if it coupled to
  the \(B - L\) current (the same relic density). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}}\): The four-dimensional strength of the portal I13 on
  the zero modes,
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = c_{\mathrm{A}}H/(2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}\Lambda_\star^6)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(1.278\times10^{-9}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter relic density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{S}_B,\ \mathcal{S}_{B-L}\): The colour-weighted sums of the
  squared I13 overlaps of the dark matter with the baryon-number and
  with the \(B - L\) charges. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathcal{S}_B = 16.351\), \(\mathcal{S}_{B-L} = 22.92\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a fermion species: a Standard-Model multiplet of
  one generation (its \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\) Weyl components share one
  zero-mode profile; the zero mode of each chirality) or a fermion of a
  medium (electron, proton, neutron); a sum over \(f\) runs over these
  species. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{c}}\): The number of Weyl components of a fermion
  multiplet that share its zero-mode profile and overlap (colour times
  isospin): 6 for a quark doublet, 3 for a quark singlet, 2 for a lepton
  doublet, 1 for a charged-lepton singlet. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(q^B_\psi,\ q^B_f\): The baryon number of the multiplet \(\psi\) or
  of the fermion component \(f\) (\(\frac13\) for quarks, 0 for
  leptons). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{of}\): The overlap of the lower component of the
  state \(o\) with the zero mode of \(f\),
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{of} = H^{-1}\int_{-L}^{0}b_o^2\varrho_f\mathrm{e}^{-3Hy}\mathrm{d}y\)
  (the overlap of I13); \(\tilde I^{(b)}_Q\), \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{u_R}\),
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{d_R}\), \(\tilde I^{(b)}_{\nu_R}\) those of the dark
  matter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: dark matter:
  \(\tilde I^{(b)}_Q = 2.6135\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(o\): The label of a Kohn-Sham orbital (a state of dirac16complex in
  the cell: block, momentum shell, fold parity and level); a sum over
  \(o\) runs over the orbitals. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(q_\psi,\ q_f\): The \(B - L\) charge: \(q_\psi\) of the multiplet
  \(\psi\) (\(q_Q = q_{u_R} = q_{d_R} = \frac13\),
  \(q_\ell = q_{e_R} = q_{\nu_R} = -1\)), \(q_f\) of the fermion
  component \(f\). Unit: dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(n_{\nu_R},\ n_{\nu_R}^{\mathrm{eq}}\): The number density of the
  right-handed neutrinos (three generations) and its equilibrium value.
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; dots denote \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit:
  length (= time, c = 1). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\Gamma_\nu\): The production rate per volume of right-handed
  neutrinos in the counterfactual \(B - L\) variant of I13. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}},\ \bar n_{\mathrm{DM,eq}}\): The equilibrium
  number densities of dark-matter particles and antiparticles of one
  species. Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_\nu\): The four-dimensional coupling of the dark matter to a
  right-handed neutrino in the \(B - L\) variant of I13. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(M_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The mass of a dark-matter particle (the lowest
  odd level of dirac16complex). Unit: GeV. Value: 44.35 TeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\zeta\): The Riemann zeta function; only its value \(\zeta(3)\)
  occurs, the sum of \(1/n^3\) over the positive integers \(n\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(\zeta(3) = 1.2020569\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x\): The ratio \(x = M_{\mathrm{DM}}/T\) of the dark-matter mass to
  the temperature. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(x_{\mathrm{f}} = 31.23\) at the freeze-out. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} If I13 coupled to the \(B - L\) current, its
coupling \(G_{\mathrm{A}}^{B-L}\) would be fixed by the same relic
density through the sums \(\mathcal{S}_B\) and
\(\mathcal{S}_{B-L} = 22.92\) (the latter including the leptons), and
the dark matter in equilibrium would produce right-handed neutrinos at
the rate \(\Gamma_\nu\) (both species, \(G_\nu\) the coupling with the
overlap of the right-handed neutrino) towards their equilibrium density
\(n_{\nu_R}^{\mathrm{eq}}\). Status: DERIVED (the three \(\nu_R\) would
reach 0.9997 of equilibrium by \(x = 40\) and add
\(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = 0.144\), excluded by the CMB: this is why
I13 couples to the baryon number). Residual \(2.5\times10^{-12}\).

\[
\Gamma_{\mathrm{el}} = 2\big(n_{\mathrm{DM}} + \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\big)\,\sigma_{\mathrm{el}} >
 \mathcal{H},\qquad
\sigma_{\mathrm{el}} = \frac{G_\nu^2E^2}{2\pi},\qquad E \simeq 3T \tag{FE-132}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-132).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Gamma_{\mathrm{el}},\ \sigma_{\mathrm{el}}\): The rate of elastic
  scattering of a right-handed neutrino on the dark matter, and its
  cross-section. Unit: GeV; \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: ESTIMATE.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{DM}},\ \bar n_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The number densities of
  dark-matter particles and antiparticles of one species. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{3}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{H}\): The expansion rate of ordinary 3-space,
  \(\mathcal{H} = \dot a_4\) (the Hubble rate of the observer; in the
  hot Universe
  \(\mathcal{H} = (8\pi^3g_\rho/90)^{1/2}T^2/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}\)). Unit:
  GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_\nu\): The four-dimensional coupling of the dark matter to a
  right-handed neutrino in the \(B - L\) variant of I13. Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(E\): The energy of a particle: of the neutrino in the flavour
  equations, \(E = (k^2 + m^2)^{1/2}\) of a particle of momentum \(k\)
  in the thermal integrals. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(T\): The temperature of the plasma (of the photons and of every
  species in equilibrium with them); in the Mermin occupations the
  temperature of the Kohn-Sham state. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The elastic scattering of the right-handed neutrinos
on the dark matter, which keeps them in kinetic equilibrium while its
rate exceeds the Hubble rate (\(E \simeq 3T\) the neutrino energy).
Status: ESTIMATE (for a Weyl \(\nu_R\) on a heavy Dirac fermion; kinetic
decoupling at \(x = 24.4\); a factor 2 in the rate moves it to 23.8 or
25.1).

\[
\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = N_{\mathrm{W}}\Big(\frac{10.75}{g_s}\Big)^{4/3}F_{\mathrm{h}},\qquad
F_{\mathrm{h}} = \Big[\frac{T_\nu}{T_\gamma}\Big(\frac{11}{4}\Big)^{1/3}\Big/\big(1 + h_\nu\big)\Big]^4
 = 1.0095 \tag{FE-133}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-133).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Delta\): The change (difference or small perturbation) of the
  quantity that follows: \(\Delta K\), \(\Delta\rho\), \(\Delta p_3\)
  are first-order changes about the static block,
  \(\Delta\varepsilon_o\) a level shift,
  \(\Delta m^2_{21} = m_2^2 - m_1^2\) a difference of squared masses,
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an addition to \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\),
  \(\Delta S_{\mathrm{m}}\) the change of an action under a change
  \(\Delta g^{MN}\) of the metric. In an uncertainty relation it is the
  spread, the root-mean-square deviation from the mean value in a state:
  \(\Delta x_0\), \(\Delta k_{x_0}\), \(\Delta\xi\), \(\Delta(fy)\) (as
  in the author's \(\Delta x_0\,\Delta p_{x_0} \ge \hbar/2\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{eff}}\): The effective number of neutrino species (the
  radiation beyond the photons in units of one neutrino species with
  \(T_\nu/T_\gamma = (4/11)^{1/3}\)); \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}}\) an
  addition to it. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.0432 (neutrinos) +
  0.0010 (fold bands). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N_{\mathrm{W}}\): The number of Weyl species that decouple early.
  Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(g_\rho,\ g_s\): The effective numbers of relativistic degrees of
  freedom of the energy density and of the entropy density of the
  plasma. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 105.75 when every species of UM-2
  in equilibrium is relativistic (no Higgs particle), 10.75 near 1 MeV,
  \(g_s = 43/11\) today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(F_{\mathrm{h}}\): The correction factor of the early-decoupled
  radiation for the heating of the neutrinos. Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 1.0095. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(T_\nu\): The temperature of the relic neutrinos (one common
  temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 1.9454 K today. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(T_\gamma\): The temperature of the photons (equal to the plasma
  temperature \(T\); the subscript is written where the neutrinos have
  their own temperature). Unit: GeV. Value: 2.7255 K today. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(h_\nu\): The relative heating of the neutrinos by the
  electron-positron annihilation (beyond instantaneous decoupling).
  Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0.00165. Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} The extra radiation of \(N_{\mathrm{W}}\) Weyl
species that decouple when the entropy degrees of freedom are \(g_s\),
diluted by the later annihilations; \(F_{\mathrm{h}}\) corrects for the
non-instantaneous neutrino decoupling (\(T_\nu/T_\gamma = 0.71664\) and
the heating \(h_\nu = 0.00165\) of the common-temperature decoupling,
FE-115). Status: DERIVED (\(F_{\mathrm{h}} = 1.0095\); used for the
\(B - L\) variant, 0.144, and for the self-interaction channel).

\[
r_{\nu_R} = \frac{G_{\mathrm{V}}^2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\tilde I_{\nu_R,i}^2}{G_{\mathrm{A}}^2\,\mathcal{S}_B}
 \tag{FE-134}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-134).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(r_{\nu_R}\): The ratio of the I11 production of right-handed
  neutrinos to the I13 annihilation rate of the dark matter. Unit:
  dimensionless. Value: \(2.0\times10^{-17}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}}\): The four-dimensional strength of the vector portal
  I11 on the zero modes,
  \(G_{\mathrm{V}} = c_{\mathrm{V}}H/(2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}\Lambda_\star^6)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: benchmark \(4.38\times10^{-17}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\) (\(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} = 0.001\)). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(i\): The generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); a repeated index is not
  summed unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\tilde I_{of}\): The overlap of the state \(o\) of dirac16complex
  with the zero mode of the fermion component \(f\),
  \(\tilde I_{of} = H^{-1}\int_{-L}^{0}(a_o^2 + b_o^2)\varrho_f\mathrm{e}^{-3Hy}\mathrm{d}y\)
  (the integral in units of \(H\)); \(\tilde I_Q\), \(\tilde I_{u_R}\),
  \(\tilde I_{d_R}\), \(\tilde I_\ell\), \(\tilde I_{e_R}\) those of the
  dark matter with \(Q\), \(u_R\), \(d_R\), \(\ell\), \(e_R\),
  \(\tilde I_{\nu_R,i}\) with the right-handed neutrino of the
  generation \(i\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: dark matter:
  \(\tilde I_Q = 8.2722\), \(\tilde I_\ell = 1.2524\),
  \(\tilde I_{u_R} = 0.3854\), \(\tilde I_{d_R} = 0.3961\),
  \(\tilde I_{e_R} = 0.3972\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}}\): The four-dimensional strength of the portal I13 on
  the zero modes,
  \(G_{\mathrm{A}} = c_{\mathrm{A}}H/(2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}\Lambda_\star^6)\).
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}\). Value: \(1.278\times10^{-9}\)
  GeV\(^{-2}\) (dark-matter relic density). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{S}_B,\ \mathcal{S}_{B-L}\): The colour-weighted sums of the
  squared I13 overlaps of the dark matter with the baryon-number and
  with the \(B - L\) charges. Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathcal{S}_B = 16.351\), \(\mathcal{S}_{B-L} = 22.92\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{The terms.} With the baryon current in I13 only I11 can produce
right-handed neutrinos; \(r_{\nu_R}\) is the ratio of that production to
the annihilation rate that sets the relic, with the overlaps
\(\tilde I_{\nu_R,i}\) of the three right-handed neutrinos. Status:
DERIVED (\(r_{\nu_R} = 2.0\times10^{-17}\): the right-handed neutrinos
are never thermalised).

\hypertarget{the-self-interaction-channel}{%
\subsection{The self-interaction
channel}\label{the-self-interaction-channel}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathcal{L}_4^{\lambda} =
 -\lambda\,\big(\bar\Psi_{\mathrm{DM}}\Psi_{\mathrm{fold}}\big)\big(\bar\Psi_{\mathrm{fold}}\Psi_{
 \mathrm{DM}}\big)
 \big(n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DF}}\big)^2,\qquad n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DF}} =
 a_{\mathrm{DM}}b_{\mathrm{fold}} + b_{\mathrm{DM}}a_{\mathrm{fold}},
 \qquad b_{\mathrm{fold}} = 0,\\
&G_\lambda = \frac{\lambda H\tilde I_\lambda}{2f^4v_{\mathrm{t}}},\qquad
 \tilde I_\lambda = \frac{1}{H}\int_{-L}^{0}\big(n^{\mathrm{sc}}_{\mathrm{DF}}\big)^2\,
 \mathrm{e}^{-3Hy}\,\mathrm{d}y,\qquad
 \sigma_\lambda v_{\mathrm{rel}} = \frac{G_\lambda^2M_{\mathrm{DM}}^2}{2\pi}\ \ \text{(per final fold
 species)}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-135}
\]

\textbf{Symbols in (FE-135).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathcal{L}_4^{\lambda}\): The four-dimensional term of the contact
  self-interaction that couples a dark-matter pair to a fold-band pair.
  Unit: \(\mathrm{GeV}^{4}\). Status: ESTIMATE.
\item
  \(\lambda\): The contact self-coupling of dirac16complex (the term
  \(-\frac{\lambda}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) of the record). Unit:
  \(\mathrm{GeV}^{-6}\). Value: not fixed; Kohn-Sham states computed for
  \(\lambda = 0\) and \(\pm7\times10^{-5}H^{-6}\); the fold-band
  radiation requires
  \(\lvert\lambda\rvert \lesssim 1.4\times10^{-10}H^{-6}\) (for
  \(\Delta N_{\mathrm{eff}} \le 0.001\)). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\Psi_{\mathrm{DM}},\ \bar\Psi_{\mathrm{DM}}\): The four-dimensional
